### [1] [A $B_{1g}$ phonon unlocks $d$-wave superconductivity in the repulsive Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37746)
*Wen O. Wang,Tianshu Li,Xiaoyue Ma,Rong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Shruti Agarwal,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Emily Z. Zhang,Brian Moritz,Steven Johnston,Zhi-Xun Shen,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用数值精确的有限温度量子蒙特卡洛方法，研究发现与掺杂的 t-t' 排斥性 Hubbard 模型耦合的 B1g 声子能够产生真正的 d 波超导，且超导转变温度显著超过任一相互作用单独作用时的水平，并随更强 Hubbard 排斥呈现增强趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在掺杂的 t-t' Hubbard 模型（描述强关联的铜氧化物等关联超导体）中，声子（尤其是 B1g 对称性的声子）与电子之间的 Hubbard 排斥如何协同作用，以产生稳健的 d 波超导并提高转变温度，从而为寻找更高 Tc 材料提供微观机制指导。

Method: 采用数值精确的有限温度量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法，研究 B1g 声子耦合到掺杂的 t-t' 排斥性 Hubbard 模型的体系；分析不同声子频率 Ω、Hubbard 排斥强度以及掺杂浓度下的超导转变行为，并以无量纲推迟标度 2πT/Ω 组织数据。

Result: 发现 B1g 声子与 Hubbard 模型耦合能产生真正的 d 波超导，且转变温度显著高于单独由声子或单独由 Hubbard 排斥所达到的水平；在费米子符号问题所限的可达窗口内，增强效应随 Hubbard 排斥增强而持续存在，并沿不同声子频率表现出共同的 2πT/Ω 标度规律；掺杂上呈现明确的超导圆顶结构。

Conclusion: B1g 声子是 Hubbard 模型中实现稳健 d 波超导的一个可信微观要素，并指明了一条具体、物理动机明确的通向更高超导转变温度材料的途径，即协同利用更强的 Hubbard 排斥与声子耦合。

Abstract: Using numerically exact finite-temperature quantum Monte Carlo, we demonstrate that a $B_{1g}$ phonon coupled to the doped $t$-$t'$ repulsive Hubbard model produces genuine $d$-wave superconductivity at a substantial transition temperature, in excess of either interaction alone. The observed trends suggest that stronger Hubbard repulsion may cooperate with the phonon to further raise the pairing scale. The enhancement persists at stronger repulsion within the accessible window, limited by the fermion sign problem, and follows a common trend across phonon frequencies $Ω$ when organized by a retardation scale $2πT/Ω$. We also find a well-defined superconducting dome in doping, echoing a characteristic feature of correlated superconductors. Our results identify the $B_{1g}$ phonon as a plausible microscopic ingredient for robust $d$-wave superconductivity in the Hubbard model and suggest a concrete, physically motivated route toward materials with higher superconducting transition temperatures.

</details>


### [2] [Parity-Resolved Many-Body Kinetics of a Topological Qubit under Nonequilibrium Phonon Pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37793)
*Han Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了电离辐射产生的声子爆发对由两根2 μm近邻化纳米线构成的马约拉纳比特中关联错误的影响。作者结合晶格Lutchyn-Oreg模型、微观电子-声子耦合与宇称分辨多体动力学，追踪激发、弛豫与逻辑相干性。结果表明：无准粒子逃逸时复合在约130 ns内恢复初始马约拉纳占据，但激发与恢复过程中交换的声子会保留逻辑态信息，造成永久相干损失；相干损失分数为激发概率的0.75–1.0倍，镜像对称破缺可抑制退相干；因子化Boltzmann动力学高估了残余宇称翻转；不同声子谱在相同沉积能量密度下产生不同激发概率与相干损失；与Kitaev链对比表明低电子密度抑制高能声子激发，而硬壁边界的高密度对照使1 meV中心脉冲的激发效率提高5×10^4倍；磁场与化学势调谐、杂化态半导体权重均显著影响声子灵敏度。该框架将声子谱与泄漏、毒化及相干损失联系起来，可指导马约拉纳器件设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离辐射引发的声子爆发会在超导量子处理器中产生关联错误，而现有研究缺乏一个能够将声子谱细节与马约拉纳比特的泄漏、准粒子毒化及相干损失定量联系的微观理论框架，阻碍了抗辐射马约拉纳器件设计。

Method: 作者提出一个综合理论框架：采用晶格Lutchyn-Oreg模型描述两根长为2 μm的近邻化纳米线构成的马约拉纳比特；引入微观电子-声子耦合描述声子激发；利用宇称分辨的多体动力学追踪体系的激发、弛豫与逻辑相干演化。同时与因子化Boltzmann动力学以及匹配能隙和马约拉纳衰减长度的Kitaev链进行比较研究，并考察磁场、化学势和半导体杂化权重的影响。

Result: 无准粒子逃逸时，复合约在130 ns内恢复初始马约拉纳占据，但激发与恢复阶段交换的声子可携带逻辑态信息，导致永久相干损失。对镜像对称纳米线，每根线的相干损失分数为两个逻辑态平均激发概率的0.75–1.0倍，即使马约拉纳劈裂为零也如此；打破镜像对称可在小劈裂下抑制退相干。因子化Boltzmann动力学忽略准粒子关联，高估了残余宇称翻转。在相等沉积能量密度下，不同声子谱给出不同的激发概率和相干损失。与Kitaev链对比显示：低电子密度抑制高能声子激发，而硬壁边界的高密度对照在1 meV中心脉冲下激发效率比真实纳米线高5×10^4倍。磁场与化学势调谐通过改变自旋和电子-空穴结构，在相同体隙下产生不同声子灵敏度。当隧穿耦合等于母体超导能隙时，计入杂化态半导体权重使近阈激发率降低四倍。

Conclusion: 该框架首次将声子谱与马约拉纳比特的泄漏、准粒子毒化和相干损失定量关联，揭示了声子携带逻辑态信息是永久退相干的来源，并指出镜像对称性、电子密度、边界条件、磁场/化学势及半导体权重等关键设计参数对声子灵敏度的决定性作用，从而为抗辐射马约拉纳器件设计提供指导。

Abstract: Phonon bursts from ionizing radiation cause correlated errors in superconducting quantum processors. We study prescribed nonequilibrium phonon pulses in a Majorana qubit formed by two $2\,μ\mathrm{m}$ proximitized nanowires. Combining the lattice Lutchyn--Oreg model, microscopic electron--phonon coupling, and parity-resolved many-body dynamics, we track excitation, relaxation, and logical coherence. Without quasiparticle escape, recombination restores the initial Majorana occupations in about 130 ns, while phonons exchanged during excitation and recovery can retain logical-state information, leaving permanent coherence loss. For mirror-symmetric wires, the fractional coherence loss per wire is 0.75--1.0 times the excitation probability averaged over the two logical states, even at zero Majorana splitting; breaking mirror symmetry can suppress dephasing at small splitting. Factorized Boltzmann kinetics neglects quasiparticle correlations and overestimates residual parity flips. At equal deposited energy density, phonon spectra yield different excitation probabilities and coherence losses. Comparisons with Kitaev chains matched to the wire's gap and Majorana decay length reveal that low electron density suppresses excitation by energetic phonons, whereas hard-wall boundaries in a high-density control increase excitation efficiency by a factor of $5\times10^4$ relative to the wire for pulses centered at 1 meV. Magnetic-field and chemical-potential tuning yield different phonon sensitivities at the same bulk gap through changes in spin and electron--hole structure. Including hybrid states' semiconductor weights reduces near-threshold excitation rates fourfold when the tunnel coupling equals the parent superconducting gap. Our framework connects phonon spectra to leakage, poisoning, and coherence loss to guide Majorana-device design.

</details>


### [3] [Influences of $N_2$ flow rate on the superconducting properties and dimensionality of epitaxial TiN thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38250)
*Shilong Chen,Yuchuan Liu,Yihui Sun,Xiaoni Wang,Wei Peng,Yu Wu,Gang Mu,Zhirong Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了N₂流量和背景真空度对DC磁控溅射外延TiN超导薄膜性能的影响，发现1.5 sccm流量下薄膜性能最优，并揭示了样品质量优化会使超导维度偏离二维行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TiN薄膜因稳定的化学/热学性质和优异导电性而具有低损耗特性，在超导量子计算领域备受关注。然而，生长参数（如N₂流量、背景真空）与超导性能之间的关系尚需系统厘清，尤其超导维度的影响因素以往多局限于膜厚，缺乏对样品质量的考察。

Method: 采用DC反应磁控溅射技术在MgO衬底上外延生长35 nm厚的TiN薄膜；表征晶体结构、膜厚与表面形貌；系统研究N₂流量对表面形貌、Tc、RRR、ρn、上临界场及超导维度的影响，并考察生长腔室背景真空度的作用。

Result: 薄膜表面极平坦（RMS粗糙度仅零点几纳米）；N₂流量1.5 sccm时性能最优，Tc最高达4.93 K，RRR最大、ρn最低、面外上临界场斜率最小；背景真空度退化会降低Tc；上临界场呈现显著各向异性，面内上临界场数据显示样品质量优化导致偏离二维超导行为。

Conclusion: 本研究为全面理解生长参数与薄膜超导性能之间的关系提供了有益参考，并首次指出除膜厚外，样品质量同样是影响超导维度的重要因素。

Abstract: TiN thin films have received great attention in the field of superconducting quantum computing due to their low loss characteristics derived from stable chemical and thermal properties, as well as excellent electrical conductivity. In this study, TiN thin films with a thickness of 35 nm were epitaxially grown on MgO substrates using the DC reactive magnetron sputtering technique. The crystal structure, film thickness, and surface morphology of the thin films were characterized. The films exhibit a very flat surface with a root mean square roughness of only a few tenths of a nanometer. The influences of nitrogen ($N_2$) flow rate on the surface morphology, superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$), residual resistivity ratio (RRR), normal-state resistivity ($ρ_n$), upper critical field, and superconducting dimensionality were investigated systematically. The experimental results show that the TiN film deposited with a $N_2$ flow rate of 1.5 sccm exhibits optimal performance, including the highest $T_c$ (4.93 K), the largest RRR, the lowest $ρ_n$, and the lowest slope of the out-of-film upper critical field near Tc. Moreover, it is found that the background vacuum inside the growth chamber has a significant impact on the superconducting properties of TiN films, and the degradation of vacuum degree can lead to the decrease of $T_c$. The upper critical field reveals a significant anisotropic feature. Importantly, the in-film upper critical field data demonstrates that the optimization of sample quality leads to deviation from two-dimensional superconducting behavior, which provides a new perspective on the influencing factors of superconducting dimensionality beyond the thickness of films. The present results provide useful references for a comprehensive understanding of the relationship between growth parameters and the superconducting properties of thin films.

</details>


### [4] [Direct real-space imaging of sign-reversal superconducting state by scanning Josephson tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38421)
*Tianheng Wei,Yanzhao Liu,Wei Ren,Zhen Liang,Ziqiang Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM），研究者在SrTiO₃(001)上外延生长的单层高Tc Fe(Te,Se)薄膜中观测到半晶胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制，并结合全超导能隙的单粒子隧道谱，证实其为符号反转的无节点d波配对对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体由于多带系统中配对对称性的复杂性，传统的基于约瑟夫森隧道的相位敏感实验面临困难，亟需一种能在原子尺度直接获取超导相位信息的方法来判定其配对对称性。

Method: 采用扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM），在s波超导针尖下测量SrTiO₃(001)上外延生长的单层高Tc Fe(Te,Se)薄膜，观测约瑟夫森隧道的空间调制，并结合单粒子隧道谱测量超导能隙。

Result: 观测到半晶胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制；约瑟夫森隧道在连接次近邻Fe原子的连线上几乎消失；单粒子隧道谱显示存在全超导能隙。

Conclusion: 实验结果与符号反转的无节点d波配对对称性一致，为理解高Tc超导机制提供了亚晶胞尺度的超导相位信息，并有望推动SJTM在各种非常规超导体中进行原子分辨约瑟夫森干涉实验。

Abstract: Phase-sensitive experiments based on Josephson tunneling have been considered as a pivotal method to determine the pairing symmetry in high-temperature (high-Tc) cuprate superconductors. However, in iron-based superconductors, traditional phase-sensitive experiments face setbacks because of the complexity of pairing symmetry in multiband systems. Here, using scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy (SJTM), we report the half-unit-cell periodic Josephson tunneling modulations in monolayer high-Tc Fe(Te,Se) film epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001). The Josephson tunneling almost vanishes along the lines connecting next-nearest-neighbor Fe atoms under an s-wave superconducting tip. Combined with single-particle tunneling spectra showing the full superconducting gap, our experimental results are consistent with the sign-reversal nodeless d-wave pairing symmetry. Our findings provide sub-unit-cell scale superconducting phase information for understanding the mechanism of high-Tc superconductivity. We anticipate our work as a starting point for atomically resolved Josephson interference experiments using SJTM in various unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [5] [Layer-Parity Control of the Superconducting Diode Effect in Monolayer NbSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38449)
*Yusuf Wicaksono,Toshikaze Kariyado*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过自洽有限 Q 的 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方法研究单层 NbSe₂ 的超导二极管效应，揭示磁性邻近效应的层宇称与取向如何选择螺旋型与三角型两种不同的整流通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应常与有限动量配对相关联，但模型研究表明整流反映的是自由能关于其极小值的非对称性，而非平衡配对动量本身。因此需要厘清二者之间的关系，并揭示实际材料中决定整流响应的微观因素。

Method: 采用基于 Wannier 表示的自洽有限 Q Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方法，并引入费米面自适应求积以保证临界电流收敛，对单层 NbSe₂ 的磁邻近效应进行系统计算，分析面内与面外交换场、层宇称与取向的影响。

Result: 面内交换下，平衡配对动量 Q_eq 与二极管效率均线性依赖于层奇宇称交换对比：两 Se 亚层平行邻近时互易，反平行邻近时响应加倍；面外分量分离了两个可观测量，Q_eq 几乎不变但效率被抑制一个数量级以上，并在低温反转。纯面外交换因 C₃ 对称禁止螺旋项，保持 Q_eq=0，却产生三角型二极管效应，由层偶宇称交换控制，低温下在 0.65 meV 以上超过面内响应。

Conclusion: 磁性层宇称在螺旋型与三角型二极管通道之间进行选择。三角通道是对 NbSe₂/CrBr₃ 中无场整流的内在对称允许贡献，并预言双侧面外邻近在反平行时抵消、平行时在同层交换强度下增强约四倍。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect is often associated with finite-momentum pairing, although model studies indicate that rectification reflects the asymmetry of the free energy about its minimum rather than the equilibrium pair momentum itself. Using a Wannier-based, self-consistent finite-$\mathbf Q$ Bogoliubov-de Gennes approach with a Fermi-surface-adaptive quadrature that converges the critical currents, we show that the layer parity and orientation of magnetic proximity select the diode response of monolayer NbSe$_2$. For in-plane exchange, the equilibrium pair momentum $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ and the diode efficiency are both linear in the layer-odd exchange contrast: parallel proximity on the two Se sublayers is reciprocal, and antiparallel proximity doubles the response. An out-of-plane component separates the two observables. It leaves $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ nearly unchanged but, through pair breaking, suppresses the efficiency by more than an order of magnitude and, at low temperature, reverses it. Purely out-of-plane exchange, for which $C_3$ symmetry forbids a helical term, keeps $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}=0$ yet produces a trigonal diode effect, controlled by the layer-even exchange and larger than the in-plane one above $0.65$~meV at low temperature. Magnetic layer parity thus selects between a helical and a trigonal diode channel. The trigonal channel is an intrinsic, symmetry-allowed contribution to the field-free rectification reported in NbSe$_2$/CrBr$_3$ and predicts that two-sided out-of-plane proximity cancels it when antiparallel and, at the same exchange per layer, enhances it about fourfold when parallel, in the computed range.

</details>


### [6] [Separating the Density Wave Phase from Zero-Resistance Superconductivity in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38959)
*P. Puphal,A. Suthar,B. Wehinger,V. Sundaramurthy,T. Priessnitz,M. Knauft,P. Sosa-Lizama,Y. -M. Wu,M. Hepting,J. Nuss,M. Isobe,P. A. van Aken,Y. E. Suyolcu,B. Keimer,H. Takagi,P. Reiss*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本工作通过在La₄Ni₃O₁₀三层镍氧化物单晶上施加增强的双轴面内压应力，将零电阻态所需的压力降低了60%，并将低压力下的密度波相与超导性完全分离，排除了密度波量子临界涨落作为配对胶水的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Ruddlesden-Popper镍氧化物在压力下超导性的关键因素十分困难，因为结构变化、氧含量与氧占位、电子有序相以及达到超导所需的高压之间存在复杂耦合。作者旨在通过干净的La₄Ni₃O₁₀三层单晶体系厘清这些相互依赖关系。

Method: 研究一系列干净的三层La₄Ni₃O₁₀单晶，施加增强的双轴面内压应力（biaxial, compressive/in-plane pressures），测量其输运性质（零电阻态）并对比研究低压力密度波相与超导相的关系。

Result: 增强的双轴面内压应力使零电阻态在比以往报道低60%的压力下出现；同时，低压力密度波相与超导性被完全分离，表明密度波序参量的软量子临界涨落并非该体系的配对胶水（pairing glue）。

Conclusion: 双轴面内压应力是促进镍氧化物超导性的有力工具，统一了不同Ruddlesden-Popper结构达到超导所需的压力尺度，并与近期发现的薄膜样品建立了联系。

Abstract: Identifying the key ingredients for superconductivity under pressure in the Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates remains a formidable challenge, due to a complex interplay between structural variations, oxygen content and location, electronically ordered phases, and the need for high pressures to reach the superconducting phase. To disentangle these complex inter-dependencies, here we study a series of clean single crystals of trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. We discover that enhanced biaxial, compressive pressures induce a zero-resistance state at a 60\% lower pressure compared to previous reports. At the same time, we completely separate the low-pressure density wave phase from superconductivity, effectively ruling out that soft, quantum critical fluctuations of the density wave order parameter act as the pairing glue in this system. Our results establish biaxial, in-plane pressures as a powerful tool for promoting superconductivity in nickelates, unifying the required pressure scales for superconductivity in different Ruddlesden-Popper structures, and providing a connection to the recently discovered thin film samples.

</details>


### [7] [Coexistence of superconductivity and magnetic order in BaFe$_2$S$_3$: evidence for orbital-selective delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38966)
*Y. Oubaid,N. S. Dhami,P. Fertey,C. V. Colin,C. Ritter,A. Forget,D. Bounoua,J. -P. Rueff,M. Verseils,P. Foury-Leylekian,V. Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过高压X射线/中子衍射、X射线发射谱和电输运测量，研究准一维自旋梯化合物\bfs\ 在进入超导态过程中结构、磁性与电子态的演化，发现长程条纹反铁磁序在压力下异常稳定，而局域铁磁矩被压制近80%，揭示轨道选择性机制导致局域与巡游电子共存、超导与长程磁序共存。

Abstract: Superconducting diode effects are characterized by direction-dependent critical currents in the superconducting state. Here, we investigate diode-like transport in 128-nm-thick Mo thin film deposited on Si_SiO2 substrate by DC magnetron sputtering. The thin film exhibit a superconducting transition temperature TC of 5.8 K. Pronounced current-direction asymmetry and hysteresis are observed in current-voltage characteristics measured in a closed-cycle refrigerator. In contrast, the same thin film exhibits a substantially more symmetric response in a 14T cryogenic system with improved sample thermalization. We attribute this strong dependence on the measurement environment to electrothermal effects arising from current-induced Joule heating and finite thermal relaxation. As the current approaches the superconducting-to-resistive switching condition, the emergence of a resistive component leads to a rapid increase in Joule heating, raising the sample temperature and modifying the switching condition. Conversely, during the resistive-to-superconducting transition, residual heating and finite thermal relaxation reduce the switching current. Since P is equal to I2R it is independent of current polarity, the observed asymmetry is not associated with polarity-dependent Joule heating but with the interplay of thermal relaxation, sweep history, and thermal boundary conditions. The suppression of the asymmetry under improved thermalization, together with similar behavior observed in NbN reference films, indicates that the apparent diode-like response is strongly influenced by electrothermal effects. These results highlight the need to distinguish thermal switching and hysteresis from intrinsic nonreciprocal superconductivity when identifying superconducting diode effects in transport measurements.

Abstract: We study a spinless $π$-flux lattice superconductor whose normal-state momentum-space fundamental domain is a Klein bottle. Within this model, a glide lift and a hidden antiunitary symmetry independently force the Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) Chern number to vanish. A diagonal pairing breaks the antiunitary obstruction exactly when its real part is nonzero; this is necessary, not sufficient, for a chiral phase. We derive explicit Pfaffians determining Chern parity and identify three analytic families of gap closings in a real-pairing section. For a chiral state on a closed real-space Klein bottle, the prescribed pairing and gluing produce a $ΔC=2$ chirality wall. Their finite-size levels depend on circumference and holonomy; the near-zero pair is lifted at first order by allowed local seam perturbations. Abstract: Altermagnets are collinear magnets with zero net magnetization and spin-split electronic bands. Here, we explore whether altermagnetism can exhibit a discontinuous jump, a phenomenon we refer to as meta-altermagnetism. We show that meta-altermagnetism can emerge in 2D systems when magnetic-order-induced band reconstruction drives spin-split van Hove singularities (vHSs) across the Fermi level. Using mean-field theory for an extended $t$-$J$ model on a two-sublattice square lattice including anisotropic hopping integral, we find that a first-order meta-altermagnetic transition produces an intermediate altermagnetic regime with reduced staggered magnetization but strongly enhanced magnetic octupole and time-reversal-odd spin-splitter conductivity. We further show that the first-order transition line terminates at a quantum critical endpoint, giving rise to a rich phase diagram as the chemical potential and anisotropic hopping strength are varied. Abstract: We study the interacting Kitaev chain with nearest-neighbor interactions and finite chemical potential using a combination of bosonization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations. Focusing away from special exactly solvable lattice limits, we develop a continuum field-theoretic description of the generic finite-density interacting regime, where doping nonperturbatively modifies the competition between superconducting pairing and commensurate charge ordering. We show that the phase diagram consists of an intermediate incommensurate Luttinger liquid between the topological superconducting and commensurate charge-density-wave Mott phases, as well as a trivial band-insulating regime at large chemical potential. The commensurate--incommensurate and incommensurate--topological-superconducting phase boundaries are governed by Pokrovsky--Talapov and Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless criticality, respectively. We analyze the interacting topological superconducting regime and its boundary excitations. At the semiclassical level, and explicitly in the free-fermion boundary theory, the topological phase supports exponentially localized Majorana edge modes at interfaces between topological and trivial regions. We also construct a dual continuum fermionic representation in which this topological bulk sector is described instead by conventional symmetry breaking. This gives a continuum version of the SPT--SSB correspondence between different fermionic representations. Overall, our results provide an analytical description of one-dimensional interacting topological superconductors, from the competition between topology and commensurability and the resulting quantum criticality to the dual symmetry-breaking description of the topological phase.

Abstract: We introduce two-dimensional toric code models with additional long-range loop interactions. For sufficiently long-ranged interactions, we argue that this confinement persists at finite temperatures, strongly constraining anyon mobility and giving rise to finite-temperature topological order. This topological phase acts as a self-correcting quantum memory and exhibits long-range entanglement. For shorter-ranged interactions, signatures of topological order survive only up to an exponentially large length scale at finite temperature, yielding a partially self-correcting quantum memory.

</details>

In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $χ(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $χ(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以自旋空间群的实表示为出发点（该表示由父空间群及自旋空间中的作用所确定），将各类物理响应（磁补偿、压磁、自旋与电荷输运、时间偶/奇 Edelstein 效应、动量空间自旋纹理等）的允许条件化为空间群表示论问题。核心工具是考察定义表示的三个外幂（exterior powers）与点群多极表示之间的重叠；同时利用外幂的波矢内容，即给定幂次下均匀响应需要一、二或三个序波矢求和等于倒格矢，建立分层选择定则，在考虑点群对称性之前即可排除大量响应。

Abstract: Spin space groups provide a complete symmetry classification of magnetic structures in the non-relativistic limit and have become an increasingly useful language for organizing magnetic phases and their physical properties. Each spin space group with a given parent space group is specified, up to spin-only symmetries, by a real representation of that space group acting in spin space. We argue that working directly with this representation not only avoids the construction and identification of the group, but also organizes and simplifies the resulting symmetry constraints on allowed responses. Determining the presence or absence of magnetic compensation, piezomagnetism, spin and charge transport, the Edelstein effect, and momentum-space spin textures becomes an ordinary problem in the representation theory of space groups. The resulting conditions reduce to overlaps between the three exterior powers of the defining representation and multipole representations of the point group. Several physically distinct responses then reduce to the same condition on the defining representation. For example, a $p$-wave spin texture and the time-even Edelstein effect are allowed under the same condition, as are piezomagnetism and a $d$-wave spin texture in compensated order. The wave-vector content of the exterior powers gives a further hierarchy of selection rules: depending on the relevant power, a uniform response requires one, two, or three ordering wave vectors to sum to a reciprocal lattice vector. These rules can exclude many responses before point-group symmetry is considered. We illustrate the approach with examples drawn from a wide variety of materials hosting collinear, coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic order. Finally, we discuss extensions of these results to magnetic space groups, connections to Landau theories of collinear order, and the characterization of odd-parity magnets.

</details>

Abstract: Electronic liquid crystals, such as stripe phases and nematics, are self-organized states of matter with spontaneously broken rotational symmetry. They are expected to emerge in a doped Mott insulator as a compromise between short- and long-range Coulomb interactions, and are believed to be intimately connected to the emergence of high temperature superconductivity either as a competing order or an intertwined order. Moiré semiconductors provide a controllable platform to explore the relationship between stripe phases and superconductivity near a Mott insulator. Here we report the generic appearance of stripe phases in a doped moiré Mott insulator and the observation of a stripe to-superconductor quantum phase transition in twisted bilayer WSe2. The stripe phases were revealed by electrical transport studies as a thermodynamic phase transition from a high temperature isotropic phase to a low-temperature anisotropic phase accompanied by a Fermi surface reconstruction. Near the stripe-to-superconductor transition, we also observed an additional thermodynamic phase transition from metallic stripes to pinned insulating stripes. The stripe-to-superconductor transition further exhibits an intriguing self-similarity that highlights the intimate relationship between the two orders. Our results pave the path for studying intertwined orders in moiré materials.

Abstract: Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>

Abstract: Perovskites exist in varied structures and show structure dependent magnetic properties which is of great interest in the field of multiferroics, magnetocaloric and spintronic devices.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In this article, we address the possibility of topological quantum spin liquids with only short-range (spin) correlations in the whole phase diagram in a ladder system, through an exactly solvable model which represents a generalization of the two-dimensional Kitaev honeycomb spin model to a ribbon geometry. This system gives rise to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge fields emerging in a static way in the ground state through Majorana fermions which then produce either $0$ or $π$ flux. Through a quantum field theory perspective, mathematical and Density Matrix Renormalization Group results, we present our progress on the geometrical, topological and physical classification of this model through observables navigating on the whole phase diagram presenting three spin-liquid phases with a spin gap and a metallic phase in the sense of Majorana fermions. Abstract: Within quantum many-body phase diagrams, continuous transition lines can appear and then terminate. However, it has remained unclear whether the neighboring regimes can be meaningfully described as distinct phases, akin to water and vapour, since any sharp characterization would have to go beyond the usual notion of phases of matter. We answer this in the affirmative by introducing quasi-phases: gapped regimes near a critical point that are connected in the broader phase diagram, but not by any gapped path accessible from small perturbations of the critical theory. The physical consequences include interface modes between regimes in the same phase of matter. More striking still, describing both the extended critical line and the gapped path connecting the regimes requires a bigger multicritical parent theory. In spin-chain examples with continuous terminating lines with central charge $c=1$, we find multicritical $c=3/2$ and $c=9/5$ orbifolds describing so-called 'quotient symmetry protected' and 'representation symmetry protected' topological phenomena. While one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases are labeled by projective representation classes, these quasi-phases can have interface modes carrying higher-dimensional linear representations. We turn this into a constructive principle through a weakening of the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis anomaly. Supporting distinct quasi-phases makes an unnecessary critical point infrared-necessary, even if it is unnecessary at the ultraviolet scale. This condition is more restrictive than simply having a codimension-one critical line, revealing richer structure within phases of matter than their conventional classification suggests.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文建立的编织融合复形/编织融合理论为阿贝尔带隙分形子序与拓扑相提供了统一的激发代数语言，能推广任意子理论并覆盖Pauli码与手性理论；所提取的互统计理论与远程探测原理深化了对分形子序统计结构的理解，并为BFT可实现性与相不变性猜想提供了支持。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic theory of fusion, mobility, and statistics in abelian gapped fracton orders and topological phases. The framework generalizes the algebraic description of abelian anyons to systems with restricted-mobility and spatially extended excitations. We consider translation-invariant bosonic systems whose pointlike and extended excitations have finite fusion order. The basic objects are braided fusion complexes (BFCs): chain complexes encoding fusion and mobility, equipped with a braiding chain map encoding statistics. Their equivalence classes define braided fusion theories (BFTs). Our framework encompasses arbitrary translation-invariant Pauli codes, as well as all abelian anyon theories in two spatial dimensions, including chiral theories. We give evidence for the conjecture that every nondegenerate BFT can be realized as the excitation theory of a gapped phase and that the BFT describing such a phase is a phase invariant. We derive constraints on pointlike fusion and mobility, showing, for example, that neither the Pauli X nor Z sectors of the X-cube and anisotropic models can occur alone in a nondegenerate BFT. For nondegenerate theories with only pointlike excitations, we extract a simpler theory of mutual statistics, consisting of a fusion module and a map to its statistical dual. This theory of mutual statistics is an invariant of the BFT and, at odd fusion order, gives an equivalent description of the full braided fusion theory. We interpret remote detection as the generalization of mutual statistics to fracton orders. In the BFC setting, we give a precise formulation of the excitation-detector principle: every nontrivial excitation can be detected by an operator supported at spatial infinity, and every nontrivial detector detects some excitation. Assuming a unique infinite-space ground state, we prove that this principle is equivalent to nondegeneracy.

</details>

Abstract: The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle $θ\in(0,π)$ is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At $θ=π/3$ liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For $θ=π/3$ anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)$^2$ and Z$_3$ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe$_2$ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an $f+if$ BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

Abstract: Research in the deep sub-mK regime around 0.1 mK may open new opportunities in condensed matter physics in light of recent rapid progress in quantum technologies and quantum materials. Here, we report the design and performance of a nuclear demagnetization system that provides efficient access to the deep sub-mK regime for a wide range of experiments, using a standard wet dilution refrigerator with a medium-sized copper nuclear stage. We show that the temperature measured by a Pt NMR thermometer reaches 50 $μ$K and that the refrigerator can maintain 100 $μ$K for more than ten days. Remarkably, even with only 12 hours of precooling, the refrigerator cools into the deep sub-mK regime and can maintain 100 $μ$K for more than three days, providing sufficient time for sample measurements. Numerical analyses indicate that the copper nuclear-spin system is cooled to about 18 $μ$K, whereas a local heat leak to the thermometer limits the thermometer temperature to about 50 $μ$K. These features establish the system as a practical platform for efficient experiments in the deep sub-mK regime.

Abstract: Classical Hamiltonian time crystals are constrained energy minima that move along symmetry orbits at frequencies set by Lagrange multipliers. Abstract: A topological Anderson insulator is an insulator whose non-trivial topological phase is induced by disorder. We develop a homogenization theory of this phenomenon for constant-coefficient elliptic systems perturbed by fluctuations oscillating at scale $\varepsilon$. Periodic fluctuations of every order below that of the operator, with appropriate amplitudes, do not average out: they produce an order-one correction $M$ given by a second-order (Born) formula, and the operator converges in the norm-resolvent sense to its homogenized limit. In particular, electric potentials and magnetic fields of zero mean, usually regarded as topologically trivial perturbations, generate corrections $M$ that change the topological phase. Fluctuations confined to a half-plane create an interface that carries the bulk-difference invariant of the homogenized operator for all $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small, a property that is stable under local defects. For random fluctuations, rare regions typically fill the homogenized gap, which becomes a pseudo-gap, and the standard interface index need no longer be defined. We define an index along cuts deformed so as to avoid the rare regions, and prove that it equals almost surely the bulk-difference invariant of the operator homogenized in the Born approximation, for first-order alloys with vanishing low moments and for a class of second-order systems with scalar disorder at criticality that includes the BHZ model.

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations. The invariant Gaussian measure of the associated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which need not be time-reversible, serves as the reference measure. We obtain explicit expressions for the entropy production rate and prove that its vanishing in the stationary state is equivalent to time reversibility. Sample-path entropy production is identified with the logarithm of the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the forward path measure with respect to the time-reversed path measure and is shown to satisfy fluctuation theorems. For nonstationary systems, sample-path entropy production decomposes into housekeeping heat and free-energy dissipation, each satisfying an integral fluctuation theorem. Both have nonnegative expectations, and the mean free-energy dissipation equals the decrease in relative entropy with respect to the stationary distribution. The theory is applied to semilinear stochastic heat equations with spatially correlated noise, a random polymer model, a nonlinear model with a divergence-free drift, and two coupled stochastic heat equations in contact with heat baths at different temperatures.

Abstract: We consider bulk-gapped fermion systems whose infinite-volume bulk is obtained by adding sufficiently weak even many-body interactions to a free Hamiltonian with spectral gap $g>0$, allowing polynomial spatial decay of both parts. Abstract: We study a translation-invariant BCS functional of superconductivity for a system of spin-$1/2$ fermions with spin-dependent chemical potentials, $μ_+\neqμ_-$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该文完整建立了 Sturm–Liouville 算子的 L^p 优化逆谱理论：证明等谱流形正则性与解存在性，给出最优势的解析表示，刻画解的唯一性/孤立性与多解情形，并提供迭代与 Newton–KKT 数值方案及其保证边界；该理论可应用于非线性边值问题以及声学/电磁谐振器的诊断与最优谱设计。

Abstract: We study an optimization inverse spectral problem (OISP) for a one-dimensional Sturm--Liouville operator on a finite interval. The problem is to find a potential $V \in L^p(0,1)$ that realizes a prescribed finite set $λ_1^* < λ_2^* < \dots < λ_m^*$ and has the smallest $L^p(0,1)$-distance from an a priori given potential $V_0 \in L^p(0,1)$.
  We formulate and prove results on the regularity of the isospectral manifold, the existence of an OISP solution, and an explicit analytic representation of the optimal potential by means of a power map applied to a linear combination of squared eigenfunctions.
  Using a unified differential-algebraic approach to squared eigenfunctions, we show that whenever these squares are linearly dependent, the corresponding potential can be expressed in terms of them and is an analytic function.

For every $1<p<\infty$, we establish uniqueness for the first eigenvalue when $λ_1^*\geλ_1(V_0)$ and generic global uniqueness in the sense of Baire category in $L^p(0,1)$.

We describe an iteration with frozen eigenfunctions and a local Newton--KKT method with continuation in the spectral data, and state explicitly the limits of their guarantees.
  The final part discusses applications of OISP theory to nonlinear boundary value problems and to applied problems of diagnostics and optimal spectral design of acoustic and electromagnetic resonators.

</details>

Abstract: The singularity theorems and the past incompleteness of inflationary spacetimes suggest that the beginning of the Universe lies beyond the domain of semiclassical gravity. We construct rotationally invariant cosmic creation gates joining any finite number $n\geq2$ of locally de Sitter regions in $D\geq3$ spacetime dimensions at a timelike perfect-fluid junction with $P=wρ$. The total spatial volume approaches finite, positive limits in the infinite past and future on the global-time foliation adapted to the junction fluid. We also construct exact deformed cosmic creation gates through nonlinear homogeneous backreaction that retain the initial volume while the future volume grows without bound. In these solutions, the junction fluid always expands, cooling for $w>0$ and heating for allowed $w<0$. Keeping the bulk regions locally de Sitter, we determine the linearized perturbations: in three dimensions, the generic inhomogeneous sector contains one hybrid sound mode and $n-1$ relative transverse modes; in four and higher dimensions, only the homogeneous hybrid sound mode survives. A finite-volume beginning requires fine-tuned homogeneous and inhomogeneous initial data, while causal response generically leads to unbounded future spatial volume. We also discuss a broader creation criterion allowing bounded past-volume oscillations. The gravitational equations determine conditioned admissible histories. We discuss implications for cosmology.

Abstract: On two-dimensional Anti-de Sitter space, Majorana fermions with a discrete set of finely tuned, non-zero mass parameters possess conserved spinor--tensors that are symmetric and traceless. Abstract: What can we learn about a putative dual to de Sitter spacetime from the bulk? Conversely, what can a candidate dual theory teach us about the bulk? In doing so, we provide a bulk realisation of $T^2+ Λ_2$ for dS$_4$ black holes. We identify the average Hamiltonian of our worldtube proposal as the seed energy of the $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$ theories. Abstract: We study the charged Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound for all horizon harmonics of the type IIB $t^+$ Kaluza--Klein tower on extremal charged AdS$_5$ black holes with equal rotations. The scalar equation follows from maximal five-dimensional gauged supergravity at level zero and the linearized exceptional-field-theory equations at higher levels. For aligned charge and rotation, a quadratic form in charge and spin changes signature on the curve of supersymmetric (BPS) black holes, where every mode satisfies the bound. Above the curve, where $Ω_H>1$, every black hole has a violating mode, although no finite truncation in level or spin detects violation at every such black hole. Below the curve, where $Φ_H>1$, the BF-passing set is confined to a thin strip and controlled by charge and spin discreteness. At large radius and fixed rotation, a level-four mode with spin proportional to the radius violates the bound even where all U(2) singlets satisfy it. These results concern the near-horizon BF bound of one tower in two-derivative supergravity; full radial stability remains open.

</details>

Abstract: Quotient quiver subtraction translates the gauging of Coulomb branch global symmetries into a combinatoric operation on 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ gauge theories. Similar to other recently-introduced techniques, the gauging operation is a two-step process; in this case, it involves the symplectisation of unitary gauge factors into symplectic groups. A host of explicit examples are used to verify the procedure, and connections are made to Class $\mathcal{S}$ theories and three-dimensional theories realising maximal implosion spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We study the circuit complexity of coupled curvature and entropy perturbations in two-field inflation. We start from the quadratic action and derive the full Hamiltonian. The Hamiltonian includes all diagonal and off-diagonal quadratic terms. We then obtain three coupled equations for the pair amplitudes of the four-mode squeezed state. From these amplitudes, we calculate the two principal squeezing strengths. We use them to determine the Nielsen circuit complexity. For an equal-weight Gaussian circuit, the complexity takes the form $\mathcal{C}_{2,k}=\sqrt{2(r_{1k}^{2}+r_{2k}^{2})}$. It depends on the squeezing strengths but not explicitly on the squeezing angles. Our numerical results show that the complexity grows after horizon crossing in both coupled and uncoupled models. Interfield coupling changes the pair amplitudes and the four-mode squeezed state. However, its effect on the circuit complexity is small for the parameters considered. This result shows that different four-mode pair-amplitude matrices can yield similar values of the chosen equal-weight circuit complexity. The present calculation provides a reference for studying Gaussian circuit complexity in more general two-field inflationary models.

Moreover, combining the diagrammatic correlator with endpoint crossing, somewhat surprisingly, we find agreement between the bulk and Wilson-line spectra in every non-singlet flavor channel through order $1/N$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有拓扑点算符的二维量子场论会分解为若干“宇宙”（universes），此前融合范畴在理解不可逆对称性中作用重大；本文旨在为这类可分解理论建立相应的多重融合范畴（multi-fusion category）框架，以系统处理平凡作用对称性、宇宙间拓扑界面，并统一理解各类宇宙对的结构与来源。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum field theories with topological point operators decompose into universes. Just as fusion categories have played an important role in understanding non-invertible symmetries, multi-fusion categories play an analogous role for the symmetries of such decomposing theories. We describe a finite symmetry as a multi-fusion category together with its action functor, which keeps track of trivially acting symmetries and of the topological interfaces between universes. Relative to a chosen symmetry, we classify pairs of universes by the topological interfaces it contains, which reflect the structure of the multi-fusion category: universes in different indecomposable summands are joined by no interface (Type~III), while within a summand they are related by invertible (Type~I) or only non-invertible (Type~II) interfaces. For orbifolds with a trivially acting abelian normal subgroup, we derive the full inherited symmetry, which characterizes the operators between universes found case by case by Robbins, Sharpe and Vandermeulen and yields pairs of Types~I and~II but never of Type~III. We construct Type~III pairs instead from extensions of $2$-group type and from renormalization group flows out of a common ultraviolet theory. Finally, we propose a correspondence with the generalized families of quantum field theories of Brennan and Intriligator, in which equivariant families correspond to Type~I pairs and categorical ones to Type~II pairs whose gauging preserves the symmetry, family anomalies appear as universes carrying different symmetry-protected topological phases, and Type~III pairs can realize families over a discrete parameter. Abstract: We revisit bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity, aiming to strengthen and unify established bounds, in particular the quantum focussing conjecture (QFC) and the smeared null energy condition (SNEC). Both the QFC and SNEC have important applications and are the strongest in their respective hierarchies; however, neither implies the other, nor reduces to the null energy condition (NEC) in the classical limit. To that end, we define a new class of bounds dubbed gravitational energy-entropy conditions (GEECs) that imply both the QFC and SNEC, and reduce to the NEC classically. We construct an explicit family of GEECs by imposing the defining criteria on a broad set of stress tensor bounds, and constrain its remaining parameter space through test cases, in an effective field theory-inspired approach. Our GEECs are powerful candidate bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity.

A sequential semidefinite optimization heuristic then searches for configurations satisfying planar factorization and estimates the ground state energy and loop expectation values. Applications to one-, two-, and three-matrix models exhibit close agreement with analytic solutions and master field optimization. By working exclusively with coordinate moments, the framework permits truncations at higher word length and offers a promising approach to models with more matrices, including the bosonic sectors of the BFSS and BMN models.

Abstract: We argue that the scattering amplitude of a single minus helicity gluon with arbitrarily many positive helicity gluons is topological. The amplitude is constant in chambers of kinematic space, its resonances are located at walls and its factorization is determined by wall-crossing. Yangian invariance dictates the amplitude to be determined by a 2d null Wilson loop that can be lifted into a positive and transverse 3d link. By reinterpreting wall-crossing and the Weinberg soft theorem as skein relations and Reidemeister moves of the subknots, we construct a state-counting description consisting of adding the `corners' of their HOMFLY-PT polynomials. Beyond the classical limit, we argue that this provides a conjectured but precise connection to topological field and string theory.
  The new formula is rigorously derived via a Lean-formalized dependency graph (DAG) as detailed in a companion paper, laying out the precise assumptions including Reidemeister III invariance. It is further tested exhaustively, including novel `star' kinematics where the amplitude and state count are shown to become Catalan numbers, in agreement with knot theory literature.

Method: 采用 bootstrap（自举）方法，即不直接积分，而是利用波函数系数应满足的解析性、对称性与运动学约束等物理条件来约束并唯一确定结果；同时对被积函数在椭圆扇区的留数、阈值不连续性及特殊动量构型（正四面体）进行分析。

Result: 三角波函数系数并非椭圆：椭圆周期相消，平空间波函数系数是膨胀对数的组合。相消的原因在于圈被积函数在椭圆扇区的留数是一个平空间振幅，对圈动量是有理的。盒图则即使在每格点仅一个外腿时仍非多重对数，其阈值不连续性由椭圆积分给出（文中给出闭式表达式）；当动量取正四面体构型时，K3 曲面的周期进入其被积函数。

Abstract: Loop corrections to the cosmological wavefunction involve similar integrals to those required for scattering amplitudes. For the one-loop triangle of a conformally coupled scalar, the master integrals are elliptic, and so the triangle wavefunction coefficient was expected to be elliptic as well. Using a bootstrap approach, we compute this triangle coefficient exactly and show that it is not: the elliptic periods cancel, and the flat-space wavefunction coefficient is a combination of dilogarithms. The cancellation occurs because the residue of the loop integrand on the elliptic sector is a flat-space amplitude, rational in the loop momentum. The one-loop box, by contrast, is not polylogarithmic, even with a single external leg at each site. Its threshold discontinuity involves elliptic integrals, which we also give in closed form. For regular-tetrahedron momenta the periods of a K3 surface enter its loop integrand.

</details>

Abstract: This is the first of two companion papers in which we study five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories engineered by $(p,q)$ five-brane webs and their associated three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=4$ magnetic quivers. We focus on the $N+\mathrm{SU}(N)_0^{k-1}+N$ box quiver theories. We propose an exact parameter dictionary relating the five-dimensional flavour masses and instanton couplings to the Fayet-Iliopoulos parameters of the corresponding strong-coupling magnetic quiver.

On the five-dimensional side, we implement these flows directly at the level of the superconformal index. The dictionary fixes the corresponding FI deformations and monopole VEVs. We analyse them in the magnetic-quiver squashed-sphere partition function using a new method that tracks Higgsing, decoupled sectors, and infrared theories. Our results promote magnetic quivers to tools for the quantitative study of how the five-dimensional superconformal index responds to deformations. Once the parameter dictionary is known, exact three-dimensional observables can be used to reconstruct the corresponding five-dimensional physics and to navigate the full deformation network in both directions.

We show that, when the Higgsing is excessive, the five-dimensional superconformal index becomes distributional, mirroring a characteristic feature of bad and broken theories in three dimensions. We also develop a five-brane web interpretation of these results, identifying the Hanany-Witten moves associated with the Higgsing cascade and relating inequivalent frames to distinct tessellations of the generalised toric diagram.

### [93] [Generalized Symmetries of QED$_4$ on Non-Spin Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38907)
*Michele Del Zotto,Vivek Saxena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究无质量QED在非自旋（仅定向）四维流形上的广义对称性，发现光子成为spin^c联络、路径积分对spin^c结构求和，导致磁1形式对称性被w2扭转，并伴随混合磁-引力反常与框架反常，需通过堆叠非可逆WRT层来抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义对称性对时空的切向结构（自旋/定向结构）敏感。此前对QED广义对称性的讨论多默认自旋流形，而物理上定向非自旋四维流形（如复射影平面CP²）上费米子需spin^c结构，光子则需量子化通量的分数移位。作者希望系统厘清这一非自旋背景下QED对称性的扭转、反常及其对相的影响。

Method: 将无质量QED置于定向非自旋四维流形上，把光子视为spin^c联络并对所有spin^c结构求和。通过约化定向配边（reduced oriented bordism）分类反常，利用Wu流形检测混合磁-引力反常；对固定spin^c扇区用counterterm消去反常；构造spin^c Hall拓扑场论处理手征缺陷，并用堆叠中性的、一般非可逆的Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev（WRT）层进行反常流入（anomaly inflow）抵消；以CP²作为显式算例验证。

Result: (1) 移位通量量子化将磁1形式对称性扩展为w2扭转的1形式对称性，其2π旋转由算子所包裹曲面的法丛决定，相当于引力符号。(2) 将其视为普通磁1形式对称性的阻碍是一个二阶混合磁-引力反常，由约化定向配边分类，并在Wu流形上被检测；在固定spin^c扇区内该反常可被counterterm消去，故属于对spin^c结构求和的性质。(3) 手征反常由spin^c Dirac算子指标支配，有理手征缺陷同时携带引力与电磁响应。spin^c Hall TFT抵消电磁部分但遗留框架反常；对每个有理角，通过堆叠中性、一般非可逆的WRT层可在反常流入层面完全抵消。同样的框架微妙性也影响弯曲自旋流形上QED的手征缺陷。CP²显式展示了上述所有特征。

Conclusion: 非自旋流形上手征费米子与QED的广义对称性被切向结构的扭结（w2）深刻修正：磁1形式对称性变为w2扭转，其普通化受阻于混合磁-引力反常；手征流需spin^c Hall项与WRT层配合以全过程抵消框架反常。这表明自旋/非自旋流形上的对称性与反常理论存在实质性差异，并为有隙、库仑与希格斯相的分类提供了新约束。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries are sensitive to the tangential structure of spacetime. We study this interplay for massless QED on oriented non-spin four-manifolds, where the photon becomes a spin$^c$ connection and the path integral sums over spin$^c$ structures. The shifted flux quantization extends the magnetic 1-form symmetry into a $w_2$-twisted 1-form symmetry: its $2π$ rotation acts as a gravitational sign, fixed by the normal bundle of the surface the operator wraps. The obstruction to treating it as an ordinary magnetic 1-form symmetry is an order-two mixed magnetic-gravitational anomaly, which is classified by reduced oriented bordism and detected on the Wu manifold. In a fixed spin$^c$ sector, this anomaly is removable by a counterterm, so it is a property of the sum over spin$^c$ structures. We discuss its consequences for gapped, Coulomb, and Higgs phases. The chiral anomaly is governed by the index of the spin$^c$ Dirac operator, so rational chiral defects carry gravitational as well as electromagnetic responses. A spin$^c$ Hall topological field theory cancels the electromagnetic part but leaves a framing anomaly. At the level of anomaly inflow, this is canceled for every rational angle by stacking a neutral, generally noninvertible Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev layer. The same framing subtlety affects the chiral defects of QED on curved spin manifolds. The complex projective plane illustrates each of these features explicitly.

</details>

Abstract: Fractional field theory is a promising framework wherein to quantize the gravitational interaction but the presence of nonlocal operators can make its handling difficult. First, we generalize the Caffarelli--Silvestre extension theorem to self-adjoint fractional field theories with multi-scale kinetic terms and nonlocal interactions, taking the example of a scalar field. However, inclusion of a local non-linear potential is not straightforward. We therefore propose an alternative picture where the nonlocal theory is not extended to a higher-dimensional space but is localized at the price of introducing a continuum of masses. This \emph{spectral localization} allows for a natural implementation of the fakeon prescription and the related classicization procedure, already employed in local theories with higher-derivative and complex ghosts. Applications to fractional quantum gravity and its comparison with quadratic quantum gravity are discussed.

Abstract: We reconstruct linearized gravitational perturbations in $AdS_{4}$ from a conserved, traceless stress tensor on a fixed conformally flat boundary metric, extending our earlier real Lorentzian construction. For admissible data with no spacelike Fourier support in a flat conformal frame, the physical response reduces to an integral over boundary light-cone cuts. Boundary improvements restore Fefferman--Graham (FG) gauge while preserving normalizability and the linearized Weyl tensor. We then reconstruct perturbations with general spacelike boundary momentum, including nonzero frequency and its zero-frequency limit, using explicit position-space generalized functions on real stress data. Boundary kernels are constructed for normalizable perturbations and sufficient conditions are derived for the reconstruction to converge. The results motivate controlled flat-space limits relevant to Bondi--Metzner--Sachs symmetries and celestial holography.

Abstract: These notes provide an introduction to Haagerup noncommutative $L^p$ spaces for quantum field theorists. The aim is to explain how the familiar calculus of density matrices, fractional powers, and Schatten norms extends to local observable algebras of type~III, where an intrinsic trace is unavailable. We develop the construction first for a matrix algebra, making the crossed product, its trace, and the dual action explicit, before passing to general von Neumann algebras. Particular attention is paid to the representation of normal states by Haagerup densities and to the distinction between the crossed-product trace and the canonical Haagerup functional used to compute expectation values. As a concrete application, we calculate the sandwiched Rényi divergence for a coherent excitation of a chiral current, verifying monotonicity under restriction and the Rényi quantum null energy condition at every real order $α>1$. The presentation assumes familiarity with quantum mechanics and elementary quantum field theory, while introducing the operator-algebraic concepts needed for the construction and calculation.

Result: 在该半经典区域中，观测者无法轻易区分黑洞视界邻域与宇宙学视界邻域的物理，这表明两类视界热力学在此极限下不可区分，从而说明二者在一般情况下不可能完全无关。

Conclusion: 这并未证明两类熵在任意宇宙中普遍等价，但表明它们不能完全无关。作者提议，任何旨在描述正宇宙学常数宇宙中黑洞的一致量子引力理论，都应在本文所考虑的极限邻域内，为领先阶半经典宇宙学熵与黑洞熵提供相互关联的微观起源。

Abstract: It has recently been argued that cosmological horizon thermodynamics and black hole thermodynamics are generically inequivalent in any consistent, UV complete theory of quantum gravity, where it was suggested that this could be falsified by the identification of a counterexample. This conjecture was motivated in part by noting that cosmological horizons do not incorporate many of the convenient features found in the black holes described by string theory. In this work, we consider as a toy model a universe with a single non-rotating black hole and a positive cosmological constant. We work in the Nariai limit where the mass of the black hole is taken to its maximum possible value, and where we also assert that the quantum field theory living on this background is strictly parity invariant. In the semi-classical regime where we neglect instability of the exact Nariai equilibrium under quantum fluctuations, we find that the observer cannot readily distinguish the physics in the neighborhood of the black hole horizon from the physics in the neighborhood of the cosmological horizon. This does not prove that the two are generically equivalent in any universe, though it does suggest that they cannot be entirely unrelated in general. We propose that any consistent theory of quantum gravity that aims to describe black holes in a universe with a positive cosmological constant should provide a related microscopic origin for the leading semiclassical cosmological entropy and black hole entropy in the neighborhood of the particular limit considered here.

</details>

Abstract: We study planar four-point scattering amplitudes on the Coulomb branch of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory, focusing on multi-parameter families of massive kinematics. We extend the deformed Amplituhedron construction of \cite{Arkani-Hamed:2023epq,Flieger:2025ekn} to general external kinematics characterized by a six-parameter embedding-space Gram matrix. We show that copositivity constraints on the inverse Gram matrix control the real geometry and restrict the numerator of the two-loop canonical form to the span of the two planar double-box structures expected for the scattering amplitude. We then specialize to a three-parameter massive configuration and evaluate the relevant one- and two-loop integrals using differential equations directly in four dimensions, obtaining analytic expressions in terms of multiple polylogarithms. We investigate multiscale infrared limits of the proposed two-loop amplitude and find exponentiation through two loops. The kinematic family contains the limits governed by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions and interpolates between them through the walking anomalous dimension introduced in~\cite{Alday:2026cpk}. Our result reproduces the one-loop interpolation and fixes the two-loop coefficients in their proposed all-loop ansatz. Interestingly, our results suggest that the walking anomalous dimension may be entirely determined through all loop orders by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions.

Abstract: The regularized master-field approximation (RMA) is an efficient computational method for large-$N$ reduced matrix models, including those with multiple matrices or complex integration weights. In this work, we apply the RMA to mass-deformed supersymmetric Yang--Mills-type matrix models with three, four, and ten matrices. All three models admit fuzzy-sphere configurations as classical solutions.
