<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿万有引力通过反距离势描述两质量间的吸引，而爱因斯坦理论中引力本身会引力化——每个质量重塑对方作用的时空几何。一个多世纪以来，从这一耦合几何中提取双体相互作用仍是根本性的解析难题。即使在无运动、无辐射的静态情形下，该相互作用仍蕴含无限层级的场耦合，是研究引力非线性的最纯粹实验室。

Method: 围绕稳态相互作用能重新表述问题：采用精确单体背景重求和其中一个源的自相互作用；利用稳态性减少另一个源所需考虑的场；运用积分响应恒等式揭示耦合模式间的相互抵消；再辅以互补展开，在有限阶上完整解析质量依赖关系。

Result: 确定了任意质量比下从九阶到十二阶（牛顿常数阶数）的静态相互作用，并推导出超越史瓦西测试体极限的一阶反作用力的、对引力耦合全阶成立的闭合表达式。

Conclusion: 两项成果共同弥合了无穷小探针与可比拟质量之间的鸿沟，揭示了爱因斯坦理论中隐藏的解析简洁性，并为轨道动力学建模与引力波产生提供了关键构件。

Abstract: Newton's law of universal gravitation describes the attraction between two masses through an inverse-distance potential. In Einstein's theory gravity gravitates: each mass reshapes the spacetime geometry through which the other acts. More than a century later, extracting the two-body interaction from this coupled geometry remains a fundamental analytical challenge. Even without motion or radiation, the static sector of the interaction harbours an infinite hierarchy of field couplings: the purest laboratory for gravitational non-linearity. Here we determine the static interaction from ninth to twelfth order in Newton's constant for arbitrary mass ratios, and derive a closed expression for the first backreaction beyond the Schwarzschild test-body limit to all orders in the gravitational coupling. We achieve both advances by formulating the problem around the stationary interaction energy. An exact one-body background resums one source's self-interaction, stationarity reduces the fields required from the other, and integrated response identities reveal cancellations across the coupled modes. Complementary expansions then resolve the complete mass dependence at finite order. Together, these results bridge the gap between an infinitesimal probe and comparable masses, exposing hidden analytical simplicity within Einstein's theory and providing essential building blocks for modelling orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave generation.

</details>


### [101] [virasoro_blocks: Software for the 2d bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39106)
*David Poland*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍 virasoro_blocks 软件，可计算任意中心荷的 Virasoro 共形块，适用于数值 bootstrap。它利用最优插值减少 Kac 极点数量以适配半定规划，支持任意精度与奇异中心荷（如最小模型和 c=1），并能计算全局块、N=1 超对称块、环面特征标与环面单点块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virasoro 共形块在数值 bootstrap 中的使用受制于 Zamolodchikov 递推给出的块在每个有限阶含有大量 Kac 极点，这些极点使半定规划（SDP）方法难以直接应用。因此需要一种方法在保持高精度的同时减少极点数量，使任意中心荷下的 Virasoro 块能够真正用于数值 bootstrap。

Method: 开发 virasoro_blocks 软件，基于 Zamolodchikov 递推在椭圆 nome 的有限阶展开得到共形块（正预因子乘以交换权重的有理函数）。通过最优插值（optimal interpolation）减少 Kac 极点数目，同时保持高精度。软件支持任意精度算术，并处理奇异中心荷情形（最小模型与 c=1），扩展支持全局块、N=1 超共形块、环面特征标和环面单点块。

Result: 通过两个应用验证软件：其一，仅假设标量 Virasoro 初级场，计算标量隙的界限，这些界限趋近于 (c-1)/16 与 (c-1)/12 之间的 Liouville 解窗口；其二，在多个中心荷下对全同标量四点函数中的平均交换维数给出约束，这些约束具有丰富的结构，许多已知二维 CFT 位于或接近其边界。

Conclusion: virasoro_blocks 软件通过最优插值解决了 Virasoro 块中 Kac 极点过多的问题，使得任意中心荷（含奇异中心荷）下的数值 bootstrap 成为可能，并在标量隙与平均交换维数的应用中表现出接近已知 CFT 边界的丰富结构，展示了该工具的实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. At each finite order in the elliptic nome, Zamolodchikov's recursion gives the block as a positive prefactor times a rational function of the exchanged weight. However, its many Kac poles make it difficult to use in semidefinite programming approaches to the bootstrap. In this software we use optimal interpolation to reduce the number of poles while keeping high accuracy. The software works in arbitrary precision and handles singular central charges, including the minimal models and $c=1$. It also computes global blocks, $N=1$ superconformal blocks, torus characters, and torus one-point blocks. We illustrate it with two applications. Assuming only scalar Virasoro primaries, we compute bounds on the scalar gap that approach the window of Liouville solutions between $(c-1)/16$ and $(c-1)/12$. We also bound the average exchanged dimension in four-point functions of identical scalars at several central charges. These bounds have a rich structure, and many known 2d CFTs lie on or near their boundary.

</details>


### [102] [Localization in Seven-Dimensional Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40514)
*Florian Gaar,Michèle Levi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在七维最大 Cartan 规范超引力中，将相对等变局域化方法推广到所有规范场均活跃的情形，无需显式求解即可从填充流形的固定点数据中重构一般超对称 AdS₇ 黑洞的作用量，并推导出线性化学势约束与非线性电荷关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 七维超引力通过其最高维超对称 AdS 真空，为强耦合六维超共形理论提供全息途径；但其独特的旋量双线性几何与高阶规范场动力学使系统分析和求解异常困难。传统相对等变局域化已能在奇数维从辅助偶数维填充的固定点数据出发获得超对称可观测，但尚未推广到七维且所有规范场活跃、具有特定旋量实性条件变化的情形。

Method: 在最大 Cartan 规范超引力中，以场强形式表述欧氏作用量与 Killing 旋量方程；构造适应于旋量实性条件从辛-Majorana 变为 Majorana 的双线性系统。在非零规范活跃旋量分支上，利用同一复背景下的 Majorana 共轭配对具有相反 R 电荷的独立旋量；只需一个代数规范修正和两个代数双线性投影，即可将等电荷扇区推广到独立 Cartan 电荷。有质量 3-形式自对偶性确定四次反常类，并由交换矩映射确定高阶形式固定点数据。在填充流形的单一固定点进行局域化，恢复一般超对称 AdS₇ 黑洞作用量。

Result: 证明了在超对称 Killing 流与 R 对称性联合作用下，规范协变 Killing 旋量的不变性，并推导出线性化学势约束。对受约束熵泛函求极值并要求实正平稳熵，在欧氏分析内得到非线性电荷关系，等价于已知球对称黑洞族的洛伦兹视界极端性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将相对等变局域化推广至七维最大规范超引力的完全活跃规范场情形，无需显式延拓解即可从单一固定点恢复一般超对称 AdS₇ 黑洞作用量，并在欧氏框架下自洽导出与洛伦兹极端性等价的电荷约束，揭示了该方法对高维高阶规范场理论的适用性。

Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity provides holographic access to strongly coupled six-dimensional superconformal theories through its highest-dimensional supersymmetric AdS vacua. Its unique spinor-bilinear geometry and massive higher-form dynamics make systematic analysis and solution-finding distinctively challenging. Relative equivariant localization can obtain supersymmetric observables in odd dimensions from fixed-point data on an auxiliary even-dimensional filling, without an explicit solution. We extend this approach to seven dimensions in maximal Cartan-gauged supergravity with all gauge fields active. We formulate the Euclidean action and Killing-spinor equations in terms of field strengths, and construct a bilinear system adapted to the change in spinor reality conditions from symplectic-Majorana to Majorana. On the non-null gauged active spinor branch, independent, oppositely R-charged spinors are paired by Majorana conjugation on the same complex background. One algebraic gauging correction and two algebraic bilinear projections suffice to extend the equal-charge sector to independent Cartan charges, and the underlying efficient reorganization also applies in the Lorentzian theory. Massive 3-form self-duality fixes the quartic anomaly class and determines the higher-form fixed-point datum from Abelian moment maps. Localization at the single fixed point of the filling recovers the general supersymmetric AdS$_7$ black-hole action, without extending the solution. We prove gauge-covariant Killing-spinor invariance under the combined supersymmetric Killing flow and R-symmetry action, deriving the linear chemical-potential constraint. Extremizing the constrained entropy functional and requiring real positive stationary entropy yields the nonlinear charge relation within the Euclidean analysis, equivalent to Lorentzian horizon extremality for the known spherical black-hole family.

</details>


### [103] [Ortho-symplectic interface line operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39207)
*Yasuyuki Hatsuda,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究4维 N=4 超对称杨-米尔斯理论（正交与辛规范群）中半BPS界面上的线算符，提出S对偶下界面处Wilson线与涡旋线的对偶关系，并通过线缺陷半指标（half-index）的匹配作为证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线算符在超对称规范理论的对偶研究中扮演关键角色，而半BPS界面上线算符的分类及其在S对偶下的行为尚未被系统刻画。作者旨在填补正交与辛规范群情形下这一空白，并给出非平凡的量子证据。

Method: 作者对NS5型和D5型两类半BPS界面分别建立线算符的标记方案：NS5型界面按界面两侧规范群的电荷对（电或磁）分类，D5型界面则按界面保留下来的单一规范群的电或磁荷分类。随后提出S对偶作用下界面处Wilson线与涡旋线的对偶猜想，并通过计算线缺陷半指标（line defect half-index）在结点（junctions）上的匹配来验证。

Result: 作者给出了两类界面上线算符的完整分类，提出了NS5/D5界面框架下S对偶作用于Wilson线与涡旋线的具体对偶映射，并发现对偶对在线算符与界面构成的结点上半指标精确匹配。

Conclusion: 正交与辛规范群的N=4 SYM中，半BPS界面的线算符分类清晰，且S对偶将界面处的Wilson线与涡旋线互换。半指标的匹配为这些对偶关系提供了高度非平凡的证据，深化了对S对偶在含边界/界面缺陷体系中的作用的理解。

Abstract: We study the line operators at the half-BPS interfaces in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theories of orthogonal and symplectic gauge groups. For the NS5-type interfaces, line operators are labeled by pairs of electric or magnetic charges under the gauge groups on either side of the interface, whereas for the D5-type interfaces they are labeled by electric or magnetic charges under the single gauge group preserved by the interface. We propose dualities between the Wilson lines and vortex lines at the interfaces under the action of S-duality. As highly non-trivial evidence of the dualities on the junctions of the line operators and the interfaces, we find matching of the line defect half-indices for the dual pairs.

</details>


### [104] [Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes: dissipation, noise and decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39243)
*Hajime Kobayashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了量子黑洞的Schwinger-Keldysh世界线有效场论，在单一实时影响泛函框架内统一处理耗散、涨落与退相干，并通过因果响应核与对称噪声核刻画约化动力学，其谱满足正性约束，热力学下由Kubo-Martin-Schwinger对称性经涨落-耗散关系关联。文中给出后牛顿Schwarzschild二体的噪声、四极退相干泛函及与DSW视界诱导标度的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子黑洞的耗散、涨落与退相干通常被分别处理，缺乏一个统一、实时的有效场论框架来同时描述这些非幺正效应，并以自洽方式关联响应与噪声（涨落-耗散关系）。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh世界线有效场论，将约化动力学编码为因果响应核与对称噪声核；噪声核的谱满足正性约束，热平衡时由Kubo-Martin-Schwinger对称性给出涨落-耗散关系。通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换将噪声核转化为驱动已解算度规和世界线的高斯源；对领头阶后牛顿Schwarzschild二体，将直接随机力与度规介导随机力合成为该阶总相对加速度噪声，并检验质心动量守恒及普朗克压低；同一噪声核还给出空间叠加态的四极退相干泛函，经吸收匹配复现Danielson-Satishchandran-Wald视界诱导标度。

Result: 得到后牛顿阶相对加速度噪声（普朗克压低且保持质心动量平衡）、四极退相干泛函，并通过吸收匹配复现DSW视界诱导标度；非平衡情形下响应与噪声保持相互独立的匹配数据。

Conclusion: 该世界线有效场论为量子黑洞的非幺正动力学提供统一框架；未来可扩展到世界线核中的微观量子结构、与空间延展粗粒化描述的匹配，以及引力分裂等问题。

Abstract: We formulate a Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes that treats dissipation, fluctuations, and decoherence within one real-time influence functional. The reduced dynamics is encoded in causal response and symmetric noise kernels. Their spectra obey positivity constraints, while thermal Kubo--Martin--Schwinger symmetry relates them through the fluctuation--dissipation relation. A Hubbard--Stratonovich representation turns the noise kernel into a Gaussian source driving the resolved metric and worldline. For a leading post-Newtonian Schwarzschild binary, direct and metric-mediated stochastic forces combine into the complete relative-acceleration noise at this order, preserve center-of-mass momentum balance, and remain Planck suppressed. The same noise kernel gives the quadrupolar decoherence functional for a supported spatial superposition and reproduces the Danielson--Satishchandran--Wald horizon-induced scaling after absorptive matching. Away from thermal equilibrium, response and noise remain independent matching data. We discuss extensions to microscopic quantum structure in the worldline kernels, matching to spatially extended coarse-grained descriptions, and gravitational splitting.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum field theory with scale-dependent weighted measures: nonlinear momentum representations, vacuum fluctuations, and causal structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39285)
*S. González-Salud,R. Cartas-Fuentevilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新考察了通过动量空间加权测度构造自由量子场的方法，引入一个独立能量标度，在低动量下恢复常规理论。所得场族由无量纲谱函数刻画，并以欧氏Snyder代数的空间权重为特例，讨论了修正色散关系、非线性动量坐标及观者协变谱权，给出了复克莱因-戈登场在任意空间维数下的等时代数、守恒加权可观测量和两点函数，确立了紫外有限性条件，并用寻常Pauli-Jordan分布的卷积分析非局域因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自由量子场理论在动量空间中采用标准测度，缺乏一个独立可调的能量标度来刻画高能（紫外）区域的非平凡行为。作者希望系统性地引入动量空间加权测度与谱函数，从而在保留低能常规理论的同时，获得一类更一般的量子场构造，并借此探究与欧氏Snyder代数、修正色散关系及非线性动量坐标之间的联系，为可能的紫外有限化与因果结构修正提供框架。

Method: 通过动量空间加权测度定义自由量子场，引入独立能量标度和无量纲谱函数来参数化场族；以欧氏Snyder代数对应的空间权重作为特例。建立谱权重与修正色散关系、非线性动量坐标的对应关系，并利用类时四速度定义观者协变的谱权重。针对复克莱因-戈登场，推导任意空间维数下的等时对易代数、守恒加权可观测量和真空涨落及两点函数；确立这些量的紫外有限性条件；将非局域因果结构表达为寻常Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积，并给出相互作用的说明性推广与核函数、振幅的数值图像。

Result: 得到一类由无量纲谱函数刻画、包含欧氏Snyder空间权重为特例的自由量子场构造；建立了谱权重与修正色散关系、非线性动量坐标的对应，并定义了观者协变谱权重。给出了复克莱因-戈登场在任意空间维数的等时代数、守恒加权可观测量、真空涨落和两点函数的显式表达式，并确立了保证其紫外有限性的条件。非局域因果结构被表示为寻常Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积，并提供了相互作用的说明性推广以及核与振幅的数值图像。

Conclusion: 动量空间加权测度为自由量子场提供了一条统一的、以无量纲谱函数参数化的推广路径，在低能处回归常规理论，并能将欧氏Snyder代数空间权重作为自然特例纳入。由此产生的理论具有可明确刻画的紫外有限性条件和非局域因果结构，且后者可由标准Pauli-Jordan分布经空间卷积理解，显示出这类构造在正则结构、可观测量和相互作用方面具有自洽且可计算的特征。

Abstract: We reconsider the construction of free quantum fields through momentum-space weighted measures, introducing an independent energy scale that restores the conventional theory at low momenta. The resulting family is characterized by a dimensionless spectral function and includes, as a distinguished case, the spatial weight associated with the Euclidean Snyder algebra. We discuss its relation to modified dispersion relations and nonlinear momentum coordinates, and introduce an observer-covariant spectral weight defined using a timelike four-velocity. For the complex Klein-Gordon field we obtain the equal-time algebra, conserved weighted observables, vacuum fluctuations, and two-point functions in arbitrary spatial dimension. The ultraviolet conditions for their finiteness are established, and the nonlocal causal structure is analyzed in terms of a spatial convolution of the ordinary Pauli-Jordan distribution. An illustrative interacting extension and numerical profiles of the kernels and amplitudes are also presented.

</details>


### [106] [Does SO(2,2) symmetry lead to an additional U(1) symmetry in 5-dimensional Einstein-scalar theories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39400)
*Saeedeh Sadeghian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文运用李对称方法研究五维含标量场的爱因斯坦方程，构造出一个同时依赖于 θ 和 ψ 坐标的解，以此作为反例，反驳了文献 [Sadeghian:2014] 中关于 SO(2,2) 对称性隐含额外 U(1) 对称性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维（四维以上）含物质场的爱因斯坦方程求解困难，而对称性（如近极端黑洞近视界几何中的 SO(2,2) 对称性）可简化计算。已有工作对含标量和 Maxwell 场的 SO(2,2) 对称解进行分类；对 Einstein-Maxwell-Lambda 理论，已有定理表明 SO(2,2) 对称性必然导致额外的 U(1) 对称性，从而解是唯一的轴对称解。然而在五维含标量场情形下，SO(2,2) 对称群不够大，无法保证解的唯一性，额外 U(1) 对称性在文献中仅作为猜想提出。本文旨在检验并挑战这一猜想。

Method: 利用求解偏微分方程的李对称方法研究五维含标量场的爱因斯坦理论的爱因斯坦方程，寻找并构造违反该猜想的解。

Result: 找到了一个同时依赖于 θ 和 ψ 两个坐标的解，该解不具备额外的 U(1) 对称性，从而构成对文献 [Sadeghian:2014] 中猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 在五维含标量场的情况下，SO(2,2) 对称性不足以保证额外的 U(1) 对称性，文献中的猜想不成立；说明标量场的存在使得该对称群无法唯一确定解。

Abstract: Solving Einstein equations including the matter fields in dimensions higher than four is computationally challenging. However, the existence of symmetries facilitates these calculations. Among these symmetries, we can mention the SO(2,2) symmetry that arises in the near-horizon geometries of extremal black holes with vanishing horizon area in four dimensions \cite{EVH/CFT} and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2015}. Solutions of Einstein theories including scalar and Maxwell fields with SO(2,2) symmetry have been classified in four, five, and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. For the Einstein-Maxwell-Lambda theory, a theorem states that SO(2,2) symmetry leads to an additional U(1) symmetry and therefore unique axisymmetric solutions. Nevertheless, in the presence of a scalar field, this symmetry group is not large enough in five dimensions to guarantee solution uniqueness, and the additional U(1) symmetry remains a conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. Here, we study the Einstein equations of theories with a five-dimensional scalar field using the Lie symmetry method for solving partial differential equations. We challenge the conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014} and find a solution that depends on both $θ$ and $ψ$ coordinates as a counterexample to that conjecture.

</details>


### [107] [Exact holographic superfluid at the Bogomolny point: transport, large charge, and Rényi entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40371)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在探针极限下研究AdS$_5$中的Abelian Higgs模型：当标量质量饱和Breitenlohner-Freedman界且四次耦合取Bogomolny值时，可在所有静态、无电荷、具有最大对称空间截面的AdS$_5$背景上解析求得其凝聚相，并证明其线性稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常Abelian Higgs超流全息模型需要数值求解。作者希望利用标量质量饱和BF界且四次耦合为Bogomolny值的特殊情形，在不同AdS$_5$切片上获得凝聚相的解析闭式解，从而无需数值计算即可回答对偶CFT中的多种物理问题。

Method: 在探针极限下取标量质量饱和BF界、四次耦合为Bogomolny值，在平面黑膜、全局AdS$_5$和双曲切片三类具有最大对称空间截面的静态无电荷背景上构造解析凝聚解；通过线性稳定性分析证明任何无节点的探针极限凝聚相（标准量子化、非负四次耦合、无超流）均稳定；并利用解析解计算超流分数、声速与衰减、标度维数和Rényi熵等物理量。

Result: 平面黑膜上该解给出T_c以下所有温度的超流：超流分数精确满足Gorter-Casimir二流体定律，声速和衰减有闭式解。全局AdS$_5$中描述R×S^3上电荷为Q的状态（可能是能量最低态），并在经典体层面给出所有Q处的闭式标度维数。其激发中包含超流有效理论无法描述的塔，源于探针区的红外不动点。双曲切片给出计算球区域带电Rényi熵的黑洞的均匀毛发相闭式解，凝聚在纠缠面上不消失；当由该处插入的算符驱动时，在零化学势下相变恰好发生在Rényi指标为1处，与中性标量的结果一致。

Conclusion: 在BF界饱和与Bogomolny耦合的特殊情形下，Abelian Higgs模型在全息超流、全局电荷态和带电Rényi熵等多个物理问题中均存在解析可解的凝聚相，且证明其线性稳定，从而为强耦合超流及相关CFT量提供了全新的解析结果。

Abstract: The Abelian Higgs model in AdS$_5$, in the probe limit, is a standard holographic model of a strongly coupled superfluid, usually solved numerically. When the scalar mass saturates the Breitenlohner-Freedman bound and the quartic coupling takes the Bogomolny value, its condensed phase can be found in closed form on every static, uncharged AdS$_5$ background with maximally symmetric spatial sections. We prove that it is linearly stable, as is any nodeless probe-limit condensate in the standard quantization with a nonnegative quartic coupling and no superflow. On each slicing it answers a different question about the dual conformal field theory. On the planar black brane it is the superfluid at all temperatures below $T_c$: the superfluid fraction follows the Gorter-Casimir two-fluid law exactly, and the speed and the attenuation of sound are found in closed form. In global AdS$_5$ it describes a state of charge $Q$ on $\mathbb{R}\times S^3$, arguably the lowest-energy one, and gives its scaling dimension in closed form at all $Q$, classically in the bulk. Its excitations include towers that the superfluid effective theory does not describe; they come from an infrared fixed point of the probe sector. The hyperbolic slicing gives in closed form a homogeneous hairy phase of the black holes that compute the charged Rényi entropies of a ball. Its condensate does not vanish at the entangling surface. When sourced by operators inserted there, the phase sets in at Rényi index exactly one at zero chemical potential, as found for a neutral scalar.

</details>


### [108] [Absorption of bound supernova axion-like particles by the neutron star weakens the heating limits on their photon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39402)
*Probit J Kalita,Subha Mitra,Bharat Kumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Bose 等人提出的"束缚 ALP 云通过光子衰变加热老年中子星以约束 ALP-光子耦合"的方案，指出其忽略了恒星对 ALP 的吸收。作者证明，轨道进入恒星的束缚 ALP 会因逆核子轫致辐射在数分钟内被吸收，远早于衰变，因此只有诞生于最终恒星之外且近圆轨道的 ALP 才能存活并贡献加热。重新自洽计算后，PSR J2144−3933 仍排除一个 ALP 质量窗口，但其光子耦合下限比无吸收情形高 280–700 倍；介子转换和 Δ(1232) 共振会将该窗口扩展至 260 MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bose 等人的先前工作忽略了新生成中子星对束缚 ALP 的吸收，仅考虑其衰变产生的光子对老年中子星的再加热，从而推导出过强的 ALP-光子耦合限制。作者旨在指出这一关键遗漏，并通过自洽计算（原中子星演化、ALP 产生、广义相对论轨道与吸收）给出修正后的、更可靠的约束。

Method: 作者对 12 条原中子星演化轨迹、3 种归一化到相同超新星光 ALP 能量损失的核子轫致辐射率，以及含/不含 Δ(1232) 等重子态的物态方程，自行计算原中子星演化、ALP 产生、广义相对论轨道及逆核子轫致辐射吸收。重点论证：进入恒星、半径达 50 km 的束缚 ALP 在 PSR J2144−3933 约 333 Myr 寿命内会穿越恒星约 6×10^18 次，从而在数分钟内被完全吸收；存活粒子仅限诞生于最终恒星之外、近圆轨道的 ALP，其携带的加热能量仅占 9×10^-9 至 6×10^-5。

Result: PSR J2144−3933 仍能排除 m_a ≈ 7.7–196 MeV 的光子耦合窗口，但其下限比无吸收情形高 280–700 倍。计入介子转换与 Δ(1232) 共振后（它们对存活 ALP 产生的增强超过对吸收的增强），排除窗口扩大至 m_a ≈ 7.7–260 MeV。该窗口对轫致辐射率与最终恒星半径的依赖程度相近，并要求存在一个直接光子耦合以抵消 ALP-介子混合所诱导的耦合，而现有超新星伽马射线限制可能已覆盖该窗口。

Conclusion: 考虑恒星吸收后，Bose 等人基于 ALP 衰变再加热导出的强限制被大幅削弱（耦合下限提高 280–700 倍），但 PSR J2144−3933 仍排除一个有限的质量窗口。该窗口的存在依赖于直接光子耦合对 ALP-介子混合诱导耦合的抵消，且可能已处于现有超新星伽马射线观测的排除范围内。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) that couple to protons are produced in a core-collapse supernova, and the slow ones remain bound to the new neutron star. Bose, Ganguly, Kumar, and Raj [arXiv:2610.30255] proposed that the photons from the decay of this bound cloud reheat old neutron stars, and they derived strong limits on the ALP-photon coupling. They neglected the absorption of the ALPs by the star, but a bound ALP on an orbit that enters the star and reaches 50 km crosses it about $6\times 10^{18}$ times over the 333 Myr age of PSR J2144$-$3933. We show that inverse nucleon bremsstrahlung in the cold star absorbs every ALP whose orbit enters it within minutes, long before it decays, so only ALPs born outside the final star on nearly circular orbits survive; they carry $9\times 10^{-9} - 6\times 10^{-5}$ of the heating. We compute the protoneutron-star evolution, the production, the general-relativistic orbits and the absorption ourselves, for twelve protoneutron-star evolutions, three nucleon-bremsstrahlung rates normalized to the same supernova energy loss of light ALPs, and equations of state with and without $Δ(1232)$ isobars. PSR J2144$-$3933 still excludes a window at $m_a\simeq 7.7-196$\ MeV, with a lower edge in the photon coupling $280-700$ times higher than without absorption. Pion conversion and the $Δ(1232)$ resonance, which raise the production of surviving ALPs more than their absorption, widen this window to $m_a\simeq$ $7.7-260$\ MeV. The window depends at similar levels on the bremsstrahlung rate and on the radius of the final star. It requires a direct photon coupling that cancels the one induced by ALP-pion mixing, and existing supernova gamma-ray limits may already cover it.

</details>


### [109] [Carrollian CFTs and the Light Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39450)
*Arjun Bagchi,Kinjal Banerjee,Sourish Banerjee,Meghaa AK*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用4维庞加莱代数与3维共形卡罗尔代数之间的同构关系，建立光前物理与卡罗尔共形场论（Carrollian CFT）之间的联系，并基于光前文献构造了连接大量体散射的卡罗尔CFT新表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡罗尔对称性在构建渐近平坦时空（AFS）的全息对偶中起关键作用，其核心在于AFS的（渐近）等距群与共形卡罗尔代数的同构。同时，卡罗尔对称性也作为光前坐标下庞加莱代数的余维一子代数自然出现。作者希望利用这一同构，把光前上的物理与卡罗尔CFT正式连接起来，从而为AFS的全息对偶提供新的构造方案。

Method: 基于4d庞加莱代数与3d共形卡罗尔代数之间的同构，作者借鉴光前文献中的洞见，构造了卡罗尔CFT的新表示，使其与大量体散射相联系；进而计算两点函数，并建立3d卡罗尔CFT与生活在AFS中的4d光前理论之间的映射。

Result: 成功建立了光前物理与卡罗尔CFT之间的连接，构造出与大质量体散射相连的卡罗尔CFT新表示，计算了两点函数，并给出了3d卡罗尔CFT与4d光前理论（位于AFS中）之间的映射。

Conclusion: 通过庞加莱与共形卡罗尔代数的同构，光前物理可作为连接卡罗尔CFT与渐近平坦时空全息对偶的桥梁，该框架为研究AFS全息及大质量体散射提供了新的表示途径。

Abstract: Carrollian symmetries play an important role in building holographic duals to asymptotically flat spacetimes (AFS). This construction hinges on the isomorphism between the (asymptotic) isometries of AFS and the conformal Carroll algebra. Carroll symmetries also naturally arise on the lightfront, as co-dimension one subalgebras in the Poincare algebra written in lightcone coordinates. In this paper, building on the isomorphism between four dimensional (4d) Poincare and 3d conformal Carroll algebras, we make a connection between physics on the lightfront and Carrollian CFTs. Using insights from the lightfront literature, we construct new representations of Carroll CFTs that connect to massive bulk scattering. We compute two-point functions and build a map between 3d Carroll CFTs and 4d lightfront theories living in AFS.

</details>


### [110] [Large-$N$ limit of chiral quiver theories: free energy, supersymmetric indices and operator counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39569)
*Edoardo Colombo,Hyojoong Kim,Nakwoo Kim,Hoseob Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文首次给出了一类具有M-理论对偶的手征𝒩=2 Chern-Simons-物质理论在S³自由能大N鞍点方程的解析解，解决了与预期N^{3/2}标度长期存在的矛盾，并验证了全息预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决手征Chern-Simons-物质理论矩阵模型的大N鞍点方程缺乏解析解的问题，以及长期存在的矩阵模型长程力与M-理论预期N^{3/2}标度之间的张力。

Method: 聚焦于对偶AdS₄×Q^{1,1,1}/Z_k的手征箭图（quiver）理论，求解其大N鞍点方程，展示矩阵模型长程力在大N时如何相消，并推广到S²×S¹上的超共形指标与拓扑扭曲指标；同时进行有限N数值分析加以支持。

Result: 所得自由能对任意trial R-charge分配均精确匹配全息预言；同一鞍点决定S²×S¹指标的大N及Cardy极限；解析解与算符计数法（operator counting）的预测一致，为该猜想在非手征箭图之外提供了有力证据。

Conclusion: 成功找到了具有M-理论对偶的手征𝒩=2理论首个解析鞍点解，确认N^{3/2}标度，并强烈支持算符计数方法猜想在更广泛箭图类中的正确性。

Abstract: We find the first analytic solution to the large-$N$ saddle point equations for the $S^3$ free energy of a chiral $\mathcal N=2$ Chern-Simons-matter theory with an M-theory dual. Focusing on the chiral quiver dual to AdS$_4\times Q^{1,1,1}/\mathbb Z_{\rm k}$, we show how the long-range forces of the matrix model cancel at large $N$, resolving a long-standing tension with the expected $N^{3/2}$ scaling. The resulting free energy precisely matches the holographic prediction for all assignments of trial R-charges. Our results extend to the superconformal and topologically twisted indices on $S^2\times S^1$, whose large-$N$ and Cardy limit is determined by the same saddle. The solution agrees with the predictions of the operator counting method, giving strong evidence for this conjecture beyond non-chiral quivers. We support the analytic solution with a finite-$N$ numerical analysis for multiple assignments of R-charges, extending recent numerical results obtained for this quiver.

</details>


### [111] [Superstring Field Theory with Double Background Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39594)
*Chris Hull*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种广义化的超弦场论，其两个解耦扇区（物理超弦和影子扇区）可分别围绕不同的背景共形场论展开，并且该理论在各自背景的独立边际形变下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超弦场论包含物理超弦与影子扇区两个解耦部分，但两者都围绕同一背景共形场论展开，受限于共同的边际形变不变性。作者希望推广该理论，使两个扇区能够独立地围绕不同背景展开，并各自保持边际形变不变性，以获得更一般的框架。

Method: 作者首先给出了一个在两个扇区之间保持对称的作用量形式；随后对该理论进行广义化，允许两个扇区分别围绕两个不同的背景共形场论展开，并论证了理论在两个背景各自的独立边际形变下的不变性。

Result: 成功构造出两扇区对称的作用量形式，并将其推广为可在两个不同背景上分别展开的理论，且证明了该理论在两组背景独立边际形变下均保持不变。

Conclusion: 该广义化超弦场论解除了两扇区共享同一背景的限制，允许物理超弦和影子扇区各自围绕不同背景展开，同时保持对独立边际形变的不变性，为超弦场论的进一步研究提供了更灵活的框架。

Abstract: A recently constructed superstring field theory has two decoupled sectors, the physical superstring and a shadow sector with a second copy of the superstring. These are both expanded around the same background conformal field theory and the theory is independent of marginal deformations of this common background. A form of the action is presented that is symmetric between the two sectors. A generalisation of this theory is given in which the two sectors can be expanded around two different backgrounds, and the theory is independent under separate marginal deformations of the two background conformal field theories.

</details>


### [112] [Majorana Neutrino Physics and Matter-Antimatter Asymmetry in Type IIA String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39804)
*Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 IIA 型弦论 T^6/(Z_2×Z_2) 定向型流形上的相交 D6-膜模型中研究中微子质量与物质-反物质不对称。E2 瞬子(Sp(2) Chan-Paton 对称性)同时生成 seesaw 机制与 Weinberg 算符；通过匹配而非紧致化计算确定味矢量，结果属于存在性证明。匹配质量平方差选出正序(seesaw 主导)情形，Swampland 判据约束瞬子作用量与再热温度。基准例复现中微子参数，给出 η_B≈8.6×10^{-11}，与观测同量级，并预言无中微子双β衰变有效质量 ⟨m_{ββ}⟩=2.594 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将中微子质量生成机制(seesaw 与 Weinberg 算符)以及宇宙物质-反物质不对称(leptogenesis)同时嵌入到一个现实的弦论紧致化框架中，检验相交 D6-膜构造能否自洽地解释中微子振荡实验与重子不对称观测。

Method: 在 T^6/(Z_2×Z_2) 定向型流形上构造相交 D6-膜模型，利用具有 Sp(2) Chan-Paton 对称性的 Euclidean D2 瞬子同时生成 seesaw 机制与 Weinberg 算符。味矢量不经紧致化计算，而是通过与实测中微子参数匹配来确定，因此该工作属于存在性证明。利用测得的质量平方差选择正序、seesaw 主导情形，并用 Swampland 判据约束瞬子作用量与再热温度，最后进行参数扫描与基准例验证。

Result: 匹配实测质量平方差选出 seesaw 主导的正序情形；基准示例复现了中微子参数，得到重子不对称 η_B≈8.6×10^{-11}(与观测值在同一数量级内)，并预言 ⟨m_{ββ}⟩=2.594 meV。参数扫描表明质量层级是被容纳(accommodated)而非被预言(predicted)。

Conclusion: 在相交 D6-膜弦论框架下，E2 瞬子能够同时驱动 seesaw 与 Weinberg 算符从而解释中微子质量和物质-反物质不对称。该构造可作为一个存在性证明，但味矢量需靠匹配确定，且质量层级是被容纳而非预言，揭示了模型的局限与进一步改进方向。

Abstract: We study Majorana neutrino masses and the matter-antimatter asymmetry in Type IIA string theory on a $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold with intersecting D6-branes. Euclidean D2-instantons with $\text{Sp}(2)$ Chan-Paton symmetry generate both the seesaw mechanism and the Weinberg operator. The flavour vectors are not computed from a compactification but fixed by matching, so our results are an existence proof. Matching the measured mass-squared differences selects the seesaw-dominated case with normal ordering. Swampland criteria constrain the instanton action and the reheating temperature. A benchmark example reproduces the neutrino parameters, gives $η_B \approx 8.6 \times 10^{-11}$, within an order of magnitude of observation, and predicts $\langle m_{ββ} \rangle =2.594 \text{meV}$. A parameter scan shows the mass hierarchy is accommodated rather than predicted.

</details>


### [113] [On functional classes in higher-spin theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39851)
*Yu. A. Tatarenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 d 维 Vasiliev 理论中关于理想分解（factorization）所必需的高自旋代数及其扭伴随模的泛函空间刻画，给出了使自由层级分解程序良定义的代数条件，并讨论了所得泛函类与局域性问题（非局域表达式发散、自旋局域表达式良定义）的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vasiliev 理论中的理想分解需要高自旋代数 A 及其扭伴随模 A^tw 的精确泛函空间描述；缺少合适的泛函空间会导致分解程序无法良定义，尤其是与局域性问题相关的非局域表达式可能发散。

Method: 作者要求代数 A 在 Moyal 星积作用下封闭，且 A^tw 是星积 A-模，并施加若干辅助条件（这些可容许函数的条件具有简单的代数形式），从而正确定义自由层级分解程序；其中 A 的类似泛函空间由 Soloviev 曾考虑过。

Result: 在这些代数与模的条件下得到了良定义的自由层级分解程序；并观察到某些非局域表达式因 A^tw 的性质而发散，而自旋局域表达式则是良定义的。

Conclusion: 通过恰当的泛函空间条件可正确定义 Vasiliev 理论的自由层级分解，所得泛函类与局域性问题密切相关：非局域量发散、自旋局域量有限，从而为高自旋理论的局域性刻画提供了依据。

Abstract: The factorization over the ideal in the $d$-dimensional Vasiliev theory requires an accurate description of the higher-spin algebra $\mathcal{A}$ and its twisted adjoint module $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ as certain functional spaces. It is shown that the conditions of $\mathcal{A}$ to be closed under action of the Moyal star product, and of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ to be a star-product $\mathcal{A}$-module along with some auxiliary conditions lead to properly defined free-level factorization procedure. The conditions on admissible functions have simple algebraic form; in the case of $\mathcal{A}$ the analogous functional space has been considered by Soloviev \cite{Soloviev:2013iba}. The relation of the obtained functional classes to the locality problem in the Vasiliev theory is discussed. In particular it is observed that certain non-local expressions diverge due to properties of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ while the spin-local expressions are well-defined.

</details>


### [114] [Initial conditions leave IR imprints in quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39965)
*Manishankar Ailiga,Akash Mondal,Gaurav Narain*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在洛伦兹信号下研究 $\mathbb{R}\times S^3$ 几何的引力路径积分，引入由参数 $c$ 和 $\omega$ 表征的无穷族协变边界作用量，分析其对宇宙波函数单圈修正的影响，并系统计算良态鞍点处的一圈波函数及其紫外/红外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力的路径积分形式需要指定边界条件，而传统无边界（Hartle-Hawking）与隧道（tunnelling）提议在边界项选取及扰动良态性上存在模糊之处。作者希望通过引入无穷族协变边界作用量，系统考察边界选择如何影响宇宙波函数及其微扰的良定义性，从而为宇宙波函数提供更一般的边界框架。

Method: 作者研究洛伦兹号差的 $\mathbb{R}\times S^3$ 几何，度规构型由 lapse、尺度因子及 $S^3$ 超曲面上的涨落表征，并以 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量的指数加权。通过对尺度因子施加特定边界要求，构造无穷族协变边界作用量（参数为 $c$ 与 $\omega$）。路径积分中初始动量的主导贡献将 $\omega$ 限制为半整数。固定 $\omega$ 后得到四条 $c$ 依赖的主导共轭动量分支；对相关 lapse 鞍点做单圈展开，分离紫外发散（其中 $c$ 充当紫外调节子并移动单圈行列式极点），并通过添加合适的抵消项获得紫外有限的无边界与 deSitter 波函数，最后分析红外行为。

Result: 无穷族协变边界作用量中，初始动量的主导贡献限定 $\omega$ 为半整数，从而生成无穷族 $c$ 依赖的共轭动量。在固定 $\omega$ 时出现四条 $c$ 依赖主导分支：两条给出 deSitter 鞍点，一条给出 Hartle-Hawking（无边界）鞍点，一条给出隧道宇宙。对这些 $c$ 依赖的初始共轭动量，lapse 鞍点均相关，但仅一个 deSitter 鞍点与 Hartle-Hawking 鞍点具有良态微扰。系统计算了这些良态鞍点处的一圈波函数：$c$ 作为紫外调节子并将单圈行列式极点移离实 $q_f$ 轴（$q_f$ 为宇宙最终尺度）。通过添加抵消项得到紫外有限的无边界与 dS 波函数；其红外行为既出现红外发散，也表现出对初始边界选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 协变边界参数 $c$ 与 $\omega$ 为宇宙波函数提供了一族边界条件的系统刻画。$c$ 不仅作为紫外调节子使一圈波函数有限，还影响鞍点结构与微扰良态性；而红外行为对初始边界选择敏感，提示边界条件在量子宇宙学中具有不可忽略的物理含义。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational path integral of $\mathbb{R} \times S^3$ geometries in Lorentzian signature along with the metric fluctuations which live on the $S^3$ hypersurfaces. Each metric configuration is characterised by lapse, scale factor and fluctuations, and is weighted by the exponential of the Einstein-Hilbert action. A particular boundary requirement for the scale-factor is achievable via an infinite family of covariant boundary actions, each restricting the boundary behaviour of the fluctuation field which cannot be arbitrarily chosen. The paper explores the effect of this infinite family of covariant boundary actions on the wavefunction of Universe to one-loop. These boundary actions are characterized by parameters $c$ and $ω$. Dominant contribution of initial momentum in the path-integral restricts $ω$ to be half-integer, leading to an infinite family of $c$-dependent conjugate momenta. In special case for fixed $ω$, four such $c$-dependent dominant branches of conjugate momenta emerge: two lead to deSitter, one leads to Hartle-Hawking and one to the tunnelling Universe. For all these $c$-dependent initial conjugate momenta, the lapse-saddles are relevant, however only, one of deSitter saddle and Hartle Hawking saddle have well-behaved perturbations. The one-loop wavefunction at these well-behaved saddles is computed systematically by isolating the UV-divergences, where the covariant boundary parameter $c$ acts as a UV-regulator and also helps in shifting the poles of one-loop determinant, away from the real $q_f$ axis for dS saddles, where $q_f$ is the final size of the Universe. UV-finite wavefunction for no-boundary and dS is obtained by adding suitable counterterms. The infrared behaviour of these wavefunctions show not only IR-divergences but also sensitivity toward the initial boundary choices.

</details>


### [115] [The Effective Kerr-Compton Tree Amplitude from Black Hole Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40101)
*N. Emil J. Bjerrum-Bohr,Gang Chen,Konstantinos Papadimos,Nabha Shah,Chenliang Su*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在PM有效场论框架下构造克尔黑洞引力波散射树级Compton振幅的新方法：利用黑洞微扰论的波散射函数，通过迭代唯一确定额外的接触项，并将结果推广至自旋六阶（含此前未知的五、六阶扇区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前构造克尔黑洞Compton振幅的方法依赖于振幅的物理一致性条件，但无法唯一确定高阶自旋部分所需的额外接触项，因此需要新方法完整确定高阶展开。

Method: 在post-Minkowskian有效场论中，利用黑洞微扰论得到的波散射函数（wave scattering functions），结合物理一致性条件，通过迭代程序唯一确定额外的接触项，从而构造树级Compton振幅。

Result: 导出至自旋六阶的显式表达式，包括此前未知的五、六阶自旋扇区；振幅呈现复杂的超越结构，并在六阶自旋出现主导的耗散接触项；结果与文献已知结果一致，并复现了黑洞微扰论的单圈tail结果。

Conclusion: 该方法能系统且唯一地构造高阶自旋的Compton振幅，验证了已有结果并拓展到新的自旋阶数，同时复现单圈tail结果，为理解旋转黑洞散射提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We develop a new method to construct the tree-level Compton amplitude for gravitational-wave scattering off a Kerr black hole in post-Minkowskian effective field theory. Building on previous computations that required physical consistency of the amplitude, the method uses wave scattering functions from black hole perturbation theory to uniquely determine additional contact terms. We illustrate the iterative procedure by deriving explicit expressions up to sixth order in spin, including previously unknown sectors at the fifth and sixth orders in spin. The amplitude features complex transcendental structures and a leading dissipative contact term at sixth order in spin. We verify our result against known results in the literature and reproduce the one-loop tail result from black hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [116] [$\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills Flux Tube in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40156)
*Victor Gorbenko,Orr Sela,Brian R. Zielazienski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 AdS₄ 中 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论边界上的超对称 Wilson 线（等价于半空间上的理论），作为探索全息弦与禁闭通量管之间联系的一个模型，在平面极限下计算缺陷谱与超共形指标，并提出强弱耦合算子映射，揭示世界面轴子的双粒子态性质及从指数增长谱到 Cardy 谱的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界（半空间）破坏四维共形对称性，预计会去除可积性，使系统更接近禁闭弦，同时保留足够的超对称性来约束动力学，从而为研究禁闭规范理论中预期的谱重整化提供一个非可积的超对称模型。

Method: 在平面极限下，分别在弱耦合和强耦合处分析缺陷谱并计算超共形指标；通过微扰规范理论描述与长全息弦世界面激发两种互补视角，建立两套描述之间的算子映射。

Result: 在强耦合下，世界面轴子（axion）成为一个双粒子态；结果表明从规范理论的指数增长谱到弦世界面的 Cardy 型谱存在平滑插值。

Conclusion: 该系统提供了一个非可积的超对称模型，支持从指数增长的规范理论谱平滑过渡到弦世界面 Cardy 谱的图景，可用于研究禁闭规范理论中预期的谱重整化。

Abstract: We study a supersymmetric Wilson line on the boundary of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory in AdS$_4$, or equivalently on a half-space, as a model for exploring the connection between holographic strings and confining flux tubes. The boundary breaks four-dimensional conformal symmetry and is expected to remove integrability, bringing this system closer to a confining string while preserving enough supersymmetry to constrain its dynamics. Working in the planar limit, we analyze the defect spectrum at weak and strong coupling and compute the superconformal index, thereby relating the perturbative gauge-theory description to the worldsheet excitations of a long holographic string. These complementary perspectives allow us to propose a map between operators in the two regimes, under which the worldsheet axion, in particular, becomes a two-particle state at strong coupling. The resulting picture supports a smooth interpolation from an exponentially growing gauge-theory spectrum to the Cardy-like spectrum of a string worldsheet, providing a non-integrable supersymmetric model for investigating the spectral reorganization expected in confining gauge theories.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum curves of 6d SCFTs on a -3 curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40203)
*Minsung Kim,Sung-Soo Kim,Xin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了 6d N=(1,0) SCFT 中半 BPS 缺陷与量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线，利用推广的 blowup 方程计算 R^4 × T^2 上余维 4 和余维 2 缺陷的配分函数，并将其 Nekrasov-Shatashvili 极限所满足的椭圆差分方程诠释为量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 6d N=(1,0) 超共形场论中带缺陷的量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线，是为了理解椭圆紧化下缺陷配分函数所满足的量子化几何结构，并将此前 blowup 方程方法推广到含缺陷情形，从而系统地刻画 Higgsing 树上一系列规范理论（如 su(3)、g_2、so(N)）的量子曲线。

Method: 作者利用推广的 blowup 方程计算 R^4 × T^2 上（-3 曲线支撑的 6d 理论）含余维 4 与余维 2 半 BPS 缺陷的配分函数；取 Nekrasov-Shatashvili 极限后，将缺陷配分函数满足的椭圆差分方程提出为量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线。该方法应用于最小 su(3) 规范理论，以及以最小 su(3) 为端点的 Higgsing 树上的 g_2 与 so(N) 规范理论；并进一步研究了 su(3) 理论在圆周带 Z_2 扭转紧化时的缺陷与量子曲线。

Result: 研究给出了多个 6d N=(1,0) 理论在含缺陷情形下的配分函数，并导出对应的量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线（椭圆差分方程）：涵盖最小 su(3)、g_2）、so(N) 规范理论，以及带 Z_2 扭转的圆紧化 su(3) 理论，验证了构造在这些理论中的一致性。

Conclusion: 推广的 blowup 方程为计算 6d N=(1,0) 理论中缺陷配分函数及其量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线提供了有效框架；半 BPS 缺陷的 Nekrasov-Shatashvili 极限满足椭圆差分方程，可作为量子 Seiberg-Witten 曲线的定义，且该构造在 Higgsing 树上多种规范理论及 Z_2 扭转紧化情形下均成立。

Abstract: We study half-BPS defects and quantum Seiberg-Witten curves in 6d $ \mathcal{N}=(1,0) $ SCFTs on a $ -3 $ curve. We compute the partition functions in the presence of codimension-4 and codimension-2 defects on $ \mathbb{R}^4 \times T^2 $ using generalized blowup equations. We then propose quantum Seiberg-Witten curves as elliptic difference equations satisfied by the Nekrasov-Shatashvili limits of the defect partition functions. We apply this construction to the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory, and to the $ \mathfrak{g}_2 $ and $ \mathfrak{so}(N) $ gauge theories on a Higgsing tree whose endpoint is the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ theory. We also investigate defects and the quantum curve of the $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory compactified on a circle with a $ \mathbb{Z}_2 $ twist.

</details>


### [118] [Competition and coexistence of baryonic condensates in AdS hardwall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40244)
*Akash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用带全反作用（full backreaction）的五维 AdS 硬墙模型研究冷致密 QCD 中双夸克与双重子凝聚能否共存。结果表明：两者仅在一个很窄的参数窗口中同时凝聚，窗口随电荷增大而变宽，并在两算符重合时关闭；在 μ_B–T 平面上，低温下双凝聚的禁闭相最为有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷致密 QCD 存在多种重子数破缺的序参量（双夸克、双重子凝聚），作者希望明确这些序参量究竟是相互排斥还是共存，从而理解致密物质相结构的竞争机制。

Method: 采用五维 AdS 硬墙模型并计入全反作用，引入两个与共同 U(1)_B 规范场耦合的带电标量场，分别对偶于电荷 q_i、共形维度 Δ_i 的算符。固定双夸克型算符为 q_1=2、Δ_1=3，扫描第二算符的 q_2、Δ_2，考察两算符的凝聚条件及其在禁闭/退禁闭相中的行为。

Result: 两个算符仅在 Δ_2 的一个窄窗口内同时凝聚；该窗口随 q_2 增大而变宽，并在两算符重合（同电荷同维度）时关闭。窗口之外只形成单一凝聚。该结构在禁闭相和退禁闭相中都持续存在。在 μ_B–T 平面内，低温下具有双凝聚的禁闭物质最受青睐，其上是一条仅含单一凝聚的窄退禁闭带。

Conclusion: 通过全反作用的 AdS 硬墙模型，双夸克与双双重子凝聚在一般情况下相互排斥，仅在特定参数窗口内共存；相图显示低温禁闭双凝聚相最稳定，暗示致密 QCD 中的两种重子数破缺序参量存在微妙的竞争与共存关系。

Abstract: Cold dense QCD admits several order parameters for baryon-number breaking, among them diquark and dibaryon condensates. We ask whether they exclude one another or coexist, using a five-dimensional AdS hardwall model with full backreaction in which two charged scalars, dual to operators of charges $q_i$ and dimensions $Δ_i$, couple to a common $U(1)_B$ gauge field. With the diquark-like operator fixed at $q_1=2$, $Δ_1=3$, both operators condense only within a narrow window of $Δ_2$, which widens with $q_2$ and closes when the operators coincide. Outside the window, only a single condensate forms. The structure persists in both confined and deconfined phases. In the $μ_B$-$T$ plane, confined matter with both condensates is preferred at low temperature, below a narrow band of deconfined matter with one condensate.

</details>


### [119] [$\mathcal{N}=2$ conformal higher-spin actions in harmonic superspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40274)
*Evgeny Ivanov,Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在谐波超空间中为 arXiv:2404.19016 引入的 N=2 超共形高阶自旋规范多重态构造了线性化的 4D、N=2 Weyl 型离壳作用量，验证了其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性，并指出向任意 N=2 超引力背景的推广仅在 Weyl 平坦超背景下自洽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在为线性化的 N=2 超共形规范高阶自旋理论建立一个几何框架。此前（arXiv:2511.16325）已为共形 N=2 超引力完成了类似构造，本文作为后续工作，进一步将这一方法推广到高阶自旋规范多重态，并希望为将其提升到完全非线性水平提供可能的途径。

Method: 作者在 4D、N=2 谐波超空间中构造 Weyl 型离壳作用量。关键之处在于：底层预势的结构及其规范与超共形变换完全由"Grassmann 谐波解析性原理"决定，而不变作用量及广义 N=2 超 Weyl 张量的构造则依赖于"Grassmann 半解析性原理"。作者检验了这些新型作用量的超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性，并进一步讨论了向任意 N=2 超引力背景的推广。

Result: 成功构造了线性化 4D、N=2 Weyl 型离壳作用量，并验证了其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性。研究表明，若不做规范变换律的根本修改和/或谐波零曲率方程的改动，线性化自旋-s 规范不变性仅在 N=2 Weyl 平坦超背景下才与任意 N=2 超引力背景自洽。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为线性化 N=2 超共形规范高阶自旋理论提供了新的几何框架，还暗示了将其推广到完全非线性水平的方法。同时指出，在推广到一般 N=2 超引力背景时，若不进行大幅度修改，线性化自旋-s 规范不变性仅能与 N=2 Weyl 平坦超背景相容。

Abstract: We construct linearized $4D, \mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-type off-shell actions in harmonic superspace for the superconformal higher-spin gauge multiplets introduced in arXiv:2404.19016. This work is a followup of our arXiv:2511.16325, where an analogous construction was carried out for conformal $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. We check the superconformal invariance of the new actions and their linearized gauge invariance. As the characteristic features of our formulation, the structure of the underlying prepotentials and their gauge and superconformal transformations are entirely determined by the fundamental \textit{principle of Grassmann harmonic analyticity}, while the construction of the invariant actions and of the generalized $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl tensors relies upon the \textit{principle of Grassmann half-analyticity}. This provides not only a new geometric framework for the linearized $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge higher-spin theories, but also suggests possible ways of promoting them to the full nonlinear level. We consider a generalization to an arbitrary $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity background and show that the linearized spin-$\mathbf{s}$ gauge invariance, without radical modifications of the gauge transformation law and/or harmonic zero-curvature equations, is consistent only with $\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-flat super-backgrounds.

</details>


### [120] [Bounds on the Diagonal Degree of the Biadjoint Scalar Amplitude Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40355)
*Freddy Cachazo,Rajbir Longia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双伴标量理论偏振幅所构成的矩阵 M_n 的可逆对角子矩阵规模问题，证明其"对角阶"Δ_n 相对 CHY 秩 (n-3)! 呈指数级压小，并以上下界形式给出定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHY 构造给出 M_n 的秩为 (n-3)!，而极大秩可逆子矩阵在 KLT 公式（将引力子振幅与胶子振幅联系起来）中起关键作用。为寻找项数最少的 KLT 公式，作者之一提出是否存在临界点 n_c，使所有 n>n_c 时存在秩为 (n-3)! 的对角子矩阵。

Method: 定义 M_n 的最大非奇异对角子矩阵的规模为其"对角阶"Δ_n，通过分析 M_n 的结构推导 Δ_n 关于 CHY 秩 (n-3)! 的比例的渐近上下界。

Result: 文章对该问题给出否定回答：对角阶相对 CHY 秩被指数压小，即 Δ_n/(n-3)! = exp(-Θ(n))（n→∞）。更具体地，建立了上下界 exp(-1.5n) ≤ Δ_n/(n-3)! ≤ exp[-0.01n + O(1)]。

Conclusion: 不存在这样的临界 n_c：对所有足够大的 n，M_n 都不存在秩达到 (n-3)! 的对角子矩阵；对角阶只能以指数级小于 CHY 秩的方式增长。

Abstract: Biadjoint scalar theory partial amplitudes, $m_n(α,β)$, define an $(n-1)!/2\times (n-1)!/2$ matrix, $M_n$, with rows and columns labeled by orderings. The CHY construction of $M_n$ implies that its rank is $(n-3)!$. Invertible submatrices of maximal rank enter in the KLT construction relating graviton amplitudes to gluon amplitudes. Motivated by finding the KLT formula with the smallest number of terms, one of the authors posed the question of whether there is a critical $n_c$ such that for all $n>n_c$ there exists a diagonal submatrix of $M_n$ of rank $(n-3)!$. In this work we obtain bounds on the size of the largest non-singular diagonal submatrix of $M_n$, which we call its diagonal degree $Δ_n$, and we answer the question in the negative. We prove that the diagonal degree is exponentially suppressed relative to the CHY rank: $Δ_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-Θ(n))$ as $n\to \infty$. More explicitly, we establish the bounds $\exp(-1.5n)\leqΔ_n/(n-3)!\leq \exp \left[ -0.01n+O(1)\right]$.

</details>


### [121] [Breaking of integrability in elliptic deformations of AdS3 strings with mixed flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40386)
*Ian Le Meur,Fiona K. Seibold*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了 IIB 型超引力中由混合 NSNS 与 RR 通量支撑的 AdS3×S3×T4 超弦的椭圆形变，并从平坦 Lax 联络与树级世界面 S 矩阵两个角度研究其可积性。结果显示，一般椭圆参数与 NSNS 通量下会出现非弹性 2→2 振幅，破坏可积性；而在 Lax 分析认定的可积情形中这些非弹性振幅消失，树级 S 矩阵满足经典 Yang-Baxter 方程。特别地，在有理与三角模型中保持可积性的 Wess-Zumino 项会破坏椭圆形变的可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 AdS3×S3×T4 超弦背景的可积形变，特别是椭圆形变这一此前较少涉足的类别，旨在厘清 Wess-Zumino 项与 B 场耦合对可积性的影响，并确定何种条件下椭圆形变仍保持可积性。此前有理和三角形变的可积性已较清楚，椭圆情形的可积性条件与量子群对称性结构尚未完全揭示，需要从 Lax 联络与散射矩阵两方面进行检验。

Method: 作者在混合 NSNS 和 RR 通量的 IIB 超引力中构造 AdS3×S3×T4 超弦的椭圆形变，NSNS 部分含 Wess-Zumino 项，并通过要求理论保持 8 个超对称引入耦合形变 AdS3 与 S3 因子的 B 场。可积性研究采用两条独立路径：一是构造平坦 Lax 联络以判定可积条件；二是计算树级世界面 2→2 S 矩阵，检验散射的弹性（elasticity）以及是否满足经典 Yang-Baxter 方程。

Result: 对一般椭圆参数与 NSNS 通量，发现存在非弹性 2→2 振幅，违反可积性所要求的弹性散射性质。在 Lax 分析所确认的可积情形下，这些非弹性振幅恰好消失，且树级 S 矩阵满足经典 Yang-Baxter 方程。关键结论是：在有理与三角模型中能够保持可积性的 Wess-Zumino 项，在椭圆形变中会破坏可积性。

Conclusion: 椭圆形变的可积性与量子群结构对 NSNS 通量及 Wess-Zumino 项的存在高度敏感：只有特定参数取值（由 Lax 联络平坦性界定）才保持可积性，此时散射弹性恢复且满足经典 Yang-Baxter 方程。一个显著差异在于，Wess-Zumino 项在有理/三角形变中无害，却会破坏椭圆情形的可积性，表明椭圆形变对应的可积结构与有理、三角情形存在本质不同。

Abstract: We construct elliptic deformations of the $AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$ superstring supported by mixed NSNS and RR fluxes in type IIB supergravity and investigate their integrability. The NSNS sector includes a Wess-Zumino term, and imposing that the theory preserves 8 supersymmetries also requires the presence of a B-field coupling the deformed $AdS_3$ and $S^3$ factors. We explore integrability from two different perspectives: through the construction of a flat Lax connection and the computation of the tree-level worldsheet S-matrix. For generic elliptic parameters and NSNS flux, we find inelastic $2 \rightarrow 2$ amplitudes, violating the elasticity required by integrability. In the integrable cases as identified by the Lax analysis, these inelastic amplitudes vanish and the tree-level S-matrix satisfies the classical Yang-Baxter equation. In particular, the addition of a Wess-Zumino term, which preserves integrability in the rational and trigonometric models, breaks integrability for elliptic deformations.

</details>


### [122] [Holographic duals of JT gravity at finite cutoff: the classical case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40391)
*Andreas Belaey,Thomas G. Mertens,Lorenzo Russo,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BF一阶形式下构造了带有限截断边界和世界末日膜（end-of-the-world branes）的JT引力，并将其动力学约化为边界上的一维系统，给出了有限截断下全息Schwarzian描述的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用一阶BF表述更清晰地构造带有限截断边界和世界末日膜的JT引力，以获得对各边界对应相空间自由度的几何理解，并借此探测体局域性（bulk locality）与时空的底层结构。

Method: 在一阶BF形式下表述JT引力，引入有限截断边界和世界末日膜；将BF模型动力学约化为一个仅生活在边界上的一维系统（推广了早期构造），从而在相空间中推导有限截断JT引力的全息Schwarzian描述的闭式表达式。

Result: 实现了BF模型向边界一维系统的动态约化，获得每条边界相空间自由度的几何诠释；推导出有限截断下全息Schwarzian描述的闭式表达式，其自由度描述有限截断曲线在AdS$_2$中的嵌入。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解JT引力的边界相空间及体局域性提供了几何与动力学的统一视角；相应系统的量子化在随附论文中给出。

Abstract: We formulate JT gravity with finite-cutoff boundaries and end-of-the-world branes in the first-order BF formulation. We dynamically reduce the BF model into a one-dimensional system living at the boundaries, generalizing earlier constructions. We obtain in particular a geometrical understanding of the phase space degrees of freedom associated to each of these boundaries. We leverage these results to probe bulk locality and the underlying structure of spacetime. Within our framework, we are able to derive a closed-form expression for the holographic Schwarzian description of JT gravity at finite-cutoff in phase space. The resulting degrees of freedom describe the embedding of the finite-cutoff curve in AdS$_2$. A quantization of these systems is presented in a companion paper.

</details>


### [123] [A Simplified Born Rule for Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40412)
*Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者证明平坦时空中等时 in-in 宇宙学关联函数的所有双边界极点会相互抵消，从而得到用"嵌入波函数系数"表示的简化 Born 规则；并在共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论中将其化为均匀加权森林割展开，据此构造 Born 多面体，其典范函数可在匹配刻面支撑后重现任意图的归一化关联函数，并给出相应的弦论公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学关联函数是原初宇宙的自然可观测量，通过 Born 规则与波函数系数相联系。但波函数系数通常含有物理上不应出现的(双)边界极点，需要理解这些极点如何在对 in-in 关联函数求和时抵消；同时希望将 Born 规则改写成极点组合关系更清晰、且显式保留相互作用依赖的形式，以便借助几何/组合方法统一描述任意图的关联函数。

Method: 作者在平坦时空中对等时 in-in 关联函数做图论分析：先证明任意图(含局域导数相互作用)的所有双边界极点在空间圈动量积分之前就已逐项抵消；由此引入"嵌入波函数系数"并导出简化 Born 规则，得到的展开式显式展示哪些物理极点可同时出现并保留相互作用依赖。对共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论，进一步把简化 Born 规则整理为均匀加权森林割展开，利用其极点组合学构造 Born 多面体，并以其典范函数再现归一化关联函数。

Result: 证明了平坦时空等时 in-in 关联函数中所有双边界极点对任意图(含局域导数相互作用)在积分前即完全抵消；得到以嵌入波函数系数表达的简化 Born 规则及其显式极点组合展开。对共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论，简化 Born 规则表现为均匀加权的森林割展开；由此构造的 Born 多面体，其典范函数在将刻面支撑匹配到物理能量后可精确重现任意图的归一化关联函数，同一几何还导出了关联函数的弦论公式。

Conclusion: 双边界极点的普遍抵消使 Born 规则得以简化为嵌入波函数系数形式，从而把关联函数的极点组合结构显式化；在 Tr(φ³) 情形下，这一结构由 Born 多面体的组合几何完全刻画，典范函数给出任意图归一化关联函数，并自然引出弦论描述，提示宇宙学关联函数可能存在统一的几何/组合与弦论框架。

Abstract: Cosmological correlators are natural observables of the primordial universe and are related to wavefunction coefficients by the Born rule. For equal-time in-in correlators in flat spacetime, we prove that all double-boundary poles cancel for arbitrary graphs, including in theories with local derivative interactions. These cancellations occur before integration over spatial loop momenta and yield a $\textit{simplified Born rule}$ in terms of what we call embedded wavefunction coefficients. The resulting expansion reveals which physical poles can occur together while keeping the interaction dependence explicit. For conformally coupled $\operatorname{Tr}(φ^3)$ theory in flat spacetime, the simplified Born rule takes the form of a uniformly weighted forest-cut expansion. From its pole combinatorics, we construct what we call the $\textit{Born polytope}$, whose canonical function reproduces the normalized correlator for arbitrary graphs after matching its facet supports to the physical energies. The same geometry also yields a stringy formula for correlators.

</details>


### [124] [Generalised $Ω$-backgrounds in twisted $N=4$ super Yang-Mills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40465)
*Elisa Iris Marieni,Itamar Yaakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入刚性超引力背景，用以变形4d N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的三种拓扑扭曲，并将N=2规范理论的Ω形变推广到N=4情形，同时推导了Weyl多重态和物质多重态在刚性玻色背景下的扭曲超对称变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将N=2规范理论中重要的Ω形变机制推广到4d N=4超杨-米尔斯理论，并为其三种不等价拓扑扭曲提供一致的刚性超引力背景框架，从而为研究N=4理论在弯曲背景或形变时空中的局域化、指标等提供基础。

Method: 构建4d N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的刚性超引力背景；针对三种不等价的拓扑扭曲分别推导其扭曲后的超对称变换；明确给出N=4 Weyl多重态以及耦合到刚性玻色背景的N=4物质多重态的超对称变分。

Result: 成功推广了Ω形变至N=4情形，得到了三种不等价拓扑扭曲下N=4 Weyl多重态与物质多重态在刚性背景中的完整扭曲超对称变换，并进一步讨论了一些更奇异的(exotic)背景。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论拓扑扭曲的刚性超引力背景与Ω形变推广的框架，为后续利用局域化等技术研究N=4理论的非微扰性质铺平了道路。

Abstract: We introduce rigid supergravity backgrounds for 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills which deform its topological twists and generalise the $Ω$-deformation of $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theories. We derive the twisted supersymmetry variations of the $\mathcal{N}=4$ Weyl multiplet, and the $\mathcal{N}=4$ matter multiplet coupled to a rigid bosonic background, for each of the three inequivalent topological twists. Other, more exotic backgrounds are also discussed.

</details>


### [125] [Thermodynamics of Swirling Black Holes and Kerr-Taub-NUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40529)
*Ernesto Frodden,Diego Hidalgo,Francisco Manzano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对Kerr旋涡(KS)、加速Kerr旋涡(AKS)及Kerr-Taub-NUT(KTN)黑洞建立黑洞热力学第一定律，采用表面电荷方法在存在弦状缺陷(锥奇点)时计算质量与角动量，发现KS和KTN的"熵"偏离面积的四分之一，而正则的AKS满足通常的熵面积定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现的KS与AKS黑洞解亟需热力学第一定律的建立；同时KTN时空中的弦状缺陷(锥奇点)与非平直渐近区域使得标准电荷定义和第一定律难以直接套用。作者希望遵循Noether关于全局对称性产生守恒律的洞见，在存在弦缺陷和非平直渐近背景下仍能给出有限、准局域的守恒荷，并澄清"熵是否仍为视界面积的四分之一"这一关键问题。

Method: 采用并改造表面电荷方法，在KS与KTN沿轴存在时空弦状缺陷(锥奇点)的情况下准局域地计算有限的质量与角动量积分，避免依赖非平直的渐近区域；建立不引入与对称性无关的独立"弦项"的第一定律。对AKS(可在奇点外正则化且无弦缺陷)则先用Christodoulou-Ruffini公式确定质量，再与表面电荷方法的结果相互校验。

Result: KS与KTN中扮演黑洞熵角色的量与"视界面积四分之一"这一简单几何规则不一致，原因在于保留了一一对应守恒律的第一定律电荷；这表明标准公式不能朴素地用于带弦状缺陷的黑洞——它们拓扑不同，暗示需要新的几何框架。相反，AKS时空可在奇点外正则化且无弦缺陷，其电荷与以通常面积四分之一为熵的第一定律一致。

Conclusion: 在带弦状缺陷的KS和KTN黑洞中，熵不再等于视界面积的四分之一，提示需要超越标准几何框架的新表述；而无弦缺陷的正则AKS黑洞则恢复标准的熵面积定律，验证了表面电荷方法与Christodoulou-Ruffini质量定义的一致性。

Abstract: We establish the first law of black hole thermodynamics for the recently discovered Kerr swirling (KS) and accelerated Kerr swirling (AKS) black hole solutions. We also revisit the first law for the Kerr-Taub-NUT (KTN) spacetime. Guided by Noether's original insight that global symmetries give rise to conservation laws, we employ and adapt the surface charge method for computing the associated charges (mass and angular momentum) in the presence of spacetime string-like defects (conical singularities) along the axes of both KS and KTN black holes. We obtain finite integrated charges in a quasi-local manner, without relying on the non-flat asymptotic regions characteristic of these spacetimes. We then establish a first law without introducing independent stringy terms unrelated to symmetries. For both KS and KTN, the quantities that play the role of the black hole entropy differ from the simple geometrical rule of one-fourth of the horizon area. This mismatch arises because we keep first-law charges associated with conservation laws. It indicates that standard formulas may not be naively used for black holes with string-like defects; they have a different topology and suggest the need for a new geometrical framework. In stark contrast, for the AKS spacetime, which can be regularized outside the singularity and lacks string-like defects, we find that the charges are consistent with a first law having the usual one-fourth of the area entropy. We first determine the mass using the Christodoulou-Ruffini formula and then check its agreement with the result obtained from the surface charge method.

</details>


### [126] [On Geometric Observables in Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40615)
*Manthos Karydas,Murat Kologlu,Allic Sivaramakrishnan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在微扰量子引力中研究几何可观测量（如距离与时间），通过引力“着装”使其坐标不变，并证明取渐近极限后这些有限距离可观测量化为记忆与Shapiro时间延迟算符，同时计算了相关关联函数中领头阶引力子效应，从而识别出一类连接实验与天球操作的几何可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量（如距离、时间）在经典引力中是核心，且在量子引力中广泛出现，但其引力子修正研究甚少。作者希望填补这一空白，在微扰量子引力框架下系统研究可实际测量的几何可观测量，尤其是存在时空涨落情形下的构造。

Method: 在微扰量子引力中构造刻画测量的距离与时间可观测量，并引入引力“着装”（gravitational dressing）以保证坐标不变性；随后取渐近极限，考察这些有限距离可观测量如何过渡为记忆（memory）与Shapiro时间延迟算符；同时在几何可观测量的关联函数中计算领头阶引力子效应。

Result: 成功构造了坐标不变的、含时空涨落的距离与时间可观测量；证明渐近极限下它们化约为引力记忆与Shapiro时间延迟算符；并给出了几何可观测量关联函数中领头阶引力子修正的计算结果。

Conclusion: 识别出一类几何可观测量，能够在微扰量子引力中连接有限的实验测量与天球（celestial sphere）上的渐近操作，为量子引力中的几何测量提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Measures of geometry like distance and time are staples of classical gravity. While geometric quantities arise broadly in quantum gravity, their graviton corrections are less explored. In this paper, we study geometric observables in perturbative quantum gravity. We form distance and time observables that model measurements, including in the presence of spacetime fluctuations. Gravitational dressing ensures these constructions are coordinate invariant. Then we show how these finite-distance observables become memory and Shapiro time delay operators upon taking asymptotic limits. We compute leading graviton effects in correlators of geometric observables throughout. Altogether, our results identify a class of geometric observables that link experiments and operations on the celestial sphere.

</details>

### [127] [Tuning La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 magnetic anisotropy via interfacial coupling in homometallic trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37736)
*Nunzia Coppola,Giovanni Vinai,Alksandr Yu Petrov,Chiara Sacco,Hafiz Sami Ur Rehman,Sofia Scarnicci,Lucia Capogna,Cinzia Di Giorgio,Carmela Aruta,Luigi Maritato,Alice Galdi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3三层膜中的复杂磁性行为，发现通过调控铁磁层LSMO的厚度可以改变界面作用占比，进而在低温下诱导自旋重取向、界面自旋玻璃态、20倍矫顽场增强与交换偏置，以及各向异性磁电阻响应变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用同金属锰氧化物异质结构来有效调谐和控制磁性行为，揭示结构/轨道重构以及铁磁-反铁磁界面竞争的物理机制。

Method: 制备嵌入两层同金属反铁磁SrMnO3（SMO）之间的铁磁La0.7Sr0.3MnO3（LSMO）三层膜，采用两种不同的LSMO厚度以改变界面作用占比；通过磁光克尔效应（MOKE）和磁电阻测量相结合，研究其磁性、矫顽场、交换偏置及各向异性磁电阻行为。

Result: 在125 K以下观察到LSMO磁易轴偏离室温取向的自旋重取向，归因于界面交换作用压过衬底诱导磁各向异性；界面超交换与交换作用竞争导致高度受挫的界面自旋玻璃态，使矫顽场和交换偏置增强约20倍；界面驱动的磁晶各向异性改变导致各向异性磁电阻响应变化，并主导低温行为。

Conclusion: 通过合理改变界面作用和LSMO层厚度，可有效调谐嵌入反铁磁层中的铁磁锰氧化物层的磁性能，证实异质结构是调控磁性行为的有效策略，与同金属锰氧化物异质结构的既有模型相符。

Abstract: In this work we explore the complex magnetic behaviour of SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3 trilayers combining magneto-optic Kerr effect (MOKE) and magneto-resistance measurements. The interplay of structural and orbital reconstruction and the competition of ferromagnetism/antiferromagnetism at the interfaces is responsible of a variety of complex magnetic properties, highlighting the use of heterostructures as an effective strategy to tune and control magnetic behaviour. We demonstrate the possibility to tune the magnetic properties of a ferromagnetic La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 (LSMO) layer, embedded in two homometallic antiferromagnetic layers (SrMnO3, SMO), by properly varying the role of interfaces using two different LSMO thickness values. Spin reorientation of the LSMO magnetic easy axis away from its room temperature orientation was observed below 125 K, due to the predominance of exchange interaction at the interface over the substrate-induced magnetic anisotropy, in agreement with established models for homometallic manganite heterostructures. The competition of super-exchange and exchange interactions at the interface results in a highly frustrated interfacial spin glass state, resulting in 20-fold enhancement of the coercive field and exchange bias. The modification of the magnetocrystalline anisotropy driven by the interfacial effects leads to a change in the anisotropic magneto-resistance response of the system, which dominates the low temperature behaviour.

</details>


### [128] [MoireKP: An Automated Workflow for Symmetry-Constrained Moiré Continuum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37760)
*Yan Zhang,Hanqi Pi,Wangqian Miao,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Xi Dai,B. Andrei Bernevig,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: MoireKP 是一个自动化工作流，用于从局域轨道从头算哈密顿量出发，构建紧致的连续模型，以研究莫尔材料；该工作流通过构建谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量、选取低能基组并投影晶体对称性操作，来确定对称性允许的哈密顿项及其系数。在三种莫尔体系中验证，所有模型在高对称 k 路径目标窗口内的能带能量与 TAPW 误差均小于 3.0 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理哈密顿量包含了研究莫尔材料所需的电子信息，但其规模庞大，导致后续计算代价高昂。因此需要一种能将大规模从头算哈密顿量压缩为紧凑连续模型的高效自动化方法。

Method: 提出 MoireKP 自动化工作流：构建谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量；选取低能基组；将晶体对称性操作投影到该基组中；由这些表示确定对称性允许的哈密顿项；通过将低能有效哈密顿量投影到相应算符基上得到各项系数。模型维度、局域参考态和谐波截断可自动选择或由用户指定，且基于哈密顿量的构造在所选低能子空间内保留了波函数信息。

Result: 将该工作流应用于 AA 堆叠扭转双层 MoTe2、AB 堆叠扭转双层 ZrS2 和 A–AB 扭转三层 MoTe2，覆盖 K、M 和 Γ 谷。所有模型在高对称 k 路径的目标窗口内均能以小于 3.0 meV 的误差复现 TAPW 能带能量。

Conclusion: MoireKP 能够在保持波函数信息的同时，高效地将大型从头算哈密顿量转化为紧凑准确的连续模型，并在多种材料体系和不同谷上得到验证，为莫尔材料的后续计算提供了兼具精度与效率的工具。

Abstract: First-principles Hamiltonians contain the electronic information needed to study moiré materials, but their size makes subsequent calculations costly. We present MoireKP, an automated workflow for constructing compact continuum models from localized-orbital ab initio Hamiltonians. The workflow constructs a valley-resolved truncated atomic-plane-wave (TAPW) Hamiltonian, selects a low-energy basis, and projects the crystal-symmetry operations into this basis. These representations determine the symmetry-allowed Hamiltonian terms, whose coefficients are obtained by projecting the low-energy effective Hamiltonian onto the corresponding operator basis. The model dimension, localized reference states, and harmonic cutoff can be selected automatically or specified by the user. This Hamiltonian-based construction retains wave-function information within the selected low-energy subspace. We apply the workflow to AA-stacked twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, AB-stacked twisted bilayer ZrS$_2$, and A--AB twisted trilayer MoTe$_2$, covering K, M, and $Γ$ valleys. All models reproduce TAPW band energies within 3.0 meV in the target windows along high-symmetry $k$ paths.

</details>


### [129] [Planar energetics do not determine twin persistence in nanocrystalline aluminium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37925)
*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Haoyu Wan,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 平面层错能虽能描述面心立方金属的孪生倾向并常被用作势函数验证目标，但并不能决定纳米晶铝中形变孪晶是否存续；决定孪晶存续的是畸变不全位错芯内的力与局部牵引方向、不全位错抵达顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示平面层错能（planar energetics）这一传统孪生判据的局限性，解释为何在匹配相同零载荷滑移曲线和相近层错能面的机器学习势下，纳米晶铝的孪晶存续行为存在显著差异。

Method: 采用两种机器学习原子间势，二者复现相同的零载荷滑移曲线与相近的分层能面；以共同的分离型层错前驱体为起点，在相同加载条件下进行模拟，比较保留孪晶覆盖率，并分析畸变不全位错芯内沿尾随平移方向的作用力及其对去除外层层错的影响。

Result: 两种势对同一分离型层错前驱体预测的保留孪晶覆盖率分别为64%和14%；差异源于畸变不全位错芯内沿尾随平移方向的作用力（该力会去除外层层错）。平面层错能虽设定了裕度——外层层错使中面剪切的增量势垒减半以上，但孪晶完成与层错去除之间仅差10–21 mJ m⁻²。孪晶是否存续最终取决于局部牵引相对于全滑移平分线的取向，以及来自晶界网络的不全滑移前沿的到达顺序。

Conclusion: 平面层错能不足以决定纳米晶铝中形变孪晶的存续；应关注畸变核心内的作用力、局部牵引方向与不全位错到达顺序等动力学因素。

Abstract: Planar energetics are the standard descriptors of twinnability in face-centred cubic metals and the usual validation targets for interatomic potentials. Here we show that they do not determine whether deformation twins persist in nanocrystalline aluminium. Two machine-learned potentials reproducing the same zero-load slip curves and closely spaced layered energy surfaces predict, from a common separated-fault precursor under identical loading, retained twin coverage of 64\% and 14\%. The divergence traces to forces within distorted partial cores acting along the trailing translation that would remove an outer fault. Planar energetics do set the margin: an outer fault more than halves the incremental barrier for middle-plane shear, yet twin completion and fault removal remain separated by only 10--21 mJ m\textsuperscript{-2}. Whether an assembled twin persists is decided instead by the orientation of local traction relative to the full-slip bisector and by the arrival order of partial-slip fronts from the grain-boundary network.

</details>


### [130] [Analytical framework for interpreting thermoreflectance measurements of nanoline gratings under finite spot illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37957)
*Abhishek Pathak,Seungwon Park,Shubham Deshmukh,Alan J. H. McGaughey,Jonathan A. Malen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于频域热反射（FDTR）的技术，用于测量表面带有金属纳米线光栅图案的材料叠层的有效热物性。作者推导了考虑高斯激光光斑与光栅尺寸的解析热扩散模型，并通过Au纳米线光栅实验验证，为后续以加热器尺寸测量有效热导率、探测声子平均自由程相关贡献奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FDTR等热反射技术通常针对连续薄膜，难以有效表征表面图案化（如金属纳米线光栅）样品的有效热特性。同时，为了通过改变加热器尺寸来探测非扩散输运区域中声子平均自由程（MFP）对热导率的贡献，需要一个能准确计入激光光斑尺寸和光栅几何效应的可靠模型作为基准。

Method: 作者建立了一种解析热扩散模型，用于预测FDTR相位响应，模型中同时考虑了泵浦光和探测光具有高斯强度分布的有限光斑半径，以及纳米线光栅的几何尺寸。随后将该模型与实验测量对比验证：样品为在非晶SiO₂和c面蓝宝石（c-sapphire）衬底上制备的Au纳米线光栅，线宽从100 nm到1000 nm不等。由于这两种衬底的声子平均自由程较短，整个线宽范围内热输运均处于扩散区域。

Result: 模型预测与实验测量在不同激光光斑半径和纳米线宽度下均表现出优异的一致性。提取得到的c-sapphire体热导率，以及Au-Cr/a-SiO₂与Au-Cr/c-sapphire界面处的界面热导值，均与此前作者的测量结果和文献报道相当。

Conclusion: 该经过验证的模型将作为一个稳健的基准，用于将该方法推广到测量有效热导率随加热器尺寸的变化。该方法尤其适用于那些存在非扩散热输运区域、从而能够探测声子平均自由程对热导率贡献的材料。

Abstract: A frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR)-based technique is presented to measure the effective thermal properties of a material stack whose surface is patterned with metallic nanoline gratings that are heated and probed by lasers. An analytical heat diffusion model is derived for predicting the FDTR phase response that incorporates a finite laser spot radius with a Gaussian intensity profile for the pump and probe lasers, as well as the dimensions of the nanoline grating. The model is validated by comparing with measurements of Au nanoline gratings, with widths ranging from 100 to 1000 nm, fabricated on a-SiO$_2$ and $c$-sapphire substrates. Due to the short phonon mean free paths (MFPs) in these substrates, thermal transport remains in the diffusive regime for the whole range of nanoline widths. The model and the measurement show excellent agreement across different laser spot radii and nanoline widths, with a bulk thermal conductivity value of $c$-sapphire and the thermal boundary conductances at the Au-Cr/a-SiO$_2$ and Au-Cr/$c$-sapphire interfaces comparable to our prior measurements and the literature. The validated model will serve as a robust baseline for extending this method to measure effective thermal conductivity as a function of the heater size. This approach is particularly relevant for materials where non-diffusive thermal transport regimes enable probing phonon MFP dependent contributions to the thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [131] [Electronic-morphological decoupling in mixed-dimensional halide perovskite heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37964)
*Mahmoud Samadpour,Alireza Fathi Beiraghvandi,Kazem Meraji,Tristan Sachsenweger Ballantyne,Fuxiang Ji,Mahdi Mohammadi,Nasim Kabir,Wolfgang Tress*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，在混合维度钙钛矿异质结构中，表面形貌的显著演变（粗糙度与高度不对称性增加）并不必然导致电子异质性的加剧；低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层虽使表面更不规则，却能增强光致发光、抑制非辐射复合并改善准费米能级劈裂的空间均匀性，且在加速老化下仍保持光电均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为表面越光滑则薄膜光电性质越均匀，本文旨在挑战这一关联，探究低维覆盖层引起的表面形态演变是否真的加剧3D钙钛矿的电子异质性，从而为耐久性钙钛矿光伏器件提供新的设计原则。

Method: 以空间位阻型5-叔丁基菲啶鎓阳离子衍生的低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层修饰3D钙钛矿，形成混合维度异质结构；将统计原子力显微镜（AFM）表面形貌分析与共聚焦、空间分辨光致发光（PL）光谱相结合，关联表面形貌与微尺度光电性质，并在加速老化条件下对比原始3D薄膜与覆盖异质结构的光发射及准费米能级劈裂（QFLS）的空间波动。

Result: 覆盖异质结构虽表面粗糙度和高度不对称性增加，但未增加电子不均匀性，反而表现出增强的PL、降低的非辐射复合以及更均匀的辐射QFLS空间分布；加速老化后，原始3D薄膜出现显著的发射与QFLS空间波动，而覆盖异质结构基本保持光电均匀性，说明低维覆盖层抑制了老化诱导的光电无序。

Conclusion: 电子均匀性可以在表面形貌不规则的情况下得以维持，形貌与电子均匀性存在解耦关系；这一发现为开发耐久、高性能的钙钛矿光伏器件提供了设计原理。

Abstract: Surface morphology is widely considered an indicator of thin-film quality, with smoother films generally expected to exhibit more uniform optoelectronic properties. Here, we demonstrate that substantial surface evolution does not necessarily lead to increased electronic heterogeneity in mixed-dimensional perovskite heterostructures. We investigate how low-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper capping layers derived from the sterically hindered 5-tert-butyl-phenanthridinium cation affect the spatial distributions of photoluminescence (PL) characteristics and quasi-Fermi level splitting (QFLS) in 3D perovskites. By combining statistical atomic force microscopy (AFM) analysis with confocal, spatially resolved PL spectroscopy, we correlate surface topography with microscale optoelectronic properties.
  Although capping increases surface roughness and height asymmetry, it does not increase electronic inhomogeneity. Instead, the heterostructures exhibit enhanced PL, reduced non-radiative recombination, and improved spatial uniformity of radiative QFLS compared with pristine 3D films. Under accelerated aging, this contrast becomes more pronounced: pristine films develop substantial spatial fluctuations in emission and QFLS, whereas capped heterostructures largely preserve their optoelectronic homogeneity. These findings indicate that the low-dimensional capping layer suppresses aging-induced optoelectronic disorder, demonstrating that electronic uniformity can be maintained despite morphological irregularity. This decoupling provides a design principle for developing durable, high-performance perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [132] [Thermal laser evaporation of superconducting Nb films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38070)
*Yifei Yan,David S. Catherall,Finley B. Donachie,Dorsa Najafipanah,Austin J. Minnich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用热激光蒸发（TLE）技术在c面蓝宝石衬底上沉积铌（Nb）超导薄膜，薄膜的电学和超导性能达到或超过其他无衬底加热沉积技术的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热激光蒸发（TLE）是一种新兴的物理气相沉积（PVD）技术，利用连续波激光可蒸发最难熔的元素。评估TLE沉积的超导难熔元素薄膜质量，以验证该技术对难熔元素及化合物的适用性与通用性。

Method: 使用自制的TLE系统（配备1070 nm连续波光纤激光器），以533 W激光功率聚焦至约500 μm直径的铌靶斑点上，在c面蓝宝石上沉积Nb薄膜，沉积速率为0.19 Å/s，并对薄膜的厚度、粗糙度、电阻率、残余电阻率比（RRR）及超导转变温度进行表征。

Result: 所沉积薄膜厚度为22.47±0.05 nm，均方根粗糙度为0.23±0.02 nm，室温电阻率为21.60±0.05 μΩ·cm，RRR为5.71；超导转变温度Tc为9.24 K，仅比块体值低10 mK，转变宽度为29 mK。

Conclusion: 在无衬底加热的条件下，本工作中TLE沉积的Nb薄膜电学和超导性能达到或超过其他沉积技术，进一步支持了TLE作为难熔元素及化合物通用PVD方法的潜力。

Abstract: Thermal laser evaporation (TLE) is an emerging physical vapor deposition (PVD) technique that uses continuous-wave lasers to evaporate even the most refractory elements. The deposition and characterization of superconducting refractory elements are of interest to assess the quality of films deposited by TLE. Here, we report the deposition of niobium (Nb) on c-plane sapphire using a home-built TLE system equipped with a 1070 nm continuous-wave fiber laser. A deposition rate of 0.19 Å/s was achieved with 533 W of laser power focused to a diameter of approximately 500 $μ$m on a Nb target. A film with a thickness of $22.47 \pm 0.05$ nm and a root-mean-square (RMS) roughness of $0.23 \pm 0.02$ nm exhibited a room-temperature resistivity of $21.60 \pm 0.05$ $μΩ\mathrm{cm}$ and a residual-resistivity ratio (RRR) of 5.71. The superconducting transition temperature, $T_\mathrm{c}$, of the film was measured as 9.24 K, only 10 mK below the bulk value, with a transition width of 29 mK. Among films deposited without substrate heating, the electrical and superconducting properties of the present films meet or exceed those deposited by other techniques. This work contributes to the emerging consensus regarding TLE as a versatile PVD method for refractory elements and compounds.

</details>


### [133] [Odd Elasticity in Strain-Gradient Continua: Anisotropy Classification and Non-Hermitian Elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40581)
*Payam Poorsolhjouy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将奇弹性(odd elasticity)框架扩展到三维应变梯度理论，系统推导了各点群对称性下的四、五、六阶奇弹性刚度张量，并研究了其弹性动力学行为，发现非厄米弹性动力学需要各向异性，数值展示了横观各向同性(hemitropic)奇弹性连续体中的奇异点与主动能量交换现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇弹性将连续介质力学推广到含内部能量源的主动非保守系统（如带非互易反馈的机器人主动超材料），建模需放松 Maxwell-Betti 互易性，导致刚度张量失去主对称性。此类异质系统具有固有长度尺度，因此需引入应变梯度理论加以刻画，并系统地确定各对称性下允许的非互易奇数模量。

Method: 将奇弹性框架推广到三维应变梯度奇弹性；沿手性(chiral)与非手性(achiral)分支应用完整的 3D 点群对称层级，穷举推导四、五、六阶奇数弹性刚度张量；利用动力学虚功原理建立平衡方程、高阶边界条件与广义声学张量进行弹性动力学分析；通过数值分析横观各向同性(hemitropic)奇弹性连续体验证理论。

Result: 非互易模量在六阶刚度中于完全各向同性下、五阶刚度中于 hemitropy 下、四阶刚度中于立方对称下依然存活。弹性动力学分析表明，在各向同性与 hemitropic 极限下，尽管存在奇数刚度模量，声学张量仍无条件为厄米(Hermitian)；非厄米弹性动力学需要各向异性。横观各向同性 hemitropic 奇弹性连续体的数值分析展示了非厄米现象，包括色散谱中的奇异点(exceptional points)以及弹性波分量的同时放大与衰减所体现的主动能量交换。

Conclusion: 该工作为各向异性应变梯度主动微结构的多尺度均匀化框架，以及非互易应变梯度超材料的设计，提供了必要的理论基础。

Abstract: Odd elasticity extends continuum mechanics to active nonconservative systems containing internal energy sources, such as robotic active metamaterials with nonreciprocal feedback. Modeling these systems as effective continua requires relaxing Maxwell-Betti reciprocity, which results in stiffness tensors lacking major symmetry. In this contribution, we extend this framework to 3D strain-gradient odd elasticity to incorporate the length scales inherent in these heterogeneous systems. By applying the full hierarchy of 3D point-group symmetries along chiral and achiral branches, we derive the exhaustive set of fourth-, fifth-, and sixth-order odd elastic stiffness tensors. Further, utilizing the dynamic principle of virtual work, we formulate the equilibrium equations, higher-order boundary conditions, and the generalized acoustic tensor for elastodynamic analysis. The results demonstrate that nonreciprocal moduli survive even under full isotropy in the sixth-order stiffness, under hemitropy in the fifth-order stiffness, and under cubic symmetry in fourth-order stiffness. In contrast, the elastodynamic analysis reveals that under isotropic and hemitropic limits, despite the underlying odd stiffness moduli, the acoustic tensor remains unconditionally Hermitian. Non-Hermitian elastodynamics is shown to require anisotropy. By numerically analyzing a transversely hemitropic odd elastic continuum, we demonstrate that this symmetry reduction results in non-Hermitian elastodynamic phenomena including exceptional points in the dispersion spectra and active energy exchange through simultaneous amplification and attenuation of the elastic wave components. These results provide the necessary theoretical foundation for future multiscale homogenization frameworks for anisotropic strain-gradient active microstructures, and for the design of nonreciprocal strain-gradient metamaterials.

</details>


### [134] [Emergent higher-order optical nonlinearity in self-assembled polar supertextures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38138)
*Daniel A. Bustamante Lopez,Deepankar Sri Gyan,Xiaojiang Li,Vladimir A. Stoica,Sujit Das,Peter Meisenheimer,Akash Surampalli,Mohit Tanwani,Xianghan Xu,Aiden Ross,Long-Qing Chen,Sang-Wook Cheong,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Venkatraman Gopalan,Wanzheng Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文报道在自组装极性斯格明子中发现了由中红外激发诱导的巨大超快二次谐波产生（SHG），其瞬态强度呈现源自高阶非线性效应的独特功率依赖，并指出该效应普遍存在于极性超晶格的多种畴结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有超越二阶的强高阶光学非线性的材料极为稀缺，而巨大光学非线性对先进材料调控及下一代光子学（量子信息处理、高速光通信）至关重要，因此需寻找具备本征强高阶非线性响应的新材料体系。

Method: 以中红外光激发自组装极性斯格明子，测量瞬态SHG强度及其功率依赖；结合相场模拟，表征平衡态各极性织构内部强且周期性的局域介电常数变化，并验证中红外激发下极性斯格明子的快速极化振荡；进一步分析局域介电常数调制满足epsilon-near-zero条件，从而无需光子工程即可增强非线性光学响应。

Result: 在极性斯格明子中观测到巨大且超快的SHG，瞬态强度呈现源于高阶非线性效应的独特功率依赖；高阶非线性在极性超晶格多种畴结构中普遍出现；相场模拟证实极性织构内部存在强周期局域介电常数变化和快速极化振荡，该调制可满足epsilon-near-zero条件，从而解锁显著增强的非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 自组装极性拓扑结构具备强的本征光学非线性，无需光子工程即可实现巨大的非线性光学响应，有望为量子信息处理与高速光通信建立新的平台。

Abstract: Giant optical nonlinearities enable advanced materials control and bridge exotic light-matter phenomena to next-generation photonics. However, materials exhibiting strong higher-order optical nonlinearity beyond the second order are typically rare. Here, we report the discovery of a giant and ultrafast second-harmonic generation (SHG) in self-assembled polar skyrmions under mid-infrared excitations. The transient SHG intensity exhibits a distinctive power dependence originating from higher-order nonlinearity. More generally, we show that large higher-order nonlinearity emerges across diverse domain structures in polar superlattices. Phase-field simulations demonstrate a strong and periodic local permittivity variation within each polar texture at equilibrium, and further confirm the fast polarization oscillation in polar skyrmions with mid-infrared excitations. This intense local permittivity modulation can satisfy epsilon-near-zero conditions, unlocking dramatically enhanced nonlinear optical responses without photonic engineering. Consequently, the self-assembled polar topologies with strong inherent optical nonlinearity could establish a platform for quantum information processing and high-speed optical communications.

</details>


### [135] [Ordered patches cap the stability of disordered frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38501)
*Joohan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 无序可以增强框架抵抗压力失效的稳定性，但其内部有序区域会削弱这一增益。作者证明在静水压力下的刚性连接框架中，若存在任意大的正密度晶体块并带有缩放晶体预应力，一旦这些块受压超过晶体的局域失稳阈值，整体无法保持线性稳定；而均匀失去正定性但所有Bloch模式（含声学极限）保持稳定则不会发生局域化。无序对晶体对称破缺型失效无保护作用，对立方晶体的保护至多限于体积坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清结构无序对压力诱导失效的稳定化能力及其根本限制，明确无序能在多大程度上保护晶体框架，以及有序区域如何制约这种增益。

Method: 采用刚性连接框架在静水压力下的力学模型，结合晶体预应力缩放、Bloch模式与声学极限的稳定性分析，将声学判据推广到预应力情形，并对所有点群进行推导；用二维Truchet框架植入有序域验证紧致性，再以876个细杆网架进行数值统计。

Result: 证明含正密度任意大晶体块的系综在超过晶体局域失稳阈值后无法线性稳定；均匀正定性丧失而Bloch模式稳定的情形不会局域化。稳定性窗口要求保持晶体对称性的应变。Truchet框架植入有序域后阈值距理论上界在1%以内，短程序列使失效局域化于有序团簇；876个细杆网架中798个首先发生局域失稳，其余78个的中位稳定性窗口为7.6%。

Conclusion: 无序带来的稳定性增益受其内部局部有序的局域失稳所封顶：除非有序块与应力隔离，无序无法保护晶体抵抗对称破缺型失效，对立方晶体的保护至多止于体积坍塌。

Abstract: Structural disorder can stabilize frameworks against pressure-induced failure, yet the ordered regions it contains limit this gain. We show that, in rigid-jointed frames under hydrostatic pressure, an ensemble containing arbitrarily large patches of a crystal at positive density, whose centres carry a scaled crystal prestress, cannot stay linearly stable once those patches are stressed beyond the crystal's threshold for localizable instabilities. A homogeneous loss of positive definiteness while all Bloch modes, including the acoustic limit, remain stable cannot localize, leaving a window for stabilization. Extending an acoustic criterion to prestress, we show for all point groups that such a window requires a strain that keeps the crystal's symmetry. Unless the patches are shielded from stress, disorder cannot protect a crystal against a failure that breaks its symmetry, and protects a cubic crystal at most against volume collapse. In two-dimensional Truchet frames, planted ordered domains bring the threshold within 1% of the bound, and correlations short of long-range order localize failure on ordered clusters. Across 876 slender-strut nets, 798 fail first by a localizable instability; the other 78 have a median window of 7.6%. Stability gained through disorder is capped by the localizable instabilities of the local order it permits.

</details>


### [136] [Crystoria: A Large-Scale Crystal Dataset Linking Thermodynamic and Dynamical Stability for Materials AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38195)
*Ke-Xin Pang,Ze-Feng Gao,Yan Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推出 Crystoria——一个包含 264 万晶体结构的大规模数据集，借助机器学习加速弛豫与声子动力学筛选，首次将动力学稳定性标注扩展到百万级规模，为材料 AI 提供物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模晶体数据库虽已包含数百万结构与热力学性质，但动力学稳定性因其计算成本远高于常规结构/能量计算而缺乏同量级的标注，形成了结构空间急剧扩张与可用物理信息之间的关键缺口。

Method: 系统筛选低能与亚稳无机晶体，标准化其结构标识，并结合机器学习加速的结构弛豫与基于声子的动力学筛选，构建大规模数据集。

Result: Crystoria 包含 2,649,640 个晶体结构，覆盖 89 种元素、230 个空间群中的 219 个，原胞含 1–48 个原子；其中 1,793,353 个（67.68%）被判定为动力学稳定，且在化学与晶体学空间中动力学稳定性呈现显著差异。

Conclusion: Crystoria 将动力学稳定性扩展到数百万晶体规模，为材料人工智能提供了物理可靠的基础，并能支撑对动力学晶体景观的系统性探索。

Abstract: Large-scale crystal databases have transformed materials discovery by making millions of structures and thermodynamic properties accessible to materials informatics. Yet dynamical stability remains sparsely characterized at a comparable scale because its evaluation is substantially more computationally demanding than conventional structure and energy calculations. This mismatch creates a critical gap between the rapidly expanding crystal-structure space and the physical information available for materials research and machine learning. Here we introduce Crystoria, a large-scale crystal dataset designed to bring dynamical stability to the scale of modern materials data. We construct Crystoria by systematically curating low-energy and metastable inorganic crystals, standardizing their structural identities, and combining machine-learning-accelerated structural relaxation with phonon-based dynamical screening. The resulting dataset contains 2,649,640 crystal structures, spanning 89 elements, 219 of the 230 space groups, and primitive cells containing 1-48 atoms. Among them, 1,793,353 structures (67.68%) are classified as dynamically stable, revealing substantial variation in dynamical stability across chemical and crystallographic space. By bringing dynamical stability to the scale of millions of crystal structures, Crystoria provides a physically grounded foundation for materials artificial intelligence (AI) and enables systematic exploration of the dynamical crystal landscape.

</details>


### [137] [Finite-Temperature Magnetism and Longitudinal Spin Transport in Compensated Half-Metal Ferrimagnets with Near-Zero Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38302)
*Jiahao Shentu,Jie Li,Jianting Dong,Meng Zhu,Chen Wang,Qing Zhang,Yizhuo Song,Wenqi Sun,Jia Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al的电子结构、有限温度磁性及自旋输运特性，证明其为0 K下100%自旋极化且磁矩完全补偿的半金属FCF，并在Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结中预测了室温下仍高达232%的巨隧穿磁电阻效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全补偿亚铁磁体（FCF）在0 K时磁矩近乎消失、电子结构完全自旋极化、自旋动力学超快，可最大限度地减少杂散磁场并降低开关电流，是实现高速、低功耗自旋电子器件的理想平台。然而其有限温度磁性和在器件（尤其磁隧道结）中的自旋输运性能尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟，系统研究Mn3Al的电子结构和有限温度磁性；进一步在Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结结构中预测其自旋相关输运性质与隧穿磁电阻效应。

Result: Mn3Al为半金属全补偿亚铁磁体，0 K下具有100%自旋极化和完全补偿的磁化强度；在300 K时净磁化强度低于11.6 emu/cc，证实其室温下近补偿亚铁磁序的稳健性；Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结在0 K呈现巨隧穿磁电阻，300 K时仍高达232%。

Conclusion: Mn3Al及其相关的半金属全补偿亚铁磁体是面向磁电阻型自旋电子器件应用的优秀候选材料，为实现高速、低功耗自旋电子器件提供了可行路径。
