Abstract: Fully compensated ferrimagnets, characterized by nearly vanishing magnetic moments at zero temperature, perfect spin-polarized electronic structures, and ultrafast spin dynamics, represent a highly promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices. By minimizing stray magnetic fields and reducing switching currents, FCFs enable a viable path toward high-speed, low-power spintronic devices. In this work, we present a systematic investigation of the electronic structure, finite-temperature magnetism, and spin-dependent transport properties of the Heusler-type fully compensated half-metallic ferrimagnet Mn3Al in magnetic tunnel junctions. We demonstrate that Mn3Al is a half-metallic FCF with 100% spin polarization and fully compensated magnetization at 0 K. Combining density functional theory with Monte Carlo simulations, we characterize its finite-temperature magnetic properties and show that Mn3Al exhibits a net magnetization below 11.6 emu/cc at 300 K, confirming the robustness of its nearly compensated ferrimagnetic order at room temperature. Furthermore, we predict that the Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ yields a giant tunneling magnetoresistance effect at 0 K, which remains as high as 232% at 300 K. Our results establish Mn3Al and related half-metallic FCFs as excellent candidates for magnetoresistance-based spintronic applications.

</details>


### [138] [First-Principles Identification of Higher-Order Configurational Energy Terms and Their Magnetic-State Dependence in Lithium-Rich Oxyfluorides: From QUBO to HUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38321)
*Joonho Bae*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用自旋极化DFT证明富锂氟氧化物中存在QUBO无法表示的三阶非加和构型能量贡献，并通过扩展至HUBO显著降低两种无序岩盐正极材料的拟合误差，同时揭示磁态对能量映射的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QUBO模型只能表示成对（二阶）相互作用，无法刻画锂富集氟氧化物中显著的三阶非加和构型能量贡献；同时需要验证高阶HUBO扩展能否提升对无序岩盐正极材料构型能量的建模精度。

Method: 采用自旋极化DFT，针对LMOF（Li₂MnO₂F）和LMTiOF（Li₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅O₂F）两种无序岩盐正极材料构建保组分的DFT能量映射；将模型从QUBO扩展至HUBO，用固定几何能量映射对比拟合误差，并通过直接DFT能量差与不同磁初始化对比验证三阶非加和性及其对电子态的依赖。

Result: 立方HUBO能够捕捉QUBO缺失的非加和贡献，并在两种材料中大幅降低拟合误差；直接DFT能量差证实三阶非加和性；LMTiOF中的贡献对收敛电子态（磁态）有强依赖。

Conclusion: 在构型能量建模中应纳入高阶项，并在构建DFT能量映射时必须考虑磁态依赖性；所提取的高阶项描述的是保组分交换变量之间的有效耦合，而非唯一确定的微观三原子相互作用。

Abstract: In this work, we use spin-polarized density functional theory (DFT) to identify substantial third-order, nonadditive configurational energy contributions in lithium-rich oxyfluorides that quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models cannot represent. We test the extension from QUBO to higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) in two disordered-rocksalt cathode materials, Li$_2$MnO$_2$F (LMOF) and Li$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$O$_2$F (LMTiOF), using composition-preserving DFT energy maps. Using fixed-geometry energy maps, we show that cubic HUBO captures nonadditive contributions absent from QUBO models and substantially reduces fitting errors in both materials. Direct DFT energy differences confirm third-order nonadditivity, while comparisons of magnetic initializations reveal a strong dependence of the LMTiOF contribution on the converged electronic state. These results support the inclusion of higher-order terms when modeling configurational energetics and highlight the need to account for magnetic-state dependence in constructing DFT energy maps. The extracted terms describe effective couplings among composition-preserving exchange variables, rather than uniquely identified microscopic three-atom interactions.

</details>


### [139] [Strain-Induced BCC-to-FCC Transformation in Tungsten Uncovered by Molecular Dynamics Simulations with Machine Learning Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38477)
*Qiu-Hong Hu,YueYue Wang,Xinyi Leng,Hezhu Shao,Luru Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过机器学习势分子动力学模拟，证明钨在应变诱导下确实可发生BCC到FCC的多晶型转变，并阐明了温度、位错等条件的影响及转变遵循的取向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨通常被认为不发生多晶型转变，但该观察可能源于传统实验中热力学与力学条件难以达到，而非其本质上没有相变可能。作者旨在探究钨是否存在本征的BCC到FCC相变可能性，以期为制备高延展性双相钨提供依据。

Method: 采用经过严格验证的机器学习势（MLP）进行分子动力学（MD）模拟，研究钨在应变诱导下的BCC到FCC转变，并分析温度、位错存在与否对相变动力学的影响，同时进行结构分析确证转变的取向关系。

Result: 模拟表明钨中确实发生应变诱导的BCC到FCC转变。低温且存在位错时，FCC相含量超过20 at.%；在完美晶体中，中等温度有利于相变动力学。结构分析证实转变遵循Bain和西山-瓦塞尔曼（Nishiyama-Wassermann）取向关系。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了钨中应变诱导多晶型转变的可行性，为实验制备具有增强延展性的双相钨提供了关键指导，有助于解决钨材料在高温结构应用中的长期挑战。

Abstract: Polymorphic transformations occur in a minority of metals. Tungsten is not one of them. This observation does not rule out its intrinsic phase transition possibility, but may be due to inaccessible thermodynamic and mechanical conditions in conventional experiments. Herein, we investigate the body centered cubic (BCC) to face centered cubic (FCC) transformation in tungsten via a rigorously validated machine learning potential (MLP) molecular dynamics (MD) approach. Our simulations demonstrate that strain induced BCC to FCC transformation indeed occurs in tungsten. The FCC phase exceeds 20 at.% at low temperature and in the presence of dislocations. In the perfect crystal moderate temperatures favor phase transformation kinetics. Structural analysis confirms the transformation following the Bain and Nishiyama Wassermann orientation relationship. These results establish the viability of strain induced polymorphic transformation in tungsten and provide a critical guideline for the experimental production of duplex tungsten with enhanced ductility addressing a longstanding challenge in tungsten material engineering for high-temperature structural applications.

</details>


### [140] [Two-Dimensional Magnetic Materials from Ferromagnetism to Altermagnetism: Fabrication, Symmetry, Evidence, and Device Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38491)
*Guojian Zhao,Ziang Meng,Li Liu,Zhiyuan Duan,Jingyu Li,Sixu Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan,Shiheng Bu,Chen Liang,Xiaoning Wang,Peixin Qin,Zhiqi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了原子级薄磁体（二维磁性）的分类框架，以净磁化强度和动量空间自旋劈裂/自旋极化图样为主坐标，辅以子晶格结构及相反自旋子晶格的对称关系，将体系划分为铁磁、亚铁磁、常规反铁磁与交变磁（altermagnet）等类别；并引入 M1–M4 报告等级以区分层级分辨的原子级薄形态、非层级分辨薄片/薄膜、块体基准及理论候选/设计路线，同时独立记录证据类别与有序温度来源。当前交变磁的实验支持主要来自 M2 薄膜与 M3 块体，完整验证的 M1 单层交变磁相仍是待实现目标。文章还对器件就绪度进行定性比较，强调必须对同一材料形态在匹配条件下开展互补测量，以可靠判定相归属并评估器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁体的性质受成分、厚度、堆叠、应变、缺陷、衬底、铁性序及界面等多重因素共同支配，导致不同体系难以用统一框架比较和归类。现有研究混杂着理论预测、薄膜/块体验证与真正原子级薄实验，缺乏一致的证据分级标准，使得相归属（尤其是新提出的交变磁相）常存争议，器件就绪度评估也难以系统化。作者旨在建立一套以对称性和自旋量子数结构为核心的统一分类坐标，并配套 M1–M4 报告分级与证据类别/有序温度来源独立记录，从而厘清实验证据强度、明确开放目标、并为可靠相判定与器件评估提供方法论。

Method: 本文采用结构化综述方法：① 以净磁化强度和动量空间自旋劈裂（或自旋极化图样）为两个主坐标，辅以子晶格结构及相反自旋子晶格之间的对称关系，对铁磁、亚铁磁、常规反铁磁、交变磁等相进行归类，并阐明交变磁在维度特定对称约束下的非相对论交替自旋劈裂机制；② 提出 M1（层级分辨的原子级薄形态）、M2（非层级分辨的薄片/薄膜）、M3（块体基准）、M4（理论候选或设计路线）四级分类，将“证据类别”（理论/实验验证程度）与“有序温度来源”独立于相分类另行列报；③ 对器件就绪度从工作温度、读出、开关、畴控制、稳定性、可扩展性、界面敏感性及集成难度等维度做定性比较；④ 提出验证准则，要求对同一材料形态在匹配条件下（理想情况下同一样品或原位）开展互补测量。

Result: 通过该框架可将二维磁性体系一致归类，并明确区分铁磁（通常具净磁矩）、亚铁磁（可实现完全补偿）、常规反铁磁（可保留自旋简并带）与交变磁（在维度特定对称约束下允许非相对论交替自旋劈裂）等情形。对交变磁，现有实验支持主要来自 M2 薄膜与 M3 块体基准，完整验证的单层 M1 交变磁相仍是未实现的实验目标，故大多数已提出的单层/少层体系落入 M4；若干争议案例由此暴露出完成相判定所需的关键测量。器件就绪度在八个维度上的定性比较表明，不同磁相在温度、开关与集成等方面优劣各异，且任何可靠结论都依赖于同一材料形态在匹配条件下的互补实验证据。

Conclusion: 二维磁性可通过“净磁化强度 + 动量空间自旋劈裂/极化图样”主坐标及子晶格对称关系加以系统分类；M1–M4 分级与证据类别/有序温度来源的独立记录为不同文献间证据强度的比较提供了标准。当前原子级薄（M1）交变磁的实验验证仍是空白，亟需针对特定单层/少层体系开展层级分辨的互补测量。可靠的相归属与器件就绪度评估必须基于同一材料形态、匹配条件下的同步验证，最好在同一样品或原位进行，而非依赖不同形态间零散证据的间接外推。

Abstract: Atomically thin magnets are governed by composition, thickness, stacking, strain, defects, substrates, ferroic order, and interfaces. This Review organizes two-dimensional (2D) magnetism using net magnetization and momentum-space spin splitting or spin-polarization patterns as primary coordinates, supplemented by sublattice structure and the symmetry relations between opposite-spin sublattices. Ferromagnets generally carry a net moment; ferrimagnets can be fully compensated. Conventional antiferromagnets may retain spin-degenerate bands, while altermagnets permit nonrelativistic alternating spin splitting under dimension-specific symmetry constraints. M1-M4 reporting classes introduced here distinguish layer-resolved atomically thin forms, non-layer-resolved thin flakes or films, bulk benchmarks, and theoretical candidates or design routes; the evidence category and ordering-temperature provenance are reported independently. Current experimental support for altermagnetism comes mainly from M2 thin-film and M3 bulk benchmarks, leaving a fully verified atomically thin M1 altermagnetic phase as an open experimental goal. Most proposed monolayer and few-layer systems therefore fall within M4, and contested cases clarify the measurements needed for phase assignment. Device readiness is compared qualitatively using operating temperature, readout, switching, domain control, stability, scalability, interface sensitivity, and integration difficulty. Reliable phase assignment and device assessment require complementary measurements from the same material form under matched conditions, ideally on the same specimen or in situ.

</details>


### [141] [Reciprocal transverse Seebeck and Peltier effects in MoSi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38506)
*Aina Sakamoto,Shoya Ohsumi,Shogo Yoshida,Ryuji Okazaki,Yoshiki J. Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在均质非磁性单晶MoSi2中同时实验验证了横向塞贝克效应及其逆过程——横向帕尔贴效应，确立了无需人工界面的互易横向热电转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向热电转换是传统纵向热电转换的潜在替代方案，但横向帕尔贴效应作为横向塞贝克效应的逆过程研究很少，且在同一均质材料中直接对比两种效应的研究十分有限。

Method: 选用MoSi2单晶这一轴依赖导电极性材料（其塞贝克系数符号随晶轴方向变化），通过多端电压测量确定感应内部电场方向、建立横向塞贝克响应；并在施加交流电流下利用锁相热成像直接可视化与横向帕尔贴效应一致的横向温度调制。

Result: 多端电压测量确定了感应内部电场方向，确立了横向塞贝克响应；锁相热成像直接观测到与横向帕尔贴效应相符的横向温度调制。

Conclusion: 在无需人工界面的均质非磁性单晶MoSi2中，成功确立了互易的横向热电转换（横向塞贝克与横向帕尔贴效应）。

Abstract: Transverse thermoelectric conversion, in which charge and heat currents are interconverted in orthogonal directions, has recently attracted increasing attention as an alternative to conventional longitudinal thermoelectric conversion. Although transverse Seebeck effect has been investigated in various materials, its reciprocal process, transverse Peltier effect, remains much less explored, and direct comparison of the two effects in the same homogeneous material is still limited. Here, we experimentally investigate both transverse Seebeck and Peltier effects in a single crystal of MoSi2, an axis-dependent conduction polarity material in which the sign of the Seebeck coefficient varies depending on the crystallographic axis. Multi-terminal voltage measurement determines the direction of the induced internal electric field and establishes the transverse Seebeck response. We also directly visualize the transverse temperature modulation consistent with the corresponding transverse Peltier effect by using lock-in thermography under an applied ac current. These results establish reciprocal transverse thermoelectric conversion in a homogeneous nonmagnetic single crystal without engineered interfaces.

</details>


### [142] [Giant piezomagnetic response driven by spin reorientation in the topological Kagome magnet TbMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38528)
*Hanyang Qian,Wen Sun,Juntao Huo,Guowei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了拓扑Kagome磁体TbMn6Sn6中与磁场诱导自旋重取向相关的异常磁致伸缩响应，在240 K时其磁致伸缩超过120 ppm，微分压磁系数约5000 ppm/T，远超其他拓扑磁材料和商用FeGa合金，并将机制归因于Tb/Mn亚晶格竞争磁各向异性通过交换耦合形成的不同平衡应变构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在磁机械材料中实现对微小磁场变化的大应变响应仍具挑战，尤其是拓扑体系中自旋重构与晶格形变的耦合机制尚不清晰。作者希望探究自旋重取向能否作为获得巨压磁响应的新途径。

Method: 以拓扑Kagome磁体TbMn6Sn6为对象，在自旋重取向温度(310 K)以下施加面内磁场，测量磁致伸缩、磁化、磁输运、热膨胀及Raman声子能量与线宽随磁场和温度的演化，综合分析自旋重取向与晶格动力学的关联。

Result: 面内磁场诱导磁致伸缩在240 K时突变增加并超过120 ppm，产生约5000 ppm/T的巨微分压磁系数，远超其他拓扑磁材料和商用FeGa合金；该峰的磁场位置与磁化及磁输运异常一致，热膨胀在自旋重取向边界发生变化，Raman测量显示声子能量与线宽的对应异常，表明重取向改变了晶格动力学。

Conclusion: 竞争磁各向异性的Tb与Mn亚晶格通过Tb-Mn交换耦合形成不同平衡应变的磁构型，自旋重取向因此成为拓扑磁体中实现巨压磁响应的途径，为近室温磁机械传感与开关提供了基础。

Abstract: Achieving a large strain response to a small magnetic-field variation remains a challenge in magnetomechanical materials, particularly in topological systems where the coupling between spin reconstruction and lattice deformation remains unclear. Here, we report an anomalous magnetostrictive response associated with field-induced spin reorientation in the topological Kagome magnet TbMn6Sn6. Below its spin-reorientation temperature of 310 K, an in-plane magnetic field induces an abrupt increase in magnetostriction, which exceeds 120 ppm at 240 K. This transition produces a giant differential piezomagnetic coefficient of approximately 5000 ppm T-1, far exceeding the values reported for other topological magnetic materials and commercial FeGa alloys, and placing TbMn6Sn6 among the most field-sensitive magnetostrictive materials. The field position of this peak agrees with the anomalies in magnetization and magnetotransport, demonstrating that the large differential response is tied to field-induced spin reorientation. Thermal expansion also changes across the spin-reorientation boundary. Raman measurements reveal corresponding anomalies in phonon energy and linewidth, indicating that the reorientation modifies the lattice dynamics. The combined results support a mechanism in which competing magnetic anisotropies of the Tb and Mn sublattices, coupled through Tb-Mn exchange, create magnetic configurations with different equilibrium strains. This finding establishes spin reorientation as a route to giant piezomagnetic responses in topological magnets and provides a basis for magnetomechanical sensing and switching near room temperature.

</details>


### [143] [Uniaxial strain tuning of functional properties in antiferroelectric PbZrO3 membrane capacitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38627)
*Umair Saeed,Huazhang Zhang,David Pesquera,Fernando Gómez-Ortiz,Jessica Padilla- Pantoja,Jose Santiso,Philippe Ghosez,Gustau Catalan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过在柔性衬底上制备 PbZrO3（PZO）自支撑反铁电薄膜电容器，揭示了面内单轴应变对反铁电-铁电（AFE-FE）态切换的调控机制，并结合原子级模拟阐明了其内在机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PZO 是典型的反铁电体系，在储能和神经形态计算中具有应用前景，但传统薄膜结构中受衬底夹持效应限制，严重制约其功能潜力。虽然自支撑膜技术可解除夹持并实现动态应变工程，但目前缺乏对外部应变如何调控 AFE 与 FE 态切换的系统理解。

Method: 采用金键合方法将 PZO 薄膜转移至柔性衬底上制成自支撑电容器，系统研究面内单轴应变对 AFE-FE 切换及功能特性的影响，并通过原子级模拟（atomistic simulations）加以验证和解释。

Result: 实验证明面内动态应变能够有效调控 AFE-FE 切换及相关功能性质。结合原子模拟揭示，应变依赖的演变量来源于两部分叠加：特定畴取向变体本身固有的应变依赖性（本征贡献），以及应变诱导的不同畴变体相对布居的再分布（外在贡献）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了对 PZO 中单轴应变调控 AFE-FE 切换的预测性理解，为开发自支撑反铁电薄膜的功能潜力铺平了道路。

Abstract: Lead zirconate (PbZrO3, PZO) is a prototypical antiferroelectric (AFE) system promising for applications such as energy storage and neuromorphic computing. However, in conventional thin-film architectures, its functional potential is often limited by substrate clamping. Although emerging freestanding membrane techniques offer an approach to release this clamping and enable dynamic strain engineering, a systematic understanding of how external strain modulates the switching between AFE and ferroelectric (FE) states is missing. In this study, we investigate the uniaxial strain effects on PZO membrane capacitors transferred onto flexible substrates via a gold-bonding method. We demonstrate that in-plane dynamic strain modulates the AFE-FE switching and functional properties. Corroborated by atomistic simulations, we reveal that the strain-dependent evolution arises from the superposition of the intrinsic strain dependence of specific domain orientation variants and extrinsic strain-induced redistribution of the relative population of different domain variants. These findings provide a predictive understanding of uniaxial strain tuning of AFE-FE switching in PZO, and pave the way for exploiting the functional potential of freestanding antiferroelectric membranes.

</details>


### [144] [Amorphous selenium-alloyed TeOx thin-film solar cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38643)
*Zhaoheng Ling,Linqu Luo,Chrysa Aivalioti,Temur Maksudov,Mengxi Zhang,Theodoulos Kalogerakis,Lazaros Panagiotidis,Elefterios Lidorikis,Spyros Doukas,Nikolaos Kalfagiannis,Epameinondas Tziortzios,Leonidas Tsetseris,Huihui Zhu,Ao Liu,Yong-Young Noh,Martin Heeney,Shadi Fatayer,Thomas D. Anthopoulos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过硒合金化策略调控真空升华法制备的二氧化碲（TeO₂）薄膜的缺陷化学，抑制残余金属Te⁰，并成功制造出光电转换效率约1.87%的薄膜太阳能电池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeO₂是一种宽带隙氧化物，具有加工灵活性和新兴的p型半导体行为，在光电和光伏领域前景广阔。但采用真空升华等可规模化工艺制备的TeO₂薄膜会残留金属态Te⁰，引入陷阱态、限制空穴传输并促进载流子复合，严重制约器件性能，因此亟需一种有效手段来调控其缺陷化学。

Method: 采用TeO₂:Se（90:10 wt%）混合粉末进行热蒸发，随后在225°C空气中退火长达9小时，制备非晶TeOₓ:Se薄膜（优化条件下光学带隙0.98 eV）。通过结构、化学、光学和电学表征，结合构建ITO/ZnO/TeOₓ:Se/MoOₓ/Ag结构的薄膜太阳能电池，系统研究退火时间对Se再分布、残余Te⁰含量、表面粗糙度及能带边位置的影响。

Result: 长时间退火促进Se再分布，将残余Te⁰抑制至约0.4%，降低薄膜表面粗糙度，并使价带边发生位移。基于80 nm、退火9小时的TeOₓ:Se活性层的器件性能最优，在300-1000 nm范围内产生宽带光电流，开路电压0.50 V，短路电流密度8.50 mA cm⁻²，填充因子0.44，最高光电转换效率约1.87%。

Conclusion: 硒合金化TeOₓ可作为可行的氧化物衍生薄膜光伏吸收层，化学态控制是提升光伏性能的有效途径。该工作为开发TeO₂基光伏材料及通过缺陷工程优化器件性能提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Tellurium dioxide (TeO$_2$) is a wide-bandgap oxide with attractive processing versatility and emerging p-type semiconducting behaviour, making it a promising material system for optoelectronic and photovoltaic applications. However, TeO$_2$-derived thin films prepared by scalable manufacturing processes, such as vacuum sublimation, can contain residual metallic Te$^0$ species that introduce trap states, limit hole transport, and promote carrier recombination. Here, we demonstrate a selenium alloying strategy to control the defect chemistry of vacuum-sublimed TeO$_x$ films. TeO$_2$:Se powder mixtures (90:10~wt\%) were thermally evaporated and subsequently annealed in air at 225~$^\circ$C for up to 9~h, yielding amorphous TeO$_x$:Se films with an optical bandgap of 0.98~eV under optimised conditions. Structural, chemical, optical, and electrical analyses show that prolonged annealing promotes Se redistribution, suppresses residual Te$^0$ to approximately 0.4\%, reduces the film's surface roughness, and shifts the valence-band edge. To evaluate the potential of TeO$_x$:Se for photovoltaic applications, we fabricated thin-film solar cells with an ITO/ZnO/TeO$_x$:Se/MoO$_x$/Ag architecture. Devices based on an 80~nm TeO$_x$:Se active layer annealed for 9~h exhibit optimal performance, with broadband photocurrent generation from 300 to 1000~nm, yielding an open-circuit voltage of 0.50~V, a short-circuit current density of 8.50~mA~cm$^{-2}$, a fill factor of 0.44, and a maximum power conversion efficiency of approximately 1.87\%. These results establish selenium-alloyed TeO$_x$ as a viable oxide-derived absorber for thin-film photovoltaics and highlight chemical-state control as an effective route for improving photovoltaic performance.

</details>


### [145] [Exceptional Superconducting Interfaces between Graphene and Atomic Chains in A15-MoRe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39694)
*Hiroto Ogura,Yuto Tsukidate,Shun Kondo,Zhuoqun Li,Natsuki Sasaki,Tsunenobu Sato,Azusa Utsumi,Tomohiro Otsuka,Mitsuhiro Saito,Kazutoshi Inoue,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Yuichi Ikuhara,Toshiaki Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示石墨烯/MoRe界面异常高透明度的微观起源：沉积态MoRe在接触石墨烯时会自发形成A15超导相，其独特的1D过渡金属原子链与石墨烯边缘晶格失配<1%，d轨道与石墨烯Dirac锥在K点附近杂化；而退火触发向hcp结构的相变会严重破坏约瑟夫森透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高透明、抗磁场超导接触是实现二维量子器件的关键瓶颈。MoRe合金虽被经验性地广泛采用（能在高磁场下对石墨烯保持弹道输运与高透明接触），但其反常透明度的微观起源长期成谜，亟待阐明。

Method: 结合低温输运测量、X射线衍射（XRD）和原子分辨电子显微镜，表征沉积态与退火态MoRe/石墨烯界面的相结构与输运性能；通过第一性原理计算分析A15相1D过渡金属原子链的d轨道与石墨烯Dirac锥在K点附近的轨道杂化；并对比退火引发的A15→hcp相变对约瑟夫森透明度的影响。

Result: 沉积态MoRe在直接接触石墨烯时自发形成A15相；A15结构中特有的1D过渡金属原子链与石墨烯边缘晶格近乎完美匹配（失配<1%）。第一性原理计算证明这些1D链的d轨道可能与石墨烯Dirac锥在K点附近发生杂化。热退火则触发向hcp结构的相变，严重降低约瑟夫森透明度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了MoRe最优化接触的结构与电子起源，并将A15相超导体确立为几何上理想、可通用的平台，可用于基于二维材料实现鲁棒的量子电子学器件。

Abstract: Realizing highly transparent and magnetic-field-resilient superconducting contacts is a critical bottleneck for the advancement of two-dimensional (2D) quantum devices. Molybdenum-rhenium (MoRe) alloys have been empirically favored as they uniquely yield ballistic, highly transparent contacts to graphene that survive in high-magnetic-field regimes; however, the microscopic origin of this anomalous transparency has remained a long-standing puzzle. Here, we resolve this paradox by revealing that pristine graphene/MoRe interfaces are governed by the classical A15 superconducting phase. Combining low-temperature transport, X-ray diffraction, and atomic-resolution electron microscopy, we demonstrate that as-deposited MoRe spontaneously forms the A15 phase in direct contact with graphene, whereas thermal annealing triggers a phase transition to a hexagonal close-packed (hcp) structure that severely degrades the Josephson transparency. Crucially, the one-dimensional (1D) transition-metal atomic chains unique to the A15 structure exhibit a near-perfect lattice match (mismatch < 1%) with graphene edges. First-principles calculations further prove that the d-orbitals of these historically renowned 1D chains possibly hybridize with graphene's Dirac cone near the K point. These findings not only uncover the structural and electronic origins of optimal MoRe contacts but also establish A15-phase superconductors as a geometrically ideal, universal platform for realizing robust quantum electronics based on 2D materials.

</details>


### [146] [A Luminescent Organic Triradical as an Optically Addressable Qudit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38762)
*Kueyoung E. Kim,Avik Bhanja,Alistair Inglis,William K. Myers,Sam L. Bayliss,Richard H. Friend,Petri Murto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种发光分子三自由基，由三个三苯甲基基团共价连接在中心苯环上，表现出633 nm和690 nm两个发光带，分别对应激发态四重态和双重态。基态四重态比双重态低约1 meV，室温下两者近等占据。作者展示了光学初始化的自旋极化、基态四重态的相干操控与光学读出，并通过室温光探测磁共振和5 mT下达3%的磁光致发光，展示了其在光学量子传感中的潜力，是一种候选的光学可寻址分子qutrit/qudit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发光分子三苯甲基自由基为光学可寻址电子自旋提供了新的选择，在量子传感领域具有广阔前景。相比传统量子比特系统，多自旋（qudit）体系拥有更大的态空间。作者旨在开发一种兼具发光、光学可初始化、相干可控且可光学读出的分子体系，以突破单一量子比特的局限，实现室温下的光学量子传感。

Method: 作者设计并合成了含三个三苯甲基基团、以中心苯环为骨架的发光三自由基。通过光致发光光谱与时间分辨测量，区分633 nm（激发四重态）和690 nm（激发双重态）两个发光带及其动力学演变。利用光学泵浦实现自旋极化初始化；借助微波脉冲对基态四重态进行相干操控；通过室温光探测磁共振（ODMR）和磁光致发光（magneto-photoluminescence, MPL）实验验证光学读出与磁场响应能力。

Result: 该三自由基呈现两个不同时间演变的光致发光带（633 nm和690 nm），分别归属为激发态四重态和双重态。基态四重态比双重态低约1 meV（对应11.5 K），使室温下双重态与四重态近等占据。实验成功实现了光学初始化的自旋极化、基态四重态的相干控制和光学读出；室温ODMR与磁光致发光在5 mT下响应高达3%。

Conclusion: 该发光三自由基是光学可寻址分子qudit的理想候选材料，成功实现了初始化、相干操控和光学读出三大要素，并展示了室温量子传感的可行性。其丰富的态空间为超越量子比特系统、利用更大希尔伯特空间进行量子信息处理与传感提供了新机遇。

Abstract: Luminescent molecular trityl radicals provide options for optically-addressable electron spins and are promising for quantum sensing. Here, we present a luminescent triradical comprising three trityl groups covalently attached to a central phenyl ring. We find two distinct photoluminescence bands at 633 nm and 690 nm with different temporal evolutions, which we attribute to excited quartet and doublet states respectively. The ground state quartet is 1 meV (11.5K) lower in energy than the doublet, resulting in near-equal population of doublets and quartets at room temperature. We demonstrate optically initialized spin polarization, coherent control of the ground state quartet, and optical readout. Potential for optical quantum sensing is shown via optically detected magnetic resonance at room temperature and magneto-photoluminescence up to 3% at 5 mT. This material is a candidate optically addressable molecular qudit, providing opportunities to leverage larger state spaces than qubit systems.

</details>


### [147] [Multiscale evaluation of the elastic response of polycrystalline NiAl using molecular statics, EBSD-based finite element and analytical homogenization methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38783)
*Szymon Nosewicz,Marcin Maździarz,Paweł Hołobut,Witold Chromiński,Katarzyna Kowalczyk-Gajewska,Grzegorz Jurczak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度框架，通过分子动力学获取NiAl单晶弹性常数，再结合EBSD构建真实多晶模型，用有限元和解析均匀化方法预测多晶NiAl的弹性模量与泊松比，并与实验和经典解析估算对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确确定金属间化合物NiAl在微观尺度的弹性常数，并建立从单晶原子尺度到多晶宏观尺度的跨尺度关联，以真实反映晶粒各向异性与形貌对多晶弹性行为的影响。

Method: 采用多尺度框架：分子动力学计算Pm$\bar{3}$m对称单晶NiAl的刚度张量；利用EBSD实验数据重建保留真实晶粒取向和尺寸分布的三维多晶模型；将原子尺度结果作为输入，结合有限元建模与解析均匀化方法模拟多晶单轴拉伸，计算有效杨氏模量E和泊松比ν，并与实验值及Hashin-Shtrikman上界、自洽近似等经典解析估算对比。

Result: 在模型假设下，有限元数值结果与实验测得的杨氏模量E和泊松比ν吻合良好；解析方法中，Hashin-Shtrikman上界和自洽近似能以较小偏差复现实验杨氏模量。

Conclusion: 所提出的多尺度框架能有效预测NiAl多晶的弹性行为，原子尺度到微观尺度的信息传递有效，有限元模拟与实验高度一致，Hashin-Shtrikman上界和自洽近似可提供可靠的解析估算。

Abstract: The presented work proposes a multiscale framework for the numerical and analytical determination of the elastic constants of the intermetallic nickel aluminide (NiAl) at the microscopic scale. Molecular dynamics (MD) calculations have been employed to evaluate the stiffness tensors of a single NiAl monocrystalline body with cubic Pm$\bar{3}$m symmetry. The atomistic results were transferred to the upper scale as an input to micromechanical models. Here, finite element modeling (FEM) and analytical homogenization methods were used to simulate the elastic behavior of polycrystalline NiAl, with special attention to grain anisotropy and its morphology. The microstructural features of the heterogeneous body were evaluated experimentally using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) and subsequently used to generate a 3D polycrystalline geometric model that preserves realistic distributions of grain orientations and grain sizes. The reconstructed NiAl polycrystal was subjected to uniaxial tensile test simulations using FEM to evaluate the effective Young's modulus ($E$) and Poisson's ratio ($ν$). The numerical results were confronted with the experimental values of $E$ and~$ν$. With these model assumptions, a very good agreement between numerical and experimental results was achieved. Finally, the experimental results were compared with classical analytical estimates. Among the analytical approaches, the Hashin-Shtrikman upper bound and self-consistent approximation reproduced the experimental Young's modulus with minor deviations.

</details>


### [148] [Autonomous Quantum Measurement with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38850)
*Eunbi Rha,Abhijatmedhi Chotrattanapituk,Mouyang Cheng,Chung-tao Chou,Shenyong Ye,Ashley S. Dale,Long Ju,Luqiao Liu,Mingda Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 QUANTA，一个基于智能体人工智能的框架，用于自主表征量子材料。它以 PPMS 为实验平台，结合文献/协议/历史测量检索与规划、排障、数据解读智能体，并由确定性控制层验证仪器动作，实现证据驱动、受保护、闭环的量子材料表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料的表征需要大量实验经验、迭代决策和难以记录与传承的实验室知识；现有流程依赖人工、可重复性和可迁移性差，因此需要一种能够自主决策、闭环运行并安全可靠的智能化表征框架。

Method: QUANTA 采用智能体 AI 框架，以广泛使用的 Physical Property Measurement System (PPMS) 为实验平台。核心设计包括：1）检索增强生成（RAG），利用科学文献、实验协议和先前测量数据；2）多个专业化智能体分别负责实验规划、故障排查与数据解读；3）独立的确定性控制层对仪器动作进行验证以保障安全；4）根据实时数据评估测量质量、修订实验计划并选择后续测量，形成闭环。

Result: 通过拓扑外尔半金属 TaP 的输运测量，以及磁性材料 Co2MnGa 非线性响应的研究，展示了 QUANTA 的实际能力，验证了其自主、证据驱动、受保护和闭环的表征能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明 QUANTA 是一个通用的、证据驱动的、受保护且闭环的量子材料表征框架，有望降低对人工专业经验的依赖，提升表征的自动化与可迁移性。

Abstract: Quantum materials exhibit diverse electronic and functional phenomena, but their characterization requires extensive experimental expertise, iterative decision-making and accumulated laboratory knowledge that is difficult to document and transfer. Here we introduce Quantum Agent (QUANTA), an agentic artificial intelligence (AI) framework for autonomous quantum-materials characterization. Using a widely adopted Physical Property Measurement System (PPMS) as an experimental platform, QUANTA combines retrieval augmentation using scientific literature, experimental protocols and prior measurements with specialized agents for experimental planning, troubleshooting, and data interpretation. A separate deterministic control layer validates instrument actions. Guided by incoming data, QUANTA evaluates measurement quality, revises experimental plans and selects subsequent measurements. We demonstrate these capabilities through transport measurements of the topological Weyl semimetal TaP, as well as investigations of nonlinear responses in magnetic material Co2MnGa. These results establish QUANTA as a generalizable framework for evidence-driven, safeguarded and closed-loop quantum-materials characterization.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Between the Lines: Interaction Interpolation in a Multi Element Machine Learning Interatomic Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38876)
*Ademola Soyemi,Kipton Barros,Timothy C. Germann,Benjamin T. Nebgen,Brenden W. Hamilton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个轻量级通用机器学习原子间势（MLIAP），覆盖12种金属元素，通过逐步主动学习构建紧凑多样的数据集，仅用一元和二元训练数据即可外推到三元、四元合金及新元素，以较低计算成本实现可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIAPs）虽加速了材料研究，但针对每个具体问题都需构建定制势函数，这一过程成为瓶颈；而基础（foundation）MLIAPs虽因训练集覆盖化学空间广而具迁移性，但其体积庞大、计算成本高昂，难以常规使用。因此需要在迁移性与计算成本之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用逐步主动学习（step-wise active learning, AL）方法，构建一个紧凑但多样化的数据集与模型；该模型通过内插此前见过的相互作用来实现良好泛化，并在网络内部利用“炼金术式外推”（alchemical extrapolation）迁移到新元素。

Result: 所得到的势函数能有效迁移到未见过的、更高阶的合金体系：仅需一元和二元训练数据即可捕捉三元和四元体系；模型还可仅凭新元素及其合金的一元数据，通过网络内的炼金术式外推迁移到新元素。

Conclusion: 在目标明确的、以多样性驱动的数据集上训练的MLIAPs能够以相对较低的计算成本提供可迁移性，为面向材料建模的通用、资源高效型MLIAPs提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: The continuous development of machine learning inter-atomic potentials (MLIAPs)has greatly accelerated materials research in recent years. However, the need to con- struct custom MLIAPs for each unique problem remains a bottleneck which raises the question of the transferability of MLIAPs. While foundation MLIAPs can achieve transferability due to the broad coverage of chemical space represented in their training sets, their size and computational cost limits their routine use. We address this gap by developing a lightweight general-purpose MLIAP for metal alloys spanning twelve elements (Al, Sc, Ti, V, Cu, Zn, Zr, Nb, Mo, Hf, Ta, W). Here, we utilize a step-wise active learning (AL) approach to develop a compact yet diverse dataset and model that generalizes well by interpolating from previously seen interactions. The resulting potentials transfer effectively to unseen, higher body-order alloys systems, requiring only unary and binary training data to capture ternary and quaternary systems. The model also transfers well to new elements via an alchemical extrapolation within the network, requiring only unary data for the new element and its alloys. These findings suggest that MLIAPs trained on targeted, diversity-driven datasets provide transferability at a relatively low computational cost, offering a practical path toward general-purpose, resource-efficient MLIAPs for materials modeling.

</details>


### [150] [Collective sliding relaxor ferroelectricity across thousand-layer van der Waals superstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38928)
*Nianze Shang,Cuiju Yu,Yucai Lin,Juan C. Arias-Muñoz,Jiajie Qi,Quanlin Guo,MD Gius Uddin,Youqiang Huang,Tuomas Pajunpää,Christopher P. Edwards,Rodolfo E. Canet-Albiach,Faisal Ahmed,Andreas C. Liapis,Yaoqiang Zhou,Zhibin Zhang,Hao Hong,Qing Dai,Andrea Alù,José L. Lado,Kaihui Liu,Zhipei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种此前未知的弛豫铁电态——滑动弛豫铁电性（S-RFE），通过在菱方氮化硼中进行堆叠相工程，利用层间协同滑动产生集体弛豫行为，实现了约-1.6%的可逆电致机械形变和高达约1200 m⁴C⁻²的电致伸缩系数，且在1.6 K低温下仍能保持巨电致机械功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弛豫铁电体虽然具有优异的电致伸缩响应，但其行为依赖于化学复杂性与结构无序，由局域成分涨落生成极性纳米区，导致其微观机制难以控制和工程化。作者希望探索一种不依赖化学无序、可通过结构工程实现并调控的弛豫铁电机制，从而为电致机械材料提供可设计的新平台。

Method: 作者在菱方氮化硼（rhombohedral boron nitride）中采用堆叠相工程（stacking-phase engineering），构建出工程化的堆叠相异质性，形成可作为极性纳米区结构类似物的滑动畴（sliding domains）。在外加电场下，这些滑动畴通过层间滑动实现协同取向对齐，从而产生集体弛豫行为。实验测量了约300 nm厚（约1000个原子层）晶体的电致机械形变与电致伸缩系数，并测试了低至1.6 K的低温行为。

Result: 所构建的滑动弛豫铁电态产生了约-1.6%的可逆电致机械形变，且迟滞几乎可以忽略；对应的电致伸缩系数 Q₃₃ 约为1200 m⁴C⁻²，比大多数晶体电致伸缩材料高出数个数量级。与传统弛豫体在低温下因偶极冻结而响应受抑制不同，S-RFE在1.6 K下依然保持活性，并维持巨电致机械功能。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了滑动弛豫铁电性作为实现弛豫行为的新途径，揭示了原子尺度的层间运动如何被放大为跨越约一千个原子层的集体介观功能，为基于范德华材料的电致机械、光子学和量子技术提供了"材料按设计构建"（materials-by-design）的平台。

Abstract: Relaxor ferroelectrics are among the most important electromechanical materials owing to their exceptionally large electrostrictive responses. However, conventional relaxor behavior is generally associated with chemical complexity and structural disorder, where local compositional fluctuations generate polar nanoregions, making the microscopic origin of this behavior difficult to control and engineer. Here we report sliding relaxor ferroelectricity (S-RFE), a previously unknown relaxor state in which engineered stacking-phase heterogeneity enables cooperative interlayer sliding to generate collective relaxor behavior. Through stacking-phase engineering in rhombohedral boron nitride, we create sliding domains that act as structural analogues of polar nanoregions and cooperatively align under an electric field through interlayer sliding. This collective state produces reversible electromechanical deformation of up to approximately $-1.6\%$ with negligible hysteresis in approximately 300-nm-thick crystals, corresponding to approximately 1000 atomic layers. The resulting electrostriction coefficient ($Q_{33}$ of approximately $1200\,\mathrm{m}^{4}\mathrm{C}^{-2}$) exceeds those of most crystalline electrostrictors by orders of magnitude. Unlike conventional relaxors, whose electromechanical response is often suppressed by dipolar freezing at low temperatures, S-RFE remains active down to 1.6 K and maintains giant electromechanical functionality. Our results establish sliding relaxor ferroelectricity as a new route to relaxor behavior and reveal how atomic-scale interlayer motion can be amplified into collective mesoscale functionality across approximately one thousand layers, providing a materials-by-design platform for electromechanical, photonic, and quantum technologies based on van der Waals materials.

</details>


### [151] [Development and application of a machine learning potential for diamond surfaces and their sliding interfaces with silicon nitride and silica: effect of passivation, orientation and load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38937)
*Enrico Pedretti,Maria Clelia Righi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个覆盖 C/H/N/O/Si 化学空间的反应性机器学习原子间势，用于模拟金刚石、氮化硅和二氧化硅的界面滑动，揭示了表面钝化、取向和载荷如何共同决定界面键合与摩擦行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石的摩擦与磨损由其表面化学决定（滑动界面是否形成共价键）。第一性原理模拟虽能研究表面取向与钝化的作用，但受限于数百原子和数十皮秒的尺度，无法覆盖实际磨损过程的长度与时间尺度，因此需要一种兼具近从头算精度与大尺度模拟能力的势函数来填补这一空白。

Method: 开发了一个 C/H/N/O/Si 化学空间的反应性机器学习原子间势，覆盖金刚石、氮化硅、氧化硅及其表面、界面和环境分子。该势函数在大型多样化结构数据集上训练，并在多项物理性质上验证，包括金刚石表面的从头算反应能垒，以及非晶 Si3N4 和 SiO2 的结构与力学性质。随后模拟了金刚石-Si3N4 界面在四种表面取向、清洁与 H/OH 钝化终端、以及 0.1 至 23 GPa 法向载荷下的滑动。

Result: 识别出两种不同机制：一种是滑动局域在界面处，剪切应力与界面键密度成正比；另一种是剪切分布到较软材料内部，此时完全界面键合导致的黏附占主导。结果表明钝化、表面取向和载荷共同决定界面键合与摩擦行为。

Conclusion: 该势函数能以近从头算精度对金刚石、氮化硅和二氧化硅进行超出第一性原理尺度的反应性模拟。研究结果揭示了金刚石磨损的环境敏感性来源，并为未来研究犁沟微凸体磨损、化学机械抛光和陶瓷非晶薄膜性能提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Friction and wear of diamond are controlled by its surface chemistry, which determines whether covalent bonds form across the sliding interface. First-principles simulations have explored the role of surface orientation and passivation, but are limited to a few hundred atoms and tens of picoseconds. To bridge this gap, we develop a reactive machine learning interatomic potential for the C/H/N/O/Si chemical space, covering diamond, silicon nitride, silicon oxide, their surfaces, interfaces, and environmental molecules. The potential is trained on a large dataset of diverse structures and is tested on multiple physical properties, including ab initio reaction barriers on diamond surfaces, and structural/mechanical properties of amorphous Si$_3$N$_4$ and SiO$_2$. We then simulate sliding of diamond-Si$_3$N$_4$ interfaces with four surface orientations, clean and H/OH-passivated terminations, and normal loads from 0.1 to 23 GPa. We identify two different regimes: one where sliding is localized at the interface, and shear stress is proportional to the interfacial bond density, and another where shear is distributed inside the softer material, and adhesion due to full interfacial bonding dominates. These results show how passivation, surface orientation and load together determine interfacial bonding and friction, with implications for the environmental sensitivity of diamond wear. More broadly, the potential enables reactive simulations of diamond, silicon nitride and silica with near-ab initio accuracy at length and time scales beyond first-principles methods, providing a useful tool for future studies of wear at plowing asperities, chemical-mechanical polishing, and properties of amorphous ceramic films.

</details>


### [152] [Comment on "Is there a density-gradient expansion for the electronic exchange energy?"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38943)
*Mario Benites,Angel Rosado,Efstratios Manousakis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 作者反驳 Perdew 等人对其早前发现的质疑，论证长波正确极化函数 q² 系数中交换项(b_x)与关联项(b_c)的单独贡献依赖于库仑相互作用的红外正规化方式，但二者之和 b_xc 与正规化无关；并指出 Perdew 等所依赖的 Qiao 推导中存在穿越奇点、需取柯西主值的不当积分，且不同正规化会给出不同交换梯度系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Perdew 等人对"交换与关联对 q² 系数的单独贡献依赖红外正规化"这一结论的质疑，厘清交换梯度系数是否唯一确定，并说明为何应优先采用与正规化无关的组合系数 b_xc。

Method: 通过分析 Qian (2015) 推导中形式为 ∫_{-a}^{b} dx/x 的积分——该积分穿越奇点，作为普通瑕积分不存在，其有限柯西主值只是通过在奇点两侧排除等长区间得到的一种特殊正规化，排除不等长区间会得到不同结果；同时独立对比 Sham 使用屏蔽库仑势 e^{-λ_0 r}/r 的计算与 Svendsen–von Barth、Engel–Vosko 使用裸库仑势的计算，发现交换梯度系数存在差异，且该差异在 λ_0 极小时依然存在；进而证明单独贡献的歧义在组合系数 b_xc 中相互抵消。

Result: Qian 推导中的积分依赖柯西主值的等区间排除，采用不同正规化方式会得到不同交换梯度系数；Sham 的屏蔽相互作用计算得出的交换梯度系数与裸库仑计算结果不同，且在 1/λ_0 达到可观测宇宙尺度时差异仍存在；单独交换、关联项的歧义在组合系数 b_xc 中恰好抵消，使 b_xc 与正规化无关。

Conclusion: 所谓"唯一确定的常规交换系数"并不成立，交换与关联的单独贡献具有正规化依赖性；应优先采用与正规化无关的组合系数 b_xc，而非坚持"完全无屏蔽"的结果。

Abstract: Perdew et al. [arXiv:2610.07201 (2026)] dispute our finding [Phys. Rev. B 113, 115136 (2026)] that the separate exchange ($b_x$) and correlation ($b_c$) contributions to the $q^2$ coefficient of the long-wavelength proper polarization function depend on the infrared regularization of the Coulomb interaction, although their sum $b_{xc}=b_x+b_c$ does not. We question their claim that the conventional exchange coefficient is uniquely determined. In Qian's [J. Math. Phys. 56, 111901 (2015)] derivation, which Perdew et al. cite in support of their claim of a unique exchange-gradient coefficient, an integral of the form $\int_{-a}^{b}dx/x$ crosses a singularity and does not exist as an ordinary improper integral. Its finite Cauchy principal value (which is a particular way of regularizing the integral) is obtained only by excluding equal intervals on the two sides of the singularity; unequal exclusions give different answers. Independently, Sham's calculation with the screened interaction $e^{-λ_0 r}/r$ gives an exchange-gradient coefficient different from the bare-Coulomb calculations of Svendsen--von Barth [Int. J. Quantum Chem. 56, 351 (1995)] and Engel--Vosko [Phys. Rev. B 42, 4940 (1990)]. The discrepancy remains when $λ_0>0$ is so small that $1/λ_0$ is comparable to the size of the observable universe, making the two interactions experimentally indistinguishable on laboratory scales. Why should the exactly unscreened result be preferred, even if its calculation is accepted as valid? Our calculation shows that the ambiguity of the separate contributions cancels in the combined, regulator-independent coefficient $b_{xc}$.

</details>


### [153] [A rhombic spin-1 interpretation of a room-temperature GaN emitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39081)
*Daniel Hashemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究GaN中单缺陷在室温下的光学检测磁共振，发现一个g≈4的谱线无法由最小S=3/2模型解释，并提出菱形S=1模型能更好拟合三条谱线位置，同时建议用零场扫描实验直接检验该模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN单缺陷在室温下展现光学检测磁共振，但占多数的缺陷类别中，最小S=3/2模型无法解释g≈4的谱线；对于某一缺陷，该谱线位于两条外线的和附近，暗示可能涉及三个共享能级间的跃迁。作者希望找到能统一解释三条谱线位置的物理模型。

Method: 对比分析两种自旋模型：其一为菱形（rhombic）S=1模型，用于复现三条谱线位置；其二为包含全部三条跃迁的二阶S=3/2拟合及已发表的最小模型。通过计算模型与观测谱线位置的均方根（rms）误差评估拟合质量，并讨论一个跃迁梯度（ladder）模型的可行性，最后提出零场扫描实验方案。

Result: 菱形S=1模型能以8.1–9.8 MHz的均方根误差复现三条谱线位置；而包含全部三条跃迁的二阶S=3/2拟合以及已发表的最小模型均无法复现这些位置。若g≈4谱线是位于两条外线和附近的独立跃迁，则拟合强线的梯度模型仍具可行性。此外，未分辨的频率窗口偏移使现有数据无法唯一确立S=1的归属。

Conclusion: 菱形S=1模型是目前最贴合观测谱线位置的自旋模型，但现有数据尚不能唯一确认该归属；作者建议进行零场扫描实验，以直接检验孤立的S=1归属。

Abstract: Single defects in GaN show optically detected magnetic resonance at room temperature. In the more abundant class, a minimal $S=3/2$ model leaves a $g\approx 4$ line unexplained. For one defect, this line lies near the sum of the two outer lines, consistent with transitions among three shared levels. A rhombic $S=1$ model reproduces the three line positions with rms errors of 8.1-9.8 MHz. The tested second-order $S=3/2$ fits containing all three transitions and the published minimal model do not reproduce these positions. A ladder fitted to the strong lines remains viable if the $g\approx 4$ line is a separate transition near the sum of the outer two. An unresolved frequency-window shift prevents the existing data from establishing the $S=1$ assignment uniquely. We propose a zero-field sweep that directly tests the isolated $S=1$ assignment.

</details>


### [154] [Lone-Pair Stereoactivity Overrides Mass Scaling of Thermal Expansion and Heat Conduction in Non-vander Waals Layered Bismuth Oxychalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39213)
*Linzhen Li,Guangwu Zhang,Ziyan Qian,Wei Jing,Yang Lu,Jiaqi Gu,Kaifeng Huang,Yanguang Zhou,Qiye Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本论文揭示铋氧硫族化合物 Bi2O2X（X=S, Se, Te）的热膨胀和热输运并非由硫族原子质量主导，而是由 Bi3+ 6s² 孤对电子的立体化学活性主导；Bi2O2Se 在该体系中呈现出最高的热导率和对晶体管鳍片散热最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为更重的硫族原子会软化等结构半导体晶格、增大热膨胀并降低热导率，但 Bi2O2Se 正被快速发展用于鳍式及全环绕栅极晶体管，其基本热膨胀和热输运机制却仍不清晰。作者旨在厘清混合阴离子层状晶体中孤对电子活性与硫族质量各自对热物性的真实贡献。

Method: 作者采用路径积分分子动力学计算三种 Bi2O2X 的体积热膨胀系数，并通过同位素替换（将 S 质量代入 Bi2O2Se）分离质量效应；结合包含四声子散射和 Wigner 相干性的声子输运计算预测热导率；用 300–500 K 热反射测量法（thermoreflectance）和原位 X 射线衍射对 Bi2O2Se 单晶进行实验验证；最后用声子玻尔兹曼输运模拟 12 nm 宽鳍片的热点温度与热失配应变。

Result: 300 K 下体积热膨胀系数从 Bi2O2S 的 8.14×10⁻⁵ K⁻¹ 降至 Bi2O2Se 的 6.72×10⁻⁵ 和 Bi2O2Te 的 6.61×10⁻⁵ K⁻¹，尽管碲化物体积大 13%；而将 S 质量代入 Bi2O2Se 仅使该值改变 0.6%。Bi2O2S 中孤对电子驱动的 Bi 偏心使 b 轴膨胀为 a 轴的 1.63 倍，但面内热导仍各向同性（偏差在 3% 内）。声子计算预测 Bi2O2Se 热导率最高（300 K 面内 1.83、面外 0.95 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹），因 Bi2O2S 受非谐散射限制、Bi2O2Te 受慢的面外声子限制。实验与预测偏差在 6.4% 以内；鳍片模拟显示 Bi2O2Se 能最小化热点温度和栅介质处的热失配应变。

Conclusion: 孤对电子立体活性而非硫族质量支配着 Bi2O2X 的热膨胀与热输运，Bi2O2Se 凭借适中的非谐性与声子特性在该体系中展现出最优导热性能，因而成为鳍式及全环绕栅极晶体管中在散热和热应力匹配方面最具优势的材料。

Abstract: Heavier chalcogens are generally expected to soften isostructural semiconductor lattices, raising thermal expansion and lowering thermal conductivity. Layered bismuth oxychalcogenides, Bi2O2X (X = S, Se, Te), are mixed-anion crystals whose covalent slabs and ionic sheets are bound electrostatically rather than by van der Waals forces and whose Bi3+ 6s^2 lone pairs can become stereochemically active. Despite the rapid development of Bi2O2Se for fin and gate-all-around transistors, its fundamental thermal expansion and heat transport mechanisms remain poorly understood. Here, we show that lone-pair stereoactivity, not chalcogen mass, governs both properties. Path-integral molecular dynamics shows that the volumetric thermal expansion coefficient at 300 K decreases from 8.14 x 10^-5 K^-1 in Bi2O2S to 6.72 x 10^-5 and 6.61 x 10^-5 K^-1 in Bi2O2Se and Bi2O2Te, respectively, despite a 13% larger unit cell in the telluride, whereas substituting the S mass into Bi2O2Se changes its value by only 0.6%. Lone-pair-driven Bi off-centering in orthorhombic Bi2O2S makes b-axis expansion 1.63 times a-axis expansion yet leaves in-plane thermal conduction isotropic within 3%. Phonon transport calculations including four-phonon scattering and Wigner coherence predict a thermal conductivity maximum in Bi2O2Se (1.83 and 0.95 W m^-1 K^-1 in the in-plane and cross-plane directions, respectively, at 300 K) because Bi2O2S is limited by anharmonic scattering and Bi2O2Te by slow cross-plane phonons. Thermoreflectance measurements from 300 to 500 K and in situ X-ray diffraction on Bi2O2Se single crystals agree with these predictions within 6.4%. Phonon Boltzmann transport simulations of 12-nm-wide fins, which run hotter than Fourier diffusion predicts, show that Bi2O2Se minimizes the hotspot temperature and the thermal-mismatch strain at the gate dielectric.

</details>


### [155] [Colossal magnetoresistance in narrow-gap magnetic semiconductor Eu$_2$CdAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39218)
*Zhiyu Zhou,Zhi-Cheng Wang,Xiyu Chen,Xin-Yu Zhao,Yaran Duan,Shiran Tao,Guang-Han Cao,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了窄带隙Zintl砷化物Eu₂CdAs₂的合成与磁输运性质，该材料在9 K发生反铁磁有序，在9 T下展现出-10⁴%的巨磁电阻（CMR），并揭示Eu基Zintl相中CMR幅值与低温活化能之间的近似指数标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现具有显著巨磁电阻（CMR）效应的反铁磁材料并精确调控其响应，是材料科学中长期存在的重大挑战；同时缺乏指导磁输运性质设计的普适策略。

Method: 合成了窄带隙Zintl砷化物Eu₂CdAs₂，系统研究了其合成、相变与磁输运性质；测量其反铁磁有序温度（T_N=9 K）、低温活化能（3.2 meV）及磁场下的磁电阻行为，并系统调研文献中已报道的Eu基Zintl相数据。

Result: Eu₂CdAs₂呈现半导体行为，低温活化能为3.2 meV，在9 T下实现-10⁴%的CMR效应；在T_N以下出现无宏观铁磁序的蝴蝶形磁电阻滞后回线，暗示由反铁磁畴动力学和/或局域磁极化子的形成与逾渗所主导的输运反常。系统调研发现Eu基Zintl相的最大CMR幅值与低温活化能之间呈近似指数标度关系。

Conclusion: 带隙工程是调控磁输运性质的有效策略，Eu基Zintl相可作为探索和操控CMR效应的通用平台。

Abstract: Discovering antiferromagnetic materials with a sizable colossal magnetoresistance (CMR) effect and precisely tuning their responses remain significant challenges in materials science. This work reports the synthesis, phase transitions, and magnetotransport properties of the narrow-gap Zintl arsenide Eu$_2$CdAs$_2$. The compound undergoes antiferromagnetic ordering at $T_\mathrm{N} = 9$ K and exhibits semiconducting behavior with a low-temperature activation energy of 3.2 meV, achieving a large CMR effect of $-10^4\%$ at 9 T. Below $T_\mathrm{N}$, a distinct butterfly-shaped magnetoresistance hysteresis emerges without any macroscopic ferromagnetic order, revealing a transport anomaly likely dictated by AFM domain dynamics and/or the formation and percolation of localized magnetic polarons. Furthermore, a systematic survey of reported Eu-based Zintl phases uncovers a nearly exponential scaling trend between the maximum CMR magnitude and the low-temperature activation energy. This correlation demonstrates that bandgap engineering serves as an effective strategy for tailoring magnetotransport, establishing Eu-containing Zintl phases as a versatile platform for exploring and manipulating CMR effects.

</details>


### [156] [Polarization-locked chiral persistent spin textures in two-dimensional lead-halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39250)
*Palanichamy Gayathri,Biplab Sanyal,Jonas Fransson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 手性间隔阳离子使二维杂化钙钛矿结晶为极性 Sohncke 群 P2₁，极性轴位于无机层内。作者分析该几何允许的自旋-轨道场，并对十种极性手性铅卤钙钛矿、其对映体及四种卤素取代类似物进行第一性原理自旋织构验证，发现导带自旋分裂由沿面内极性轴的 Rashba 相互作用（线性项 γ₁kₓσ_z）主导，而非螺旋 Rashba 场，且对映体选择决定自旋方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维手性杂化钙钛矿在极性 Sohncke 群 P2₁ 中结晶时，极性轴位于无机层内，这种几何允许的自旋-轨道场与传统面外极性假设不同。作者旨在厘清该几何下究竟允许何种自旋-轨道相互作用，并验证何种模型真实描述这些材料的自旋织构，而非仅凭能带能量（缺乏辨别力）来选模型。

Method: 对十种极性手性铅卤钙钛矿、其镜像对映体及四种卤素取代类似物进行第一性原理自旋织构计算；利用点群 C₂（二重轴在层内）的对称性分析允许的 k·p 项，排除螺旋 Rashba 场，保留线性项 γ₁kₓσ_z、手性相关 Dresselhaus 项和径向项；为每个能带对拟合 34 个二能带 k·p 模型，并按自旋方向与计算的一致程度（而非能带能量）排序筛选。

Result: 在所有强极性化合物中，导带仅由持久项（persistent term）描述，γ₁ = 1.09–1.98 eV Å，自旋一致性不低于 0.98；而最佳 Rashba 或 Dresselhaus 模型的自旋一致性仅达 0.20。每种化合物的 R 与 S 对映体极化相反，因而 γ₁ 和持久自旋织构大小相等、方向相反，符合镜像对称要求，且十种化合物全部成立，即对映体选择决定自旋方向。γ₁ 的大小与面内 Pb-X-Pb 键角的不对称程度相关（r = 0.95）。自旋分裂方向上的电子有效质量为 0.26–0.43 m_e，对应的持久自旋螺旋波长为 3.4–8.2 nm。

Conclusion: 极性手性二维钙钛矿的面内极性轴产生一种新型持久自旋织构，由线性项 γ₁kₓσ_z 描述，而非通常的螺旋 Rashba 场。对映体的选择可决定自旋方向，γ₁ 的大小可由 Pb-X-Pb 面内键角不对称性通过化学手段调控，这为基于手性二维钙钛矿的自旋电子学器件设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Chiral spacer cations crystallize two-dimensional hybrid perovskites in the polar Sohncke group $P2_1$, which places the polar axis in the inorganic layer. We analyze the spin-orbit field that this geometry allows and test it against first-principles spin textures of ten polar chiral lead-halide perovskites, their mirror enantiomers and four halide-substituted analogues. The point group $C_2$ with the twofold axis in the layer forbids the helical Rashba field and allows a linear term $γ_1 k_x σ_z$, the Rashba interaction of the in-plane polar axis, together with chirality-related Dresselhaus and radial terms. Thirty-four two-band $k \cdot p$ models were fitted to each band pair and ranked by the agreement of their spin directions with the calculation rather than by the band energies, which cannot distinguish them. In all strongly polar compounds, the conduction band is described by the persistent term alone, with $γ_1 = 1.09$-$1.98$ eV Å and a spin agreement of at least 0.98, whereas the best Rashba or Dresselhaus model reaches 0.20. The $R$ and $S$ enantiomers of each compound have opposite polarizations, and therefore opposite $γ_1$ and opposite persistent spin textures of equal magnitude, as required by the mirror that relates them; this holds in all ten compounds, so the choice of the enantiomer selects the spin direction. The magnitude of $γ_1$ correlates with the disparity of the in-plane Pb-X-Pb angles ($r = 0.95$). With electron masses of $0.26$-$0.43\,m_e$ along the spin-splitting directions, the associated persistent spin helices have wavelengths of 3.4-8.2 nm.

</details>


### [157] [Th$^{4+}$ ion cluster structures and electric field gradients in $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39288)
*Yuxiang Li,Lin Li,Qiaorui Gong,Shanming Li,Chengchun Zhao,Lu Deng,Peixiong Zhang,Yin Hang,Longsheng Ma,Shining Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究 Th:CaF₂ 中由四个填隙 F⁻ 离子电荷补偿的中性 Th 二聚体构型，发现一类低能量构型具有大的电场梯度和小的不对称参数，与实验高 EFG 位点 3 一致，支持其为位点 3 的微观候选结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ²²⁹Th 的低能核异构跃迁是固态核光学钟的理想频率基准，CaF₂ 等宽带隙氟化物晶体可高密度嵌入 Th 核。但 Th⁴⁺ 取代 Ca²⁺ 需要电荷补偿，导致局域缺陷结构与核四极分裂紧密相关，因此亟需从微观上确定 Th:CaF₂ 中高 EFG 位点（位点 3）的具体缺陷结构。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，研究中性的 Th 二聚体（由四个填隙 F⁻ 离子即 Fᵢ⁻ 补偿电荷）。通过比较不同补偿几何构型的相对形成能进行筛选，识别出一族能量低且局域结构高度相似的构型，并计算其电场梯度（EFG）、不对称参数及核四极分裂，再与实验测得的高 EFG 位点 3 及 C13 光谱进行对比。

Result: 筛选出的低能构型族均具有大的电场梯度和小的不对称参数，与实验高 EFG 位点 3 的特征一致；其计算的四极分裂与实测 C13 光谱进一步吻合。能量与光谱双重证据共同支持这些构型。

Conclusion: 由四个填隙 F⁻ 离子补偿电荷的 Th 二聚体可作为 Th:CaF₂ 中位点 3 的微观候选结构，为理解该材料局域缺陷结构与核光谱的联系提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The low-energy nuclear isomeric transition of $^{229}$Th is a promising frequency reference for a solid-state nuclear optical clock. Wide-band-gap fluoride crystals such as CaF$_2$ offer transparent hosts for embedding Th nuclei at high density. Substitution of Th$^{4+}$ for Ca$^{2+}$ requires charge compensation, linking the local defect structure to nuclear quadrupole splitting. We use first-principles calculations to examine neutral Th dimers compensated by four interstitial F ions ($\mathrm{F}_{i}^{-}$). Screening different compensation geometries by their relative formation energies identifies a family of low-energy configurations with closely similar local structures. These configurations share large electric field gradients and small asymmetry parameters, consistent with the experimental high-EFG site 3. Their calculated quadrupole splittings are further compared with the measured C13 spectra. Together, the energetic and spectroscopic results support $\mathrm{F}_{i}^{-}$-compensated Th dimers as candidate microscopic structures for site 3 in Th:CaF$_2$.

</details>


### [158] [Predicting unstable planar fault energies in multicomponent alloys from pure elemental components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39495)
*C Tang,J Liu,J Yi,M R Barnett*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可测的不稳定层错能（UPFEs）的温度与成分依赖性及其与剪切模量的关系，发现UPFEs对温度的敏感性低于层错能，并提出通过成分加权元素剪切模量与伯氏矢量乘积（μbp）来估算UPFEs的简便方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定层错能（UPFEs）控制滑移与孪生行为，但无法通过实验直接测量，因此需要建立理论关联并寻找计算简便的估算途径，以理解和预测材料（尤其是多元合金）的变形机制。

Method: 采用嵌入原子势（EAM）并辅以密度泛函理论（DFT），系统研究UPFEs的温度和成分依赖性，以及其与剪切模量μ和伯氏矢量bp乘积（μbp）之间的关系；并针对Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni体系的二元及多元合金分析成分加权μbp的线性关联。

Result: 低层错能（SFEs）的纯fcc元素中，UPFEs对温度的敏感性远低于SFEs；UPFEs与μbp的线性关系对密排元素和固溶体成立，但对金属间化合物和非密排元素出现偏差；Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni体系中二元合金表现出成分相关的偏差，化学复杂性增加时相关性增强；多元合金的μbp与成分加权的元素μbp呈线性相关（R²=0.95），尽管存在系统性负偏差。

Conclusion: 利用成分加权的元素μbp可在无需昂贵计算的情况下直接估算多元合金的μbp并筛选UPFEs，为通过纯元素组元快速评估合金的滑移/孪生倾向提供了简单而有效的路径。

Abstract: The unstable planar fault energies (UPFEs) govern slip and twinning but are not experimentally measurable. Using EAM potentials complemented by DFT, we investigate their temperature and compositional dependence and their relationship with the shear modulus. For pure \textit{fcc} elements with low stacking fault energies (SFEs), UPFEs are substantially less temperature-sensitive than SFEs. The reported linear relationship between UPFEs and the product of shear modulus and Burgers vector ($μb_{\mathrm{p}}$) holds for close-packed elements and solid solutions, but deviates for intermetallics and non-close-packed elements. In the Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni system, binary alloys show composition-dependent deviations, while the correlation strengthens with increasing chemical complexity. Furthermore, $μb_{\mathrm{p}}$ of multicomponent alloys linearly correlates with the composition-weighted elemental $μb_{\mathrm{p}}$ ($R^2=0.95$), despite a systematic negative deviation. This provides a simple, computationally inexpensive route for estimating $μb_{\mathrm{p}}$ and screening UPFEs directly from pure elemental constituents.

</details>


### [159] [Spin-Polarized Weyl Fermions and Unconventional Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39604)
*Chenhao Liang,Haohao Sheng,Sheng Zhang,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Zhijun Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在二维非磁体系中提出并建立了“临界自旋外尔费米子”的通用框架，预言α-Bi类第五主族褶皱层状二维铁电材料（如BiAs）可作为同时承载临界自旋外尔费米子、巨自旋劈裂及电可调自旋输运的材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非磁材料中实现大且可电控的自旋劈裂，对凝聚态物理与自旋电子学具有重要价值，有助于获得强自旋极化准粒子、非常规自旋输运及其电学调控；但此前缺少一个系统的非磁框架及具体材料平台。

Method: 作者建立临界自旋极化外尔费米子的通用理论框架，并采用第一性原理计算研究α-Bi类第五主族褶皱层二维铁电家族（以BiAs单层为例），分析自旋轨道耦合诱导的自旋劈裂、自旋织构、自旋外尔点以及铁电极化反转、组分、应变和磁场对输运性质的调控。

Result: 计算表明BiAs单层中自旋轨道耦合诱导出交换量级（exchange-scale）的自旋劈裂和p波自旋织构；BiAs具有四个临界自旋外尔点，每个点形成于单一自旋通道内；近量子化的⟨S_z⟩由低能极限下涌现的S_z守恒对称性保护。自旋外尔锥产生非常规自旋输运，包括纯面外Edelstein响应和显著的自旋Nernst效应。铁电极化反转可实现p波自旋织构及奇宇称响应的电学切换；组分、应变和磁场可系统调节这些性质。

Conclusion: 该工作既为非磁材料中临界自旋外尔物理、非线性霍尔现象与非常规自旋输运提供了通用理论框架，也确立了一个有前景的材料实现平台。

Abstract: Large and electrically controllable spin splitting in nonmagnetic materials is highly desirable for condensed-matter physics and spintronics, enabling strongly spin-polarized quasiparticles and unconventional spin transport as well as their electrical control. Here, we establish a general framework for critical spin-polarized Weyl fermions in two-dimensional (2D) nonmagnetic systems. We predict the $α$-Bi-like Group-V puckered-layer family of 2D ferroelectrics as a material platform combining critical spin-Weyl fermions, giant spin splitting, and electrically switchable nonlinear and spin transport. Our calculations reveal exchange-scale spin splitting induced by spin-orbit coupling and a $p$-wave spin texture in the BiAs monolayer. Moreover, BiAs hosts four critical spin-Weyl points, each formed within a single-spin channel. The nearly quantized $\langle S_z\rangle$ is protected by an emergent $S_z$-conservation symmetry in the low-energy limit. The spin-Weyl cones also give rise to unconventional spin-transport phenomena, including a purely out-of-plane Edelstein response and a pronounced spin Nernst effect. Ferroelectric reversal further enables electrical switching of the $p$-wave spin texture and inversion-odd responses, while the tunability of these properties through composition, strain, and magnetic field is also systematically demonstrated across the entire family. These results establish both a general nonmagnetic framework and a promising material platform for exploring critical spin-Weyl physics, nonlinear Hall phenomena, and unconventional spin transport.

</details>


### [160] [Ultrasound-Assisted Facile Nanoconfinement of Luminescent Guests in Zirconium Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39622)
*Yiyi Wang,James D. Taylor,Jin-Chong Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加速超声化学法，利用探针超声（而非传统浴式超声）在锆基金属有机框架（UiO-66、UiO-67）多孔主体中纳米限域发光分子，快速制备发光客体@MOF（LG@MOF）复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统溶剂热法合成UiO及负载发光客体通常需24小时，耗时长、效率低；需开发更快、更简便的方法在保持晶体结构的同时实现客体原位封装，以满足固态照明、光学传感和纳米光子学的需求。

Method: 采用探针超声化学法：10分钟内合成结晶UiO-66和UiO-67纳米晶体（产率最高92%），1小时内原位封装发光客体；以UiO-66和大孔径UiO-67为多孔主体，构建含罗丹明B（RB）和荧光素（F）的单、双客体荧光主客体系，并进行光物理表征及薄膜制备。

Result: 获得宽带发射、颜色可调、高量子产率且寿命改善的LG@UiO复合材料；超声条件、结构缺陷与光物理行为之间存在协同作用；颗粒呈现清晰晶面，可制成光活性薄膜用于固态照明。

Conclusion: 该加速超声化学法可高效制备可调谐的LG@MOF复合材料，有望作为光学传感与纳米光子的可调光源，且超声法制备的粒子适于光活性薄膜应用。

Abstract: An accelerated sonochemical route for fabricating luminescent guest-MOF (LG@MOF) composites is demonstrated, for which light-emitting molecules are nanoconfined within porous zirconium-based hosts using probe ultrasonication rather than conventional bath sonication. Ultrasonication enables the rapid synthesis of crystalline UiO-66 and UiO-67 nanocrystals within 10 minutes, with material yields of up to 92%, and facile in situ guest encapsulation within 1 hour, hence notably faster than the traditional 24-hour solvothermal route. Using UiO-66 and the larger-pore UiO-67 as porous hosts, tuneable single- and dual-guest fluorescent host-guest systems incorporating Rhodamine B (RB) and Fluorescein (F) were achieved. Photophysical characterisation showed broadband emission, tuneable colours, high quantum yield and improved lifetime, highlighting the synergy between sonochemical conditions, structural defects, and photophysical behaviour. Additionally, the sonochemically prepared LG@UiO particles show well-defined crystal facets and potential as photoactive thin films for solid-state lighting, demonstrating their promise as a tuneable light source for optical sensing and nanophotonics.

</details>


### [161] [Origin and photoluminescence of a shallow hydrogen-related donor in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39623)
*Miaomiao Han,Peter Deak,Bing Huang,Adam Gali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用高精度密度泛函理论计算，研究氢与硅中自间隙复合体 Iₙ 的相互作用，证明氢以键心间隙位插入 Iₙ 可形成浅有效质量理论施主（SHD），其中 I₄H_BC 复合体的电子、自旋及光致发光性质与实验观测的 MT2 中心高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中顺磁性色心在量子技术中备受关注，但大多数与氢相关的光致发光谱线（如浅氢相关施主 SHD）的原子结构仍不明确。近期实验提示 SHD 与 X 线和 Bᵢ（Xᵢ）谱线存在联系，其中 MT2 中心被初步归因于氢与自间隙复合体 I₄ 相互作用形成的双稳态负 U 缺陷，作者旨在从理论上阐明其原子与电子结构。

Method: 采用高精度密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究氢与硅自间隙复合体 Iₙ 的相互作用；考察氢攻击 Iₙ 中 Si-Si 键并以键心间隙位（IₙH_BC）插入的放热反应过程，具体分析单个自间隙（I₁）及四自间隙复合体（I₄）两种情形，并解析其电子结构、自旋、稳定性及光致发光性质。

Result: 氢插入 Iₙ 键心位形成浅有效质量理论施主是放热过程；I₄H_BC 复合体的电子、自旋及 PL 性质与 MT2 极为接近。氢在 I₄ 存在下与在纯净硅中行为一致，为负 U 双稳态缺陷，仅光照下可探测。在 I₄H_BC 中，氢可通过光辅助机制在 I₄ 的等价键心位间跳跃，导致运动平均化的四方对称性，与 MT2 的实验观测一致。其零声子线不同于归属裸 I₄ 复合体的 X 线，且落在 Xᵢ 线范围之内。

Conclusion: I₄H 复合体可视为一类浅氢相关施主的原型；当氢攻击 Iₙ 团簇时，可能产生类似的 SHD，且 Xᵢ 线是其 PL 指纹。该工作为理解硅中氢相关浅施主的原子结构及其光致发光起源提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Paramagnetic color centers in silicon are being investigated recently for use in quantum technologies. While the origin of most of the prominent photoluminescence (PL) lines are well understood, that is not the case for those connected to hydrogen, even though it is almost always present in silicon, e.g., shallow hydrogen-related donors (SHDs), but their atomic structure remains elusive. Recent experiments suggested connection between the SHDs and the so-called X and the B$_i$ (or X$_i$) PL-lines. An SHD, termed the MT2 center, was tentatively assigned to a bistable, negative-U defect, arising due to the interaction of hydrogen with the self-interstitial complex I$_4$. Using high-level density functional theory calculations, we investigate the interaction of hydrogen with self-interstitial complexes In. A shallow effective-mass-theory donor arises when hydrogen attacks a Si-Si bond in I$_n$, inserting itself as a bond-center interstitial (I$_n$H$_{BC}$) in an exothermic reaction. We demonstrate this in the case of a single self-interstitial and of a complex of four. The latter I$_\mathrm{4}$H$_\mathrm{BC}$ complex shows electronic, spin and PL properties remarkably close to MT2. We find that hydrogen in the presence of I$_4$ follows the same behavior as in pristine silicon, i.e., it is a negative-U bistable defect, only detectable under illumination. In the I$_4$H$_{BC}$ complex, H can hop via photo-assisted mechanism between equivalent BC sites of the I$_4$ complex, leading to a motional averaged tetragonal symmetry as experimentally observed for MT2. The zero-phonon line is distinct from the X-line (attributed to the bare I$_4$ complex), and falls well into the range of the X$_i$ lines. Our results identify the I$_4$H complex as a prototype for a class of SHDs, inferring that similar SHDs may arise, with the X$_i$ lines as their PL fingerprints, when H attacks I$_n$ clusters.

</details>


### [162] [Excitons in solid molecular hydrogen from quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39894)
*Edgar Josué Landinez Borda,Ravindra Shinde,Claudia Filippi,Markus Holzmann,Saverio Moroni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法研究压缩过程中相I固态分子氢的最低中性激发，重点考察激子的结构与响应性质，并与Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）和时间依赖密度泛函理论（TDDFT）结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态分子氢在压缩过程中的最低中性激发（激子）性质对理解其电子结构及金属化（绝缘体-金属转变）过程至关重要。现有基于BSE和TDDFT的近似方法在描述激子局域化和多体效应上存在局限，亟需用更高精度的QMC方法进行基准检验，并厘清激发态波函数多组态展开对局域化与响应性质的影响。

Method: 采用QMC方法，构建两种激发态试探波函数：仅含单轨道替换及包含所有单粒子-空穴跃迁的多行列式展开；并考察代表热涨落和量子涨落的多种核构型。结果与BSE和TDDFT比较，分析量包括激发能、激子局域化、跃迁偶极矩以及激发诱导力，同时检验波函数完全优化与虚时投影的作用。

Result: 多行列式展开对QMC激发能影响甚微，但对激子局域化及相关量至关重要，且与是否完全优化和虚时投影无关。在低密度强绝缘区，多行列式QMC的局域化程度与BSE相当，部分情况下更优；跃迁偶极矩与激发诱导力总体与BSE吻合良好，但在局域化差异显著的核构型上存在明显分歧。TDDFT给出的激子更扩展、跃迁偶极矩更弱。随压缩加剧、体系接近半导体区，多粒子-空穴跃迁重要性下降，各方法间差异缩小。

Conclusion: 多行列式展开对准确描述固态氢激子局域化必不可少，QMC与BSE在强绝缘区总体一致，而TDDFT倾向于低估局域化。这一方法学差异随压缩至半导体区而减弱，说明多体激子效应在低密度绝缘区尤为关键。

Abstract: We use quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods to investigate the lowest neutral excitation of solid molecular hydrogen in phase-I under increasing compression, with emphasis on structural and response properties. We employ QMC wave functions for the excited state that feature only a single orbital substitution, or a multideterminant expansion including every single particle-hole transition, and compare the results with the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) and time-dependent density-functional theory (TDDFT). We examine a number of nuclear configurations representing thermal and quantum fluctuations. The effect of the multideterminant expansion is marginal on the QMC excitation energies but crucial on the exciton localization and related quantities, irrespective of full optimization and imaginary time projection. In the strong insulating regime at low density, multideterminant QMC results match and in some cases surpass the degree of localization afforded by BSE. A similar trend is observed for transition dipole moments and excitation-induced forces, with generally good agreement between QMC and BSE but marked discrepancies for nuclear configurations where localization differs significantly. TDDFT generally gives a more extended exciton with correspondingly weaker transition dipole moments. These differences decrease upon compression, as the system approaches a semiconducting regime where multiple particle-hole transitions are less important.

</details>


### [163] [Observation of light-induced topological state in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39695)
*Sotirios Fragkos,Ann Chantal Goutier,Umberto Dellasette,Dominique Descamps,Stéphane Petit,Wiebke Bennecke,Philip Hofmann,Marcel Reutzel,Stefan Mathias,Yann Mairesse,Michael Schüler,Samuel Beaulieu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合圆二色时间分辨极紫外动量显微术与从头算理论，研究圆偏振光驱动的石墨烯，观测到狄拉克点附近泵浦诱导的光电子圆二色性随泵浦螺旋性和狄拉克能量反转，从而为石墨烯中Floquet工程拓扑绝缘态提供了谱学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的输运与全光学测量虽已证明光诱导的反常霍尔响应，但无法在能量和动量空间同时分辨底层电子态；时间/角分辨光电子能谱虽观察到Floquet-Bloch边带和杂化带隙，却因使用线偏振驱动而保持时间反演对称性，未能诱导拓扑相。因此需要在能量-动量空间对圆偏振光驱动的石墨烯拓扑态进行直接谱学验证。

Method: 采用圆二色时间分辨极紫外（XUV）动量显微术，结合从头算理论计算，对圆偏振光驱动的石墨烯进行测量，重点分析狄拉克点附近的泵浦诱导光电子圆二色性。

Result: 在狄拉克点附近观测到泵浦诱导的光电子圆二色性，其符号随泵浦螺旋性（左手/右手圆偏振）及狄拉克能量上下方发生反转，与理论计算一致。该二色响应被关联到光诱导的赝自旋织构及其贝里曲率。

Conclusion: 实验结果将测得的二色响应与光诱导赝自旋织构及贝里曲率联系起来，为石墨烯中Floquet工程的拓扑绝缘态提供了谱学证据。

Abstract: Driving quantum materials with light can reshape not only their electronic band structures but also the geometry of their electronic wavefunctions. In graphene, circularly polarized light is predicted to open gaps at the Dirac points and induce a pseudospin texture and Berry curvature characteristic of the Haldane model, realizing a Floquet Chern insulator. Transport and all-optical measurements have provided evidence for a light-induced anomalous Hall response, but cannot resolve the underlying electronic states in both energy and momentum space. Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy has revealed Floquet-Bloch sidebands and hybridization gaps at crossings between Floquet bands. However, these experiments used linearly polarized driving, which preserves time-reversal symmetry and thus does not induce a topological phase. Here, we combine circular-dichroic time-resolved extreme-ultraviolet momentum microscopy with ab initio theory to investigate graphene driven by circularly polarized light. We observe a pump-induced photoemission circular dichroism near the Dirac point that reverses sign with pump helicity and across the Dirac energy, in agreement with our calculations. These results link the measured dichroic response to the light-induced pseudospin texture and its associated Berry curvature, providing spectroscopic evidence for the Floquet-engineered topological insulating state in graphene.

</details>


### [164] [Pulse Laser Deposited GaFeO3 Thin Film Grown on FTO: Correlated Ferroic Orders and Strong Magnetodielectric Coupling at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39696)
*Ishita Jana,Sourav Sarkar,Soham Saha,Kalyan Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在FTO玻璃衬底上通过脉冲激光沉积生长的约100.6 nm厚多晶正交GaFeO3薄膜，该薄膜在室温下同时兼具铁磁性与铁电性，并展现出强磁电耦合，是室温多功能器件的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温单相多铁材料兼具铁电序与磁序，是下一代磁电存储与自旋电子技术的有力候选，但实现室温下单相内铁电与磁性共存及其强耦合仍具挑战。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积在氟掺杂氧化锡镀膜玻璃（FTO）衬底上生长约100.6 nm厚的多晶正交GaFeO3薄膜；利用X射线光电子能谱表征化学态，结合铁磁/铁电测试、压电力显微镜（PFM）表征局域极化翻转，并测量介电与磁介电性能。

Result: 薄膜呈现室温铁磁性与铁电性共存；XPS显示Fe2+浓度降低、氧空位被抑制，漏电流减小、铁电性改善；PFM显示可逆局域极化翻转及不对称蝴蝶曲线，矫顽电压为-0.31 V和+1.07 V；1 MHz下相对介电常数约1700，介电损耗正切约2.83；在仅0.1 kOe的小磁场下磁介电系数约2.3%，表明磁电耦合较强。

Conclusion: GaFeO3薄膜在室温下实现单相铁电与铁磁共存并具有强磁电耦合，是低场磁电存储与自旋电子架构等室温多功能器件的有前景平台。

Abstract: Room-temperature single-phase multiferroics exhibiting coupled ferroelectric and magnetic order are promising candidates for next-generation magnetoelectric memory and spintronic technologies. Here, we report the growth of approximately 100.6 nm thick polycrystalline orthorhombic GaFeO3 thin films on fluorine-doped tin oxide-coated glass (FTO) substrates by pulsed laser deposition. Core-level X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals a reduced Fe2+ concentration and suppressed oxygen-vacancy formation, contributing to reduced leakage current and improved ferroelectric characteristics. The films exhibit robust ferrimagnetism and ferroelectricity at room temperature, confirming the coexistence of ferroic orders within a single phase. Piezoresponse Force Microscopy further demonstrates reversible local polarization switching, accompanied by an asymmetric butterfly loop with coercive voltages of -0.31 V and +1.07 V. The films also exhibit a high relative dielectric constant of approximately 1700 with a relatively low dielectric loss tangent of approximately 2.83 at 1 MHz. Moreover, a significant magnetodielectric coefficient of approximately 2.3% is observed under a relatively small magnetic field of only 0.1 kOe, highlighting strong magnetoelectric coupling. These results establish GaFeO3 thin films as a promising platform for room-temperature multifunctional devices, mainly low-field magnetoelectric memory and spintronic architectures.

</details>


### [165] [I$_4$ complex acting as near-infrared emitter with a quantum memory in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39713)
*Miaomiao Han,Song Li,Gergő Thiering,Thomas Frauenheim,Peter Deák,Bing Huang,Adam Gali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用HSE06杂化泛函密度泛函理论，作者确认了硅中1039.7 meV处X发光谱线的起源为四自间隙原子（I₄）复合体，并证明其存在可作自旋量子存储的近红外亚稳态自旋三重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基近红外色心是实现与成熟CMOS工艺兼容的自旋-光子接口的候选方案，但X线（1039.7 meV）的微观结构与自旋性质长期存在争议，缺乏预测层面的表征，亟需从理论层面确定其结构、发光与自旋物性。

Method: 采用HSE06杂化泛函密度泛函理论（DFT）对裸I₄复合体进行计算，定量复现其零声子线、完整声子边带结构及EPR超精细常数；计算电荷转变能级并分析缺陷的D2d对称性对自旋-轨道耦合与系间窜越的抑制作用。

Result: 计算结果定量复现零声子线、声子边带（确认次级峰为强耦合准局域模的一、二声子复制）及EPR超精细常数，从而确立裸I₄复合体为X线的起源；电荷转变能级位于VBM+0.08 eV，排除了此前将其关联到B3 EPR中心及其深施主能级的说法；发现一个亚稳态自旋-1三重激发态，与单重态相隔约1.6 meV，与NL51 EPR信号一致；D2d对称性抑制一阶自旋-轨道耦合导致的系间窜越与磷光，预示着极长的自旋-晶格弛豫时间。

Conclusion: I₄复合体的三重态兼具可分辨的零场分裂、近红外发射与极长自旋-晶格弛豫时间，构成硅中本征可行的自旋量子存储。

Abstract: Silicon color centers emitting in the near infrared offer a route to spin--photon interfaces compatible with mature complementary metal--oxide--semiconductor fabrication. A key unresolved candidate is the X-line at 1039.7 meV in the photoluminescence (PL) spectrum, long attributed to a complex of four self-interstitials (I$_4$), yet whose microscopic structure and spin properties have remained controversial and uncharacterized at a predictive level. Using hybrid-functional (HSE06) density functional theory, we unambiguously establish the bare I$_4$ complex as the origin of the X-line: our calculations quantitatively reproduce the zero-phonon line, the complete phonon sideband structure---identifying the prominent secondary peaks as one- and two-phonon replicas of strongly coupled quasi-local modes---and the EPR hyperfine constants. The charge transition level is found at VBM$+$0.08~eV, conclusively ruling out the previously proposed association with the B3 EPR center and its correlated deep donor level. Most significantly, we identify a metastable spin-1 triplet excited state separated from the singlet by $\sim$1.6~meV, consistent with the NL51 EPR signal. The $D_{2d}$ symmetry of the defect suppresses intersystem crossing and phosphorescence by first-order spin--orbit coupling, implying an exceptionally long spin--lattice relaxation time. Combined with a spectrally resolvable zero-field splitting and near-infrared emission, the triplet state of the I$_4$ complex constitutes a viable spin quantum memory intrinsic to silicon.

</details>


### [166] [Direct Observation of Electron Trapping States in SiO$_2$ by Constant-Final-State Photoelectron Yield Spectroscopy: Insights into the Electron-Transfer Model of Contact Electrification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39748)
*Renjiro Shibuya,Manato Tateno,Runa Hoshikawa,Tomohiro Ishibashi,Hirohiko Fukagawa,Hisao Ishii*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用恒终态光电子产额谱（CFS-YS）首次直接、定量测定了非晶SiO₂的带隙内态密度（DOS），发现电子陷阱态分布于宽能量范围，并揭示表面吸附物（如水）对带电行为的影响；基于实测DOS的充放电模拟近似再现了已有带电行为，同时表明累积电荷集中于近表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触起电（CE）是SiO₂带电的主要来源，电子转移被认为是其主导机制之一，但参与电子转移的态密度（DOS）一直缺乏实验表征。由于绝缘材料在光电子能谱测量中会因样品充电导致谱形畸变甚至信号丢失，标准光电子能谱难以测量SiO₂的DOS，因此迫切需要一种能直接、定量测定绝缘体带隙内DOS的方法，以理解其带电机制。

Method: 采用恒终态光电子产额谱（CFS-YS）技术，直接定量测量非晶SiO₂的带隙内DOS；通过电子注入实验观察占据态的变化以确认电子陷阱态；结合真空退火处理改变带隙内DOS与电子陷阱行为；再基于实验测得的带隙内DOS进行充放电模拟，并与文献报道的带电行为对比。

Result: 在带隙内观测到弱占据态，电子注入后这些态显著增加，表明电子陷阱态分布于宽能量范围。真空退火显著改变了带隙内DOS及电子陷阱行为，提示包括水在内的表面吸附物在SiO₂带电行为中起作用。基于实测DOS的充放电模拟近似再现了已有的带电行为，并显示累积电荷集中于近表面。

Conclusion: 分布于宽能量范围的带隙内态介导了接触起电中的电子转移；在评估各种电子转移模型时，应重视实验测定的DOS，而非仅依赖理论假设。

Abstract: Understanding electrostatic charging of silicon dioxide (SiO$_2$) is important for electrostatic control in a wide range of applications. Contact electrification (CE) is a major source of such charging, and electron transfer is considered one of its leading mechanisms. Several electron-transfer models have been proposed to explain CE; however, the density of states (DOS) involved in electron transfer has not been experimentally characterized. Determining this DOS is therefore essential for understanding the charging mechanism. However, measuring the DOS of insulating materials such as SiO$_2$ remains challenging: photoemission spectroscopy, the standard technique for probing the DOS of materials, is unsuitable for highly insulating materials because sample charging during measurement can cause spectral distortion or even complete signal loss. Here, we directly and quantitatively determined the in-gap DOS of amorphous SiO$_2$ using constant-final-state photoelectron yield spectroscopy (CFS-YS). Weak occupied states were observed within the band gap, and their marked increase after electron injection revealed that electron-trapping states are distributed over a broad energy range. Vacuum annealing markedly altered the in-gap DOS and electron-trapping behavior. The observed changes suggest a role for surface adsorbates, including water, in the charging behavior of SiO$_2$. Charging simulations based on the experimentally measured in-gap DOS approximately reproduced the previously reported charging behavior and showed that the accumulated charge was concentrated near the surface. These findings indicate that in-gap states distributed over a broad energy range mediate electron transfer during CE and highlight the importance of considering experimentally determined DOS when evaluating proposed electron-transfer models.

</details>


### [167] [Sign reversal of ST-FMR signals in CoFeB/Pt explained by contributions beyond spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39749)
*Christian Berg,Ao Du,Durgesh Kumar,Sinan Shu,Sourabh Manna,Sachin Krishnia,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示传统ST-FMR电压测量中混入的非转矩寄生贡献会导致自旋轨道矩效率被错误提取，甚至出现符号反转。作者通过互补测量几何与分析方法分离出真实转矩信号，可靠测定了CoFeB/Pt体系随Pt厚度变化的阻尼类转矩效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ST-FMR是量化自旋轨道矩效率的常用手段，但整流电压中可能包含与SOT无直接关系的额外贡献，影响效率提取的准确性，这也是文献中SOT效率报道差异巨大的潜在原因之一。

Method: 以CoFeB/Pt异质结为对象，系统改变Pt厚度，采用传统ST-FMR分析并结合互补的测量几何（如不同磁场/电流取向配置）与多种分析方法，分离出与转矩相关的信号及具有相同角度依赖关系的额外电压贡献。

Result: 传统ST-FMR分析在Pt厚度约10 nm处给出效率的"表观符号反转"；非转矩电压贡献可产生这种假象。通过分离寄生贡献后，能够可靠地确定真实的阻尼类转矩效率。

Conclusion: ST-FMR测量中必须区分SOT诱导信号与寄生电压贡献，否则会得到错误的效率乃至虚假的符号反转；该工作给出了可靠测定SOT效率的方法。

Abstract: Spin-orbit torques (SOT) provide an efficient route for electrical control of magnetization and is of central interest for low-power magnetic memory applications. Spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance (ST-FMR) is a widely used technique to quantify SOT efficiencies. However, the rectified voltage measured in ST-FMR can contain contributions that are not directly related to SOTs, which affects the accuracy of the extracted torque efficiencies. This may contribute to the large variation of reported SOT efficiencies in literature. In this study, we investigate the damping-like torque efficiencies in CoFeB/Pt heterostructures as a function of Pt thickness. Conventional ST-FMR analysis yields an apparent sign reversal of the extracted efficiency at a Pt thickness of approximately 10 nm. By combining complementary measurement geometries and analysis approaches, we separate the torque-induced signal from additional voltage contributions that share the same angular dependence. Our results show that non-torque voltage contributions can produce an apparent sign reversal in conventional ST-FMR analysis, highlighting the need to distinguish SOT-induced signals from parasitic contributions and we show how to reliably determine the SOT efficiencies.

</details>


### [168] [Growth and morphology of core-shell nanowires incorporating dissimilar hexagonal semiconductors: the case of InAs-ZnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39770)
*Danylo Mosiiets,Susheng Tan,Sergey Frolov,Moïra Hocevar,Joel Cibert,Edith Bellet-Amalric*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过分子束外延在超高真空连通的双腔室中生长InAs(核)/ZnTe(壳)核壳纳米线，并用电镜研究了其结构与生长行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索III-V族核与II-VI族壳结合的同轴核壳纳米线的外延生长可行性，研究跨族异质结构的结构与生长动力学。

Method: 采用分子束外延(MBE)在两个超高真空连通的专用腔室中分别生长III-V族InAs核和II-VI族ZnTe壳，并利用电子显微镜(EM)进行结构表征。

Result: 壳层生长时，InAs核的纤锌矿结构被无缺陷地复刻到ZnTe壳层中；壳层厚度可从约1个单层到25 nm，得益于InAs与ZnTe良好的晶格匹配。核侧壁上(1-100)m面和(11-20)a面共存，壳层生长过程中m面逐渐增大而a面最终消失。ZnTe壳最初几个单层生长较快且粘附系数高，随后生长变慢，其速率与ZnTe核相当，且依赖纳米线半径，表明为单核成核机制。

Conclusion: III-V/II-VI跨族核壳纳米线可在晶格匹配条件下实现高质量、无缺陷的同结构外延，壳层生长受晶面演化和单核成核动力学共同控制。

Abstract: Core-shell nanowires incorporating semiconductors from two groups, III-V for the core and II-VI for the shell, have been grown by molecular beam epitaxy in two dedicated chambers connected under ultra-high vacuum, and studied by electron microscopy. During the growth of the shell, the wurtzite structure of the core is replicated in the shell, without any creation of defects. This is true for a wide range of shell thickness, from about one monolayer, up to 25 nm thanks to the good lattice match between the InAs material of the core and the ZnTe material of the shell. While (1 100) m-facets and (11 20) a-facets coexist on the sidewalls of the core, during the growth of the ZnTe shell the size of the m-facets progressively increases at the expense of the a-facets which eventually disappear. A fast growth of the ZnTe shell is observed for the first few monolayers, with a high sticking; this is followed by a slower growth, of the same order of magnitude as observed on a ZnTe core, with a dependence on the nanowire radius which points to the occurrence of mononuclear nucleation

</details>


### [169] [Epitaxial Magnon Junctions with Y3Fe5O12/Gd3Ga5O12/Pt-nanoparticles/Y3Fe5O12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39841)
*Jianing Lin,Haoyu Zhuang,Tianyi Zhang,Jiyang Ou,Jihao Xia,Hongyu Wang,Jiang Niu,Qinglong Yu,Caihua Wan,Guoqiang Yu,Richeng Yu,Xiufeng Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种外延磁振子结，采用Gd3Ga5O12（GGG）/Pt纳米颗粒混合间隔层，在保持相干外延的同时实现反平行磁构型，获得了43%的磁振子结比（MJR），并发现两层亚铁磁绝缘体之间的层间耦合可增强MJR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量FI/AFI/FI磁振子结理论上可实现近100%的磁振子结比，是高性能非易失存储器的候选器件。此前磁振子结中晶格失配的AFI间隔层会破坏两层YIG之间的外延关系，虽带来不同矫顽力，却劣化了顶层YIG的晶体质量，限制了磁振子传输效率。

Method: 提出并制备了外延磁振子结，采用GGG/Pt纳米颗粒混合间隔层替代传统AFI间隔层，保持与YIG层的相干外延，同时通过调节顶层YIG的矫顽力实现反平行磁构型；并与基于氧化镍的磁振子结进行对比表征。

Result: 该结构中的顶层YIG层相比氧化镍基磁振子结具有更优的晶体质量和更低的磁阻尼，观察到清晰的磁振子结效应，实现了43%的MJR；且两层亚铁磁绝缘体之间的层间耦合被发现能增强MJR。

Conclusion: 所提出的外延磁振子结在保持外延质量和实现反平行磁构型方面取得平衡，为发展先进的基于磁振子的自旋电子学器件提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: The excitation, propagation, manipulation, and detection of magnons lie at the core of magnonics. Among various device architectures, high-quality magnon junctions with a ferromagnetic insulator (FI)/antiferromagnetic insulator (AFI)/ferromagnetic insulator (FI) configuration are predicted to exhibit a near-100% magnon junction ratio (MJR), making them promising candidates for high-performance non-volatile memory applications. Yttrium iron garnet (Y3Fe5O12, YIG) is widely employed as the FI material in such structures for its exceptionally low magnetic damping. However, in previously reported magnon junctions, the epitaxial relationship between the two YIG layers is often disrupted by lattice-mismatched AFI spacers. Although this leads to distinct coercivities, it simultaneously degrades the crystalline quality of the top YIG layer, thereby limiting the magnontransmission efficiency. Here, an epitaxial magnon junction is demonstrated incorporating a hybrid Gd3Ga5O12 (GGG)/Pt-nanoparticles spacer that maintains coherent epitaxy while enabling an antiparallel magnetic configuration. Compared with nickel oxide-based magnon junctions, the coercivity-tuned top YIG layer in our structure exhibits superior crystalline quality and much lower magnetic damping. A clear magnon junction effect is observed with a 43% MJR achieved. The interlayer coupling between the two ferrimagnetic insulators is found to enhance the MJR. These results offer valuable insights for the development of advanced magnon-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [170] [Ammonium thiocyanate enables in-situ structural templating of MAPbI3 via quasi-2D perovskite scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39887)
*Sophie Tucker,Isaac Metcalf,Rachel C. Kilbride,Elena J. Cassella,Jessica H. Persaud,Faiz Mandani,Niranjana Raj,Max Allen,Charlie H. G. Nicholls,David G. Lidzey,Alice L. B. Pyne,Robert D. J. Oliver,Silvia Motti,Jacky Even,Aditya D. Mohite,Alexandra J. Ramadan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了硫氰酸铵（NH4SCN）提升准二维钙钛矿太阳能电池性能的真正机制：它通过动力学调控准二维相的成核，使高 n 值的二维骨架相原位模板化生长出高度(110)取向的 3D MAPbI3，从而改善 3D 钙钛矿结构，而非此前认为的电荷沿垂直取向二维层传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
