Motivation: NH4SCN 虽被广泛用于改善准二维钙钛矿太阳能电池的电流提取与器件性能，且已知它能影响准二维钙钛矿的结晶，但其确切作用机制尚未被清楚阐明。此外，此前报道普遍将其益处归因于电荷通过垂直取向的二维钙钛矿层传输，这一解释缺乏充分依据，亟需厘清。

Method: 作者采用光谱学与结构学相结合的研究手段，深入分析 NH4SCN 对准二维钙钛矿结晶的影响。具体包括：探究 NH4SCN 对准二维相形成的动力学调控作用（如抑制不利的 n=1 相、促进高 n 值二维骨架相生长、延长成核窗口），并通过结构分析证明该机制依赖于准二维相与 3D 相之间紧密的晶格匹配，同时验证了这种结构调控具有阳离子依赖性。

Result: 研究表明，NH4SCN 的作用并非通过垂直取向二维层传输电荷，而是通过改善 3D 钙钛矿结构实现。该添加剂提供动力学调控，抑制不利的 n=1 钙钛矿相，并延长成核窗口以促进高 n 值二维骨架相生长，进而原位模板化生长 MAPbI3。结构分析证实所得 3D 钙钛矿高度(110)取向，且该机制由准二维相与 3D 相之间的紧密晶格匹配所确认。此外，这种结构调控具有阳离子依赖性，要求准二维与 3D 钙钛矿相晶格匹配良好，并能成功实现相关准二维相的动力学调控。

Conclusion: NH4SCN 改善器件性能的机制在于对准二维相形成的动力学控制，通过高 n 二维骨架相原位模板化生长出高度(110)取向、结构更优的 3D MAPbI3。这种模板化机制依赖于准二维相与 3D 相之间的晶格匹配，且具有阳离子依赖性。该结论修正了此前关于垂直取向二维层电荷传输的观点。

Abstract: Ammonium thiocyanate (NH4SCN) has been used extensively to improve the current extraction and device performance of quasi-two-dimensional perovskite solar cells. Whilst this additive has been shown to influence quasi-2D perovskite crystallisation, a detailed understanding of its mechanism has not yet been established. Through spectroscopic and structural studies, we demonstrate that NH4SCN enables quasi-2D perovskite phases to structurally template the dominant 3D phase. Contrary to previous reports, we suggest that the benefit of this additive comes from an improvement to the 3D perovskite structure, not from charges transferring through vertically oriented 2D perovskite layers. We show that this additive provides kinetic control over the quasi-2D phase formation, suppressing the unfavourable n = 1 perovskite phase and promoting the growth of high-n 2D scaffold phases by extending the nucleation window. This promotes the in-situ templated growth of MAPbI3. We provide robust structural analysis demonstrating highly (110)-ordered 3D perovskite, with the mechanism confirmed by close lattice matching between this phase and quasi-2D phases. Furthermore, we show that this structural control is cation dependent, requiring both close lattice matching between quasi-2D and 3D perovskite phases, and successful kinetic control of the relevant quasi-2D phases.

</details>


### [171] [Confinement Controlled Phase-Pure 2D Layered Perovskites for Band-Edge Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39974)
*Danila A. Tatarinov,Alexander Schleusener,Roman Krahne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以铯和丁基铵为A位阳离子的简单可放大合成方法，制备具有高n相纯度的二维溴化铅钙钛矿微晶粉末，并通过混合不同n相的纯粉末实现带隙与吸收光谱的精准调控，用于带边工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维金属卤化物钙钛矿中八面体层数n的调控可精确控制半导体带隙，对光电和发光应用极具价值；而n值的相纯度对窄发射带和电荷载流子漏斗化的能带结构排列至关重要，需要一种能制备高n相纯度材料的合成方法。

Method: 采用铯（Cs）和丁基铵（butylammonium）作为A位阳离子，通过简单且可放大的合成方法制备二维溴化铅钙钛矿微晶粉末；利用X射线衍射（XRD）和能量色散谱（EDS）确认结构与组成，通过紫外-蓝光区吸收陡峭起始和单一窄发射峰验证相纯度；将不同n相纯粉末可控混合并压制成片，进行光谱光电流测试。

Result: 所得微晶在紫外-蓝光区具有尖锐的吸收起始和单一窄发射峰，证实了高n相纯度；通过可控混合n相纯粉末可灵活调控样品的光谱吸收，基于纯粉末和混合粉末压片进行的光电流测试（包括光谱响应和动态响应）强烈依赖于粉末组成，展示了带边工程的能力。

Conclusion: 该合成方法简单、可放大，能制备高n相纯度的二维钙钛矿微晶粉末，并通过相纯粉末的混合实现带隙和吸收光谱的精准设计，为光电和发光应用中的能带结构排列与电荷载流子漏斗化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Controlling the number n of connected octahedra layers in two-dimensional metal halide perovskites enables fine control of the semiconductor band gap that is highly advantageous for optoelectronic and light emission applications. Phase purity in terms of n is crucial towards narrow emission bands and the design of band structure alignment for charge carrier funneling. We present a simple and scalable synthesis method using cesium and butylammonium as A-cations to fabricate microcrystal powders of 2D lead bromide perovskites that results in samples with very high n-phase purity. The obtained microcrystals show a sharp onset in absorbance and single narrow emission peak in UV-blue spectral range, and we confirm their structure and composition with X-ray diffraction and energy dispersive spectroscopy. We show that we can tailor the spectral absorption of microcrystals samples by controlled mixing of the n-phase pure powders, and we demonstrate this capability with spectral photocurrent measurements using pellets pressed from pure and mixed powders. Both the spectral and dynamic response of the photocurrent of the pellets depend strongly on the composition of the powders, highlighting the design potential of our approach towards band-edge engineering.

</details>


### [172] [Spin-Orbit Coupling Response Theory within the Oriented Spin Space Group Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40149)
*Yuntian Liu,Xiaobing Chen,Yutong Yu,Jesús Etxebarria,J. Manuel Perez-Mato,Qihang Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于取向自旋空间群（OSSG）的一般理论框架，通过将自旋轨道耦合（SOC）哈密顿量用连接实空间与自旋空间的SOC张量进行参数化并作微扰展开，系统描述SOC诱导的对称性破缺对物理性质的影响，确定任一SOC诱导响应的最低阶次及其磁各向异性依赖，并应用于II类多铁磁电耦合、反铁磁中的反常霍尔效应与自旋磁化，最后通过MAGNDATA高通量筛选给出材料候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道耦合在凝聚态物理众多现象中起关键作用，但如何系统刻画SOC诱导的对称性破缺及其对物理性质和新型物理效应的作用仍缺乏统一理论；新发展的自旋空间群理论提供了新视角，但其潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 采用取向自旋空间群（OSSG）形式明确描述SOC引起的对称性降低；将SOC哈密顿量用连接实空间与自旋空间的SOC张量参数化，并对描述任意物理量的响应张量按SOC分量作展开，从而判定SOC诱导响应的对称性容许性、出现的最低阶次及其磁各向异性依赖。将该框架应用于II类多铁磁电耦合、反铁磁反常霍尔效应与自旋磁化，并基于MAGNDATA数据库进行高通量分析。

Result: 发现不同系统中磁致电极化可源于不同阶次的SOC效应，并呈现不同的磁各向异性特征；反常霍尔响应与自旋磁化在OSSG下具有不同的变换性质，使大的反常霍尔响应可与弱自旋磁化共存；通过MAGNDATA高通量分析识别出若干有利于该共存的对称性材料类别。

Conclusion: 所提框架为SOC诱导现象提供了统一的对称性描述，并为识别SOC驱动的功能材料提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Spin-orbit coupling (SOC) plays a pivotal role in a broad range of phenomena in condensed matter physics. The recently developed spin space group theory offers a new perspective for characterizing the fundamental symmetry-breaking features of SOC-related phenomena, yet its full potential remains to be explored. Here, we provide a general framework that relates the SOC-induced symmetry breaking to modifications of physical properties and emergence of new physical effects. This approach incorporates the recently developed oriented spin space group (OSSG) formalism, which can explicitly describe the symmetry reduction induced by SOC. By parameterizing the SOC Hamiltonian using a SOC tensor that connects real and spin spaces, an expansion in terms of the SOC components can be constructed for the response tensor describing any physical quantity. This framework determines not only whether a SOC-induced response is symmetry allowed, but also the lowest order in SOC at which it emerges and its dependence on magnetic anisotropy. As representative applications, we analyze the magnetoelectric coupling in Type-II multiferroics and show that magnetically induced electric polarization in different systems can be the result of SOC effects of different orders, and can exhibit varying characteristics of magnetic anisotropy. We further apply this theory to the anomalous Hall effect and spin magnetization in antiferromagnets, demonstrating that their distinct transformation properties under the OSSG allow a large anomalous Hall response to coexist with weak spin magnetization. A high-throughput analysis of materials in the MAGNDATA database identifies several symmetry-based material classes favorable for this coexistence. Overall, the proposed framework provides a unified symmetry description of SOC-induced phenomena and offers a systematic way for the identification of SOC-driven functional materials.

</details>


### [173] [Cubic-Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te optical modes across the phonon and phonon-polariton regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40157)
*M. Mina,A. Elmahjoubi,T. Ngulezhu,S. Thiam,A. V. Postnikov,T. Tite,P. Bourges,F. Bourdarot,N. Biniskos,A. Polian,Y. Le Godec,N. Guignot,J. -P. Itie,S. Diliberto,S. Michel,A. Maillard,A. En Naciri,L. Broch,P. Franchetti,K. Strzalkowski,O. Pages*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过实验（前向拉曼散射与非弹性中子散射）结合从头计算，研究近晶格匹配的立方 Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te 合金在极小晶格/键畸变下的晶格振动，发现短 Zn-Te 键呈"1键2模"行为，支持逾渗模型优于传统的 MREI（修正随机元素等位移）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合金化是调控半导体物理性质的便捷途径，但代价是需容纳晶格失配带来的局域晶格/键畸变。立方 Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te 近乎晶格匹配且因强共价键而高度抗拒晶格与键畸变，因此成为检验晶格振动对合金化诱发的最小晶格/键畸变敏感性的理想基准体系。

Method: 实验上采用两种横向光学声子探测手段并辅以从头计算：(1) 前向拉曼散射探测准无限（类光子）波长下的声子-极化激元区，该区对合金无序做平均；(2) 非弹性中子散射探测纯力学声子区，波长下探至接近晶格常数的最小尺度，从而放大合金无序细节。

Result: 研究发现短 Zn-Te 键呈现紧凑的光学声子"二重态"，能区分同质与异质局域键环境（即"1键2模"），这可由逾渗模型解释。该结果偏离了源自经典拉曼与红外研究的历史性 MREI 模型，后者认为光学模对键环境"盲目"（即"1键1模"）。

Conclusion: 该检验结果为正面，强化了逾渗模型作为通过晶格动力学稳健解读合金化诱发（即使是微小的）晶格畸变的有力工具的地位。

Abstract: Alloying offers a convenient route to tune the physical properties of semiconductors, at the price, however, of local lattice/bond distortions needed to accommodate the lattice mismatch. Being nearly lattice-matched and moreover highly resistant to lattice and bond distortions thanks to a largely covalent bonding, cubic Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te sets a benchmark to test the sensitivity of lattice vibrations to minimum lattice and bond distortions induced by alloying. We explore experimentally the two transverse optical regimes of cubic Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te (x<0.7), with ab initio calculations in support: (1) the phonon-polariton regime at quasi-infinite (photon-like) wavelengths averaging over the alloy disorder, probed by forward-Raman scattering, and (2) the purely-mechanical phonon regime down to minimum wavelengths nearly matching the lattice constant zooming into the alloy disorder, achieved by inelastic neutron scattering. The short Zn-Te bond is found to exhibit a compact optical duo distinguishing homo from hetero local bond environments (1 bond: 2 mode), as explained by the percolation model. This deviates from the historical Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te MREI (modified-random-element-isodisplacement) scheme inspired from classical Raman and infrared studies, in which the optical modes are blind to the bond environment (1-bond: 1-mode). So, the test is positive, reinforcing the status of the percolation scheme as a robust decryptor of (even small) lattice distortions induced by alloying through the lattice dynamics.

</details>


### [174] [Néel vector switching by magnetization-dependent layer-resolved spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40215)
*Sheng Pan,Weiwei Wang,Jie-Xiang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究了反铁磁拓扑绝缘体MnBi2Te4的层分辨自旋轨道矩，发现其自旋霍尔电导由与磁化无关的自旋霍尔项和两个随磁序演化的自旋反常霍尔项组成，并利用这些项实现了有限层薄膜中奈尔矢量的无场可逆翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用层分辨自旋轨道矩通过局域自旋-电荷转换来操控反铁磁序，解决反铁磁材料电流驱动翻转困难、需要外磁场辅助的问题。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，分析反铁磁拓扑绝缘体MnBi2Te4的层分辨自旋霍尔电导，将其分解为与磁化无关的自旋霍尔项和两个对奈尔矢量偶对称、随局域磁序演化的自旋反常霍尔项，并考虑局域反演不对称导致的表面层与内层差异，进而模拟有限七倍层（septuple-layer）薄膜中奈尔矢量的翻转动力学。

Result: 层分辨自旋霍尔电导包含磁化无关自旋霍尔项与两个自旋反常霍尔项；局域反演不对称区分了表面层和内层。该响应可实现有限七倍层薄膜中奈尔矢量的无场可逆翻转。磁化无关自旋霍尔项单独即可反转磁序，而自旋反常霍尔项提供磁化依赖的反馈，重新分配层分辨力矩并重塑翻转轨迹。

Conclusion: 层分辨自旋轨道矩为反铁磁序的操控提供了新途径，通过层和界面工程可定制电流驱动的翻转，实现反铁磁拓扑绝缘体中的无场可逆磁序开关。

Abstract: Layer-resolved spin-orbit torques offer a route to controlling antiferromagnetic order through local spin-charge conversion. First-principles calculations show that the layer-resolved spin Hall conductivity of the antiferromagnetic topological insulator $\mathrm{Mn{Bi}_{2}{Te}_{4}}$ consists of a magnetization-independent spin Hall term plus two spin anomalous Hall terms that are even in the Néel vector yet evolve with the local magnetic order, while local inversion asymmetry differentiates surface and inner layers. This response enables reversible, field-free switching of the Néel vector in finite-septuple-layer films. Although the magnetization-independent spin Hall term alone can reverse the order, the spin anomalous Hall terms provide magnetization-dependent feedback that redistributes the layer-resolved torques and reshapes the switching trajectory, offering a route to tailoring current-driven switching through layer and interface engineering.

</details>


### [175] [Large Magnetocaloric Effect and Adiabatic Temperature Change in the Tellurate: Gd2TeO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40296)
*Abhijit Nayak,Gurunathan Angusamy,Kusampal Yadav,S. K. Panda,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Raivo Stern,Tanmoy Chakrabarty,Krishnamurthy Jyothinagaram*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了多晶 Gd₂TeO₆ 的结构、磁性、热力学和磁热性质，发现其无常规长程磁有序、具有弱反铁磁相互作用和接近理论极限的磁熵变，是一种有前景的低温磁制冷候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索尚未被研究的稀土碲酸盐 Gd₂TeO₆ 作为低温磁制冷材料的潜力，填补该体系在低温磁热应用方面的空白。

Method: 采用粉末 X 射线衍射确认晶体结构；通过磁化率、磁化强度测量表征磁性；利用热容测量结合 Debye-Einstein 分析分离晶格与磁贡献；计算磁熵变（-ΔS_M）与绝热温变；并用 GGA+U 第一性原理计算研究竞争反铁磁态。

Result: Gd₂TeO₆ 为正交 P2₁2₁2₁ 相，含两个晶体学不等价的七配位 Gd 位点；2 K 以上无常规长程磁序，约 4 K 以下出现低场磁不可逆；Weiss 温度 -1.25 K 表明弱反铁磁相互作用；7 T 时磁化达理论饱和值的约 97% 且几乎无磁滞。2.5 K 时，0–2、0–5、0–7 T 的 -ΔS_M 分别为 21.40、48.51、54.81 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹，体熵变达 400.50 mJ cm⁻³ K⁻¹；14 T 下 -ΔS_M = 63.51 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹，接近理论极限 64.25 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹；0–7 T 的绝热温变约 22.7 K。

Conclusion: Gd₂TeO₆ 兼具弱反铁磁相互作用、可忽略的磁滞以及接近理论极限的巨大磁熵变，是极具潜力的低温磁制冷候选材料。

Abstract: We investigate the structural, magnetic, thermodynamic, and magnetocaloric properties of polycrystalline Gd2TeO6, a rare-earth tellurate unexplored for cryogenic magnetic refrigeration. Powder X-ray diffraction confirms single-phase orthorhombic symmetry (P212121), featuring two crystallographically distinct seven-coordinate Gd sites. Magnetization measurements reveal no conventional long-range magnetic ordering down to 2 K, although low-field magnetic irreversibility emerges below approximately 4 K. Curie-Weiss analysis yields a Weiss temperature of -1.25 K, indicating weak predominantly antiferromagnetic interactions. The magnetization reaches approximately 97% of the theoretical saturation moment at 7 T with negligible hysteresis. Heat-capacity measurements reveal no sharp zero-field anomaly above 2 K, while a broad field-induced maximum indicates redistribution of low-energy magnetic entropy. Debye-Einstein analysis separates the lattice and magnetic contributions. At 2.5 K, the maximum magnetic entropy changes (-DeltaSM) reach 21.40, 48.51, and 54.81 J kg-1 K-1 for field changes of 0-2, 0-5, and 0-7 T, respectively. The corresponding volumetric entropy change reaches 400.50 mJ cm-3 K-1. Measurements up to 14 T yield (-DeltaSM) = 63.51 J kg-1 K-1, approaching the theoretical limit of 64.25 J kg-1 K-1. The estimated adiabatic temperature change is approximately 22.7 K for 0-7 T. First-principles calculations with GGA+U approximation reveal competing antiferromagnetic states, highlighting Gd2TeO6 as a promising candidate for cryogenic magnetocaloric applications.

</details>


### [176] [Soliton-Lattice Origin of Incommensurability in High-Pressure Antimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40399)
*M. Smirman,K. Sun,H. Sheng,I. I. Mazin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文揭示了高压锑主客体相 Sb-II 的非公度性微观起源：它是一个孤子晶格，局域的客体-主体错配角饰在一个公度的 4/3 参考结构上，解释了主客体体系的非公度性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主客体晶体中非公度性的微观起源一直未被解决。研究旨在阐明 Sb-II 这种高压锑主客体相为何呈现非公度结构，其背后的微观机制是什么。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算揭示出具有波纹状且带迟滞的错配（registry）势能面，据此建立 Frenkel-Kontorova 模型，推导出形式为 (4n+1)/(3n+1) 的孤子晶格近似结构；再利用机器学习势计算这些近似结构的能量，确认孤子间距存在能量最优值，其局域错配核心亦由第一性原理弛豫验证。在有限温度下，热展宽的孤子晶格表现为沿客体链的原子密度波，与实验衍射一致。

Result: 计算表明 Sb-II 是以公度 4/3 结构为参考的孤子晶格，负载体-主体错配局域化；第一性原理揭示出波纹状且带迟滞的错配势能面；Frenkel-Kontorova 模型给出 (4n+1)/(3n+1) 形式的孤子晶格近似结构；机器学习势计算显示孤子间距具有能量优选值。有限温度下热展宽的孤子晶格表现为原子密度波，与实验衍射观测吻合。

Conclusion: 该孤子错配图像为 Sb-II 中主客体非公度性提供了微观机制，说明非公度结构来源于在公度参考结构上装饰的局域客体-主体错配（孤子）。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of incommensurability in host-guest crystals remains unresolved. Here we show that the high-pressure antimony host-guest phase Sb-II is a soliton lattice, where localized guest-host misregistries decorate a
  commensurate 4/3 reference structure. First-principles calculations reveal a corrugated and hysteretic registry landscape, motivating a Frenkel-Kontorova description that accounts for soliton-lattice approximants of the form
  $(4n+1)/(3n+1)$. Machine-learning-potential calculations of these approximants show an energetically preferred soliton spacing, and their localized misregistry cores are also verified by first-principles relaxations. At finite
  temperature, the thermally broadened soliton lattice appears as an atomic density wave along the guest chains, consistent with experimental diffraction. This soliton-registry picture provides a microscopic mechanism for host-guest
  incommensurability in Sb-II.

</details>


### [177] [Light-induced chiral altermagnets from conventional antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40410)
*Jorge Cardenas-Gamboa,Devika Jayadeep,Aritz Leonardo,Gregory A. Fiete,Jeroen van den Brink,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过相干晶格控制（非线性声子学）光诱导交变磁性（altermagnetism）的路径，并以 Fe₂TeO₆ 为原型演示了中红外脉冲驱动手性畸变、产生非相对论自旋劈裂的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体兼具补偿型共线磁序与动量依赖的非相对论自旋劈裂，在自旋电子学中极具潜力，但目前缺乏可控、可切换的制备与调控手段。作者希望利用光与晶格的相干耦合，在常规反铁磁体中动态、超快地诱导出交变磁性，实现按需的光学控制。

Method: 首先通过对称性分析，找出能破除自旋简并、同时保持连接相反自旋亚晶格的旋转对称性的声子畸变模式；其中几何手性声子将结构手性与交变磁性联系起来。随后以 Fe₂TeO₆ 为原型，利用红外活性声子与光学沉默的破对称模式之间的负双二次耦合激发结构失稳；采用基于第一性原理的晶格动力学计算瞬态手性畸变，并用电子结构计算验证光致结构的非相对论自旋劈裂。

Result: 对称性分析确定了几何手性声子可作为诱导交变磁性的有效途径；线性偏振中红外脉冲能通过负双二次耦合驱动结构失稳；第一性原理晶格动力学显示出现瞬态手性畸变；电子结构计算证实该光致结构具有交变磁性特征的非相对论自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条受对称性引导的、通过非线性声子学实现交变磁态超快光学控制的路径，为交变磁体的按需调控与自旋电子学应用提供了新方案。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated collinear magnetic order with momentum-dependent nonrelativistic spin splitting, offering new opportunities for spintronic applications. Here, we propose a route to optically induce altermagnetism through coherent lattice control. Using symmetry analysis, we identify phonon distortions that lift the spin degeneracy of conventional antiferromagnets while preserving the rotational symmetries connecting opposite-spin sublattices. Among these, geometric chiral phonons provide a particularly appealing pathway, linking the emergence of altermagnetism to structural chirality. Taking Fe$_2$TeO$_6$ as a prototype, we demonstrate that a linearly polarized mid-infrared pulse can drive a structural instability through negative biquadratic coupling between an infrared-active phonon and an optically silent, symmetry-breaking mode. First-principles-based lattice dynamics reveal the emergence of a transient chiral distortion, while electronic-structure calculations establish that the light-induced structure exhibits nonrelativistic spin splitting characteristic of altermagnetism. Our results establish a symmetry-guided pathway toward the ultrafast optical control of altermagnetic states through nonlinear phononics.

</details>
